   342	// ── Active hours (content-creation window) ───────────────────────────────────
   343	
   344	export interface ActiveHours {
   345	    /** "HH:MM", server-local. */
   346	    start: string;
   347	    end: string;
   348	}
   349	
   350	/** Overnight by default (Todd 2026-07-27): content creation runs 8pm–8am. */
   351	export const DEFAULT_ACTIVE_HOURS: ActiveHours = { start: '20:00', end: '08:00' };
   352	
   353	function parseHM(s: unknown): number | null {
   354	    if (typeof s !== 'string') return null;
   355	    const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(s.trim());
   356	    if (!m) return null;
   357	    const h = Number(m[1]);
   358	    const min = Number(m[2]);
   359	    if (h < 0 || h > 23 || min < 0 || min > 59) return null;
   360	    return h * 60 + min;
   361	}
   362	
   363	/** Strict shape check: {start, end} as valid "HH:MM" with start ≠ end
   364	 *  (use `null` for 24/7, not a zero-length window). Returns null on anything else. */
   365	export function normalizeActiveHours(v: unknown): ActiveHours | null {
   366	    if (!v || typeof v !== 'object') return null;
   367	    const o = v as Record<string, unknown>;
   368	    const start = parseHM(o.start);
   369	    const end = parseHM(o.end);
   370	    if (start === null || end === null || start === end) return null;
   371	    return { start: String(o.start).trim(), end: String(o.end).trim() };
   372	}
   373	
   374	/** Config/event value → effective window. Absent or malformed → the overnight
   375	 *  default (ships active, per Todd); explicit null → 24/7 (no window). */
   376	export function resolveActiveHours(v: unknown): ActiveHours | null {
   377	    if (v === null) return null;
   378	    return normalizeActiveHours(v) ?? DEFAULT_ACTIVE_HOURS;
   379	}
   380	
   381	export function isWithinActiveHours(date: Date, hours: ActiveHours | null): boolean {
   382	    if (!hours) return true;
   383	    const start = parseHM(hours.start);
   384	    const end = parseHM(hours.end);
   385	    if (start === null || end === null || start === end) return true;
   386	    const m = date.getHours() * 60 + date.getMinutes();
   387	    // start < end: same-day window. start > end: wraps midnight (overnight).
   388	    return start < end ? m >= start && m < end : m >= start || m < end;
   389	}
   390	
   391	/** Milliseconds until the window closes: null = no window (24/7), 0 = already
   392	 *  outside it. Used to clamp the unlimited chain so 8am actually ends it. */
   393	export function msUntilWindowClose(date: Date, hours: ActiveHours | null): number | null {
   394	    if (!hours) return null;
   395	    if (!isWithinActiveHours(date, hours)) return 0;
   396	    const end = parseHM(hours.end);
   397	    if (end === null) return null;
   398	    const nowM = date.getHours() * 60 + date.getMinutes();
   399	    const deltaMin = end > nowM ? end - nowM : 24 * 60 - nowM + end;
   400	    return deltaMin * 60_000 - (date.getSeconds() * 1_000 + date.getMilliseconds());
   401	}
   402	
   403	export interface ContentSchedulerConfig {
   404	    enabled?: boolean;
   405	    intervalMinutes?: number;      // default 30
   406	    imageCount?: number;           // images per cycle, default 2
   407	    videoCount?: number;           // videos per cycle, default 1
   408	    /** i2v (base→animate), t2v (text→scene), or mixed (both, per video). Default 'mixed'. */
   409	    videoMode?: 'i2v' | 't2v' | 'mixed';
   410	    /**
   411	     * Unlimited-within-cycle images (Todd 2026-07-20): instead of a fixed
   412	     * imageCount, keep generating for the whole cycle window — each render
   413	     * chains the next one only while the window is open, so throughput
   414	     * adapts to actual render times and the single-flight GPU queue never
   415	     * backs up into the next cycle (no logjams). imageCount is ignored
   416	     * while this is true.
   417	     */
   418	    unlimitedImages?: boolean;
   419	    /**
   420	     * LEGACY single-window (v1) — used only when `tiers` is absent, feeding
   421	     * the behavior-preserving migration in resolveTierMap. Absent →
   422	     * DEFAULT_ACTIVE_HOURS (overnight); explicit null → 24/7.
   423	     */
   424	    activeHours?: ActiveHours | null;
   425	    /**
   426	     * v2 canonical shape: per-tier schedule + pacing. Present ⇒ the flat
   427	     * pacing fields above are ignored (the API persists tiers-only).
   428	     */
   429	    tiers?: TierMap;
   430	    /**
   431	     * Round-robin the schedule-driven pick across OPEN tiers (mild→explicit)
   432	     * instead of most-explicit-wins, so overlapping/24-7 tiers all render
   433	     * across the day (Todd 2026-07-28 "all modes all day"). Default false.
   434	     */
   435	    tierRotation?: boolean;
   436	    /**
   437	     * Arc lifecycle durations (Inner Loop F5): arcs age fresh → evolving →
   438	     * finale → retired from createdAt. Hand-edited config — NOT on the policy
   439	     * PUT surface (the canonical rewrite preserves it); absent → PRD defaults
   440	     * {freshDays: 3, evolveDays: 4}.
   441	     */
   442	    arcLifecycle?: ArcLifecycleConfig;
   443	}
   444	
   445	interface ArcState {
   446	    streakDays?: number;
   447	    mood?: string;
   448	    positiveRate?: number;
   449	    /** Set by the (backlog #1270) film-aesthetic path; 'film' forces 1:1 square,
   450	     *  normal-FOV framing (Minolta Autocord). Absent today → digital alternation. */
   451	    cameraAesthetic?: string;
   452	}
   453	
   454	export class KiraContentScheduler implements EventSource {
   455	    readonly name = 'kira_content_scheduler';
   456	    private config: ContentSchedulerConfig;
   457	    private intervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
   458	    private lastGeneratedAt = 0;
   459	    /** Last schedule-driven tier — rotation cursor, persisted in BREE_HOME so
   460	     *  deploy restarts CONTINUE the walk (2026-07-28 follow-up: in-memory-only
   461	     *  reset the walk to neutral on every restart). Overrides don't advance it. */
   462	    private lastScheduleMode: SchedulerContentMode | null = null;
   463	
   464	    constructor(private pushEvent: (event: DaemonEvent) => void) {
   465	        this.config = this.loadConfig();
   466	        this.lastScheduleMode = loadRotationCursor();
   467	    }
   468	
   469	
   470	
   471	    private loadConfig(): ContentSchedulerConfig {
   472	        try {
   473	            const raw = JSON.parse(
   474	                fs.readFileSync(
   475	                    path.join(breeHome(), 'config.json'),
   476	                    'utf-8',
   477	                ),
   478	            );
   479	            return raw?.contentScheduler ?? { enabled: true, intervalMinutes: 30, imageCount: 2, videoCount: 1 };
   480	        } catch {
   481	            return { enabled: true, intervalMinutes: 30, imageCount: 2, videoCount: 1 };
   482	        }
   483	    }
   484	
   485	    private loadArcState(): ArcState {
   486	        try {
   487	            const arc = JSON.parse(
   488	                fs.readFileSync(
   489	                    path.join(breeHome(), 'inner-life', 'arc.json'),
   490	                    'utf-8',
   491	                ),
   492	            );
   493	            return {
   494	                streakDays: Number(arc?.streakDays) || 0,
   495	                mood: arc?.currentMood || 'neutral',
   496	                positiveRate: arc?.positiveRate || 0.5,
   497	            };
   498	        } catch {
   499	            return { streakDays: 0, mood: 'neutral', positiveRate: 0.5 };
   500	        }
   501	    }
   502	
   503	    async start(): Promise<void> {
   504	        if (!this.config.enabled) return;
   505	
   506	        // Idempotency guard (#kira-scheduler-double-start): start() is invoked
   507	        // twice in production — once by initKiraContentScheduler() and again by
   508	        // the daemon's module-source lifecycle loop. Without this early return
   509	        // the second call scheduled a SECOND setInterval and orphaned the first
   510	        // (overwriting this.intervalId), so tick() fired twice per cycle. If a
   511	        // timer is already live, do nothing. stop() nulls intervalId, so a
   512	        // genuine restart after stop() is still allowed.
   513	        if (this.intervalId !== null) return;
   514	
   515	        const intervalMs = (this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30) * 60_000;
   516	
   517	        // Run initial check after short delay
   518	        setTimeout(() => this.tick(), 5000);
   519	
   520	        // Then run on the fixed interval
   521	        this.intervalId = setInterval(() => this.tick(), intervalMs);
   522	    }
   523	
   524	    stop(): void {
   525	        if (this.intervalId !== null) {
   526	            clearInterval(this.intervalId);
   527	            this.intervalId = null;
   528	        }
   529	    }
   530	
   531	    /**
   532	     * Live pause lever: a `scheduler-paused` file in breeHome() halts the 24/7
   533	     * content cycle without a daemon restart (tick never re-reads config). Any
   534	     * error (fs unavailable, etc.) is swallowed → not paused, never throws.
   535	     */
   536	    private isPaused(): boolean {
   537	        try {
   538	            return fs.existsSync(path.join(breeHome(), 'scheduler-paused'));
   539	        } catch {
   540	            return false;
   541	        }
   542	    }
   543	
   544	    /**
   545	     * Reconcile the tick timer with the (re-read) policy. Called by the
   546	     * policy PUT route (via notifyContentSchedulerPolicyChanged) so an
   547	     * enabled flip takes effect IMMEDIATELY — previously the timer only ever
   548	     * started at construction, so enabling a daemon that booted with
   549	     * enabled=false did nothing until a restart (timer-start bug, 2026-07-25).
   550	     * Idempotent: reload config, then start/stop only on an actual mismatch.
   551	     */
   552	    applyPolicyChange(): void {
   553	        const previousInterval = this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30;
   554	        this.config = { ...this.config, ...this.loadConfig() };
   555	        if (this.config.enabled && this.intervalId === null) {
   556	            console.log('[kira-scheduler] policy enabled — starting tick loop');
   557	            void this.start();
   558	        } else if (!this.config.enabled && this.intervalId !== null) {
   559	            console.log('[kira-scheduler] policy disabled — stopping tick loop');
   560	            this.stop();
   561	        } else if (this.intervalId !== null
   562	                   && (this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30) !== previousInterval) {
   563	            // Live cadence change (Codex W7): the timer used to be pinned to
   564	            // its start() interval until a daemon restart.
   565	            const intervalMs = (this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30) * 60_000;
   566	            clearInterval(this.intervalId);
   567	            this.intervalId = setInterval(() => this.tick(), intervalMs);
   568	            console.log(`[kira-scheduler] tick interval rescheduled to ${this.config.intervalMinutes}m`);
   569	        }
   570	    }
   571	
   572	    /** The live config (status surface). */
   573	    currentConfig(): ContentSchedulerConfig {
   574	        return this.config;
   575	    }
   576	
   577	    /** Rotation cursor for status preview (buildStreamStatus). */
   578	    lastScheduledMode(): SchedulerContentMode | null {
   579	        return this.lastScheduleMode;
   580	    }
   581	
   582	    /**
   583	     * Stream-mode slot changed (tool / /stream / API): reset the double-fire
   584	     * gate and tick NOW so overrides take effect within seconds, not at the
   585	     * next interval (Codex W6). An already-running unlimited chain is not
   586	     * pre-empted — it finishes its cycle at the stamped tier.
   587	     */
   588	    pokeStreamModeChanged(): void {
   589	        this.lastGeneratedAt = 0;
   590	        this.tick();
   591	    }
   592	
   593	    private tick(): void {
   594	        // Checked BEFORE the lastGeneratedAt gate so a paused cycle is skipped
   595	        // entirely and does not advance lastGeneratedAt.
   596	        if (this.isPaused()) {
   597	            console.log('[kira-scheduler] paused (scheduler-paused file present) — skipping cycle');
   598	            return;
   599	        }
   600	
   601	        // Live policy: re-read config every cycle so PUT
   602	        // /v1/kira/content-scheduler/policy edits (Kira tab) take effect on
   603	        // the next tick without a daemon restart. The interval TIMER stays as
   604	        // started; intervalMinutes changes apply to window math immediately
   605	        // and to the timer on the next daemon restart.
   606	        this.config = { ...this.config, ...this.loadConfig() };
   607	
   608	        // Honor a live disable (hand-edited config or a PUT the notifier
   609	        // missed): a disabled scheduler must not keep generating. stop() nulls
   610	        // intervalId, so a later enabled=true policy update can restart it.
   611	        if (this.config.enabled === false) {
   612	            console.log('[kira-scheduler] disabled by policy — stopping tick loop');
   613	            this.stop();
   614	            return;
   615	        }
   616	
   617	        // Tiered resolution (v2, Todd 2026-07-27): precedence todd override >
   618	        // Kira's choice > schedule (per-tier windows, most explicit wins —
   619	        // or the rotation walk when tierRotation is on). Checked before the
   620	        // lastGeneratedAt gate so a skipped slot never counts as "generated".
   621	        const tiers = resolveTierMap(this.config);
   622	        const cycle = resolveCycle(new Date(), tiers, {
   623	            enabled: this.config.tierRotation === true,
   624	            prevMode: this.lastScheduleMode,
   625	        });
   626	        if (cycle === null) {
   627	            console.log('[kira-scheduler] no tier window open (and no override) — skipping cycle');
   628	            return;
   629	        }
   630	        const now = Date.now();
   631	        const intervalMs = (this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30) * 60_000;
   632	
   633	        // Skip if already generated recently (prevents double-fire)
   634	        if (now - this.lastGeneratedAt < intervalMs * 0.9) return;
   635	
   636	        this.lastGeneratedAt = now;
   637	
   638	        // Advance + persist the rotation cursor only for a cycle that actually
   639	        // FIRES — a double-fire-gated tick must not step the walk (that would
   640	        // skip tiers), and the persisted cursor lets a deploy restart continue
   641	        // the walk instead of resetting to neutral (2026-07-28 follow-up).
   642	        if (cycle.modeSource === 'schedule') {
   643	            this.lastScheduleMode = cycle.mode;
   644	            saveRotationCursor(cycle.mode);
   645	        }
   646	
   647	        const { imageCount, unlimitedImages, videoCount } = cycle.pacing;
   648	        const imageLabel = unlimitedImages ? 'unlimited (fit to cycle)' : String(imageCount);
   649	
   650	        // Emit generation event. `modeSource` — NOT `source` — the top-level
   651	        // DaemonEvent.source stays the handler's discriminator (Codex B4).
   652	        this.pushEvent({
   653	            id: generateId(),
   654	            source: 'kira_content_scheduler',
   655	            summary: `Generate ${cycle.mode} content (${cycle.modeSource}): ${imageLabel} images + ${videoCount} videos`,
   656	            details: JSON.stringify({
   657	                imageCount,
   658	                unlimitedImages,
   659	                intervalMinutes: this.config.intervalMinutes ?? 30,
   660	                videoCount,
   661	                // Default 'i2v', NOT 'mixed': un-pinning t2v (2026-07-28) must
   662	                // not flip behavior for configs that never set videoMode.
   663	                videoMode: this.config.videoMode ?? 'i2v',
   664	                clipSeconds: (this.config as { clipSeconds?: number }).clipSeconds,
   665	                contentMode: cycle.mode,
   666	                modeSource: cycle.modeSource,
   667	                activeHours: cycle.window,
   668	                promptMethod: 'jit-local-model',
   669	                videoMethod: this.config.videoMode ?? 'i2v',
   670	                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   671	            }),
   672	            priority: 'low' as EventPriority,
   673	            createdAt: Date.now(),
   674	        });
   675	    }
   676	}
   677	
   678	// ── Live policy notifier ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   679	// The policy PUT route (kira-api.ts) persists config.json but has no handle on
   680	// the scheduler instance the daemon lifecycle owns. This tiny registry gives it
   681	// one: init registers the live instance; the route calls the notifier after a
   682	// successful write so an enabled false→true flip starts the tick loop NOW (and
   683	// true→false stops it) instead of waiting for a daemon restart.
   684	
   685	let activeScheduler: KiraContentScheduler | null = null;
   686	
   687	/** Register (or clear, with null) the live scheduler instance. */
   688	export function setActiveContentScheduler(scheduler: KiraContentScheduler | null): void {
   689	    activeScheduler = scheduler;
   690	}
   691	
   692	/** Tell the live scheduler its policy changed (no-op when none registered). */
   693	export function notifyContentSchedulerPolicyChanged(): void {
   694	    activeScheduler?.applyPolicyChange();
   695	}
   696	
   697	/**
   698	 * Event handler: kira_generate_content
   699	 * Generates content at the event's fruit mode (falling back to the live store
   700	 * default) with local model prompts and i2v videos. Neutral mode is the
   701	 * Autocord art-photography stream: film still lifes, no character injection,
   702	 * images only.
   703	 */
   704	export async function handleKiraContentGenerationEvent(
   705	    event: DaemonEvent,
   706	    ctx: PersonaTurnContext,
   707	): Promise<void> {
   708	    if (event.source !== 'kira_content_scheduler') return;
   709	
   710	    let data: any = {};
   711	    try {
   712	        data = JSON.parse(event.details ?? '{}');
   713	    } catch {
   714	        // fall through to defaults
   715	    }
   716	    const unlimitedImages = data.unlimitedImages === true;
   717	    const imageCount = data.imageCount ?? 2;
   718	    const videoCount = data.videoCount ?? 1;
   719	    const intervalMinutes = Number.isFinite(Number(data.intervalMinutes))
   720	        ? Number(data.intervalMinutes)
   721	        : 30;
   722	    // Absent → 'i2v' (backward-compatible default for older events/tests). The
   723	    // live scheduler emits 'mixed' by config default, so production gets both.
   724	    const videoMode: string = data.videoMode ?? 'i2v';
   725	    const clipSeconds = resolveClipSeconds((data as { clipSeconds?: number }).clipSeconds);
   726	    // Fruit mode: the event's stamp wins (what the cycle announced); an event
   727	    // without one (older queue entries, tests) resolves the live store default.
   728	    const contentMode: SchedulerContentMode =
   729	        normalizeSchedulerContentMode(data.contentMode) ?? resolveScheduledContentMode();
   730	    // Neutral is Kira's ART stream, not SFW-Kira: Autocord film still lifes,
   731	    // 6×6 square, her description NEVER enters the prompt, and no videos (a
   732	    // TLR shoots stills).
   733	    const filmStream = contentMode === 'neutral';
   734	    // Window clamp for the unlimited chain. The tick gate is the real window
   735	    // enforcement (it stamps activeHours on every event it emits); an event
   736	    // WITHOUT the field (pre-deploy queue entries, tests) clamps nothing.
   737	    const activeHours = 'activeHours' in data ? resolveActiveHours(data.activeHours) : null;
   738	
   739	    console.log(
   740	        `[kira-content-scheduler] Enqueuing ${unlimitedImages ? 'unlimited (fit to cycle)' : imageCount} images + ${filmStream ? 0 : videoCount} videos (${videoMode}) — ${contentMode} — off-turn`,
   741	    );
   742	
   743	    // Load arc state to inform prompt generation
   744	    const arcState = loadArcState();
   745	
   746	    // Arc lifecycle (F5): read live once per cycle — same tolerant config
   747	    // read the status snapshot uses — so a duration edit applies next cycle
   748	    // without a daemon restart. The SAME config must govern EVERY
   749	    // activeArcTexts evaluation this cycle (muse tick included): that helper
   750	    // write-through-retires arcs under whatever config it is handed, so a
   751	    // default-config call ahead of planCycleSuggestions would permanently
   752	    // retire arcs the configured longer lifecycle still considers live.
   753	    const arcLifecycle = loadSchedulerConfigForStatus().arcLifecycle;
   754	
   755	    // F2 muse tick (Inner Loop, PRD §3 F2): her solitude generates ideas — a
   756	    // thin box (< 3 pending usable by this tier) plus a stale tick (> 6h,
   757	    // persisted) triggers ONE sealed local-LLM call that files 1-3
   758	    // kira-authored ideas BEFORE the cycle plans its seeds, so a fresh idea
   759	    // can seed THIS cycle. Fire-and-await under try/catch (review checklist
   760	    // 3 — a muse failure must never cost a render cycle); the time budget is
   761	    // the optimizer client's own timeoutMs, no second timer.
   762	    try {
   763	        const muse = await runScheduledMuseTick(contentMode, { arcLifecycle });
   764	        if (muse.ran) {
   765	            console.log(`[kira-content-scheduler] Muse tick: ${muse.added} idea(s) filed${muse.reason ? ` (${muse.reason})` : ''}`);
   766	        }
   767	    } catch (err) {
   768	        console.warn(`[kira-content-scheduler] Muse tick failed (${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}) — proceeding`);
   769	    }
   770	
   771	    // Canonical model sketches (Todd 2026-07-29): the Characters tab (engine
   772	    // /v1/characters) owns Divine/Rhea/Bea's descriptions — pull them once per
   773	    // erotic-film-eligible cycle. FIRE-AND-FORGET, deliberately not awaited:

   775	    // very first), and the cycle never spends its window on network — the
   776	    // gate's unlimited-chain timing test caught an awaited version stalling
   777	    // cycle start behind a real fetch.
   778	    if (contentMode === 'banana' || contentMode === 'avocado') {
   779	        void refreshEroticFilmModels();
   780	    }
   781	
   782	    // Suggestion box (Todd 2026-07-17): pending image/video ideas replace the
   783	    // random seeds FIFO (consumed); a session idea + active arcs theme every
   784	    // seed this cycle. See src/kira/suggestions.ts for the semantics.
   785	    // Unlimited mode consumes up to 6 image ideas; the chain falls back to
   786	    // generated intents past the suggestion supply.
   787	    const plan = planCycleSuggestions(unlimitedImages ? 6 : imageCount, videoCount,
   788	                                      undefined, undefined, {
   789	                                          tier: contentMode,
   790	                                          // Arc lifecycle (F5): the cycle's single
   791	                                          // live read, shared with the muse tick
   792	                                          // above (see that comment).
   793	                                          arcLifecycle,
   794	                                      });
   795	    // Inspiration well (Todd 2026-07-29 "the box should always serve as
   796	    // inspiration for random creativity"): a cycle with no explicit theme
   797	    // (no session idea, no active arc for this tier) draws ONE entry from the
   798	    // whole box — pending or already-consumed, weighted toward the least-used
   799	    // — so past ideas keep sparking new cycles instead of dying at consumption.
   800	    if (!plan.theme) {
   801	        const insp = drawInspiration(undefined, { tier: contentMode });
   802	        if (insp) {
   803	            plan.theme = insp.text;
   804	            console.log(`[kira-content-scheduler] Inspiration well: themed cycle from "${insp.text.slice(0, 60)}" (${insp.id})`);
   805	        }
   806	    }
   807	    if (plan.imageSeeds.length > 0 || plan.videoSeeds.length > 0 || plan.theme) {
   808	        console.log(
   809	            `[kira-content-scheduler] Suggestion box: ${plan.imageSeeds.length} image, `
   810	            + `${plan.videoSeeds.length} video idea(s), theme=${plan.theme ?? 'none'}`,

codex
The first mismatch is already visible: Kira’s “queue” is not the engine FIFO today. The daemon queues composite callbacks locally at low priority; an i2v callback can contain seed generation, up to three VLM checks/retries, a memory gate, and then video generation. I’m tracing that boundary carefully because front-of-engine reorder cannot preempt work that has not yet been submitted to the engine.

     326 /Users/toddwalderman/Projects/coffeeshop-server/src/kira/media-pool/render-queue.ts
     1	/**
     2	 * render-queue.ts — in-process single-flight GPU render queue (#1158 FDD G2).
     3	 *
     4	 * dans-pe MLX and ComfyBox share the ONE Mac GPU, so heavy media producers
     5	 * (image/video renders, up to the 600s tool timeout) are serialized here at
     6	 * concurrency=1 instead of racing each other. This is the substrate the
     7	 * AsyncEnvelope (src/kira/async-envelope.ts) rides on; nothing on the
     8	 * ThreadMailbox turn path ever awaits a job — enqueueRender returns
     9	 * immediately and the drain loop delivers completions off-turn.
    10	 *
    11	 * v1 is deliberately in-memory (FDD N2): a daemon restart strands in-flight
    12	 * jobs, and the existing gallery orphan-scan (turn-hooks) adopts finished
    13	 * renders on a later turn. Persisting the queue is a v2 option.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	import { writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync, unlinkSync } from 'fs';
    17	import { tmpdir } from 'os';
    18	import { join } from 'path';
    19	
    20	export type RenderKind = 'image' | 'video' | 'voice';
    21	
    22	/** Cross-daemon snapshot file for the /que command. Only the ACTIVE renderer
    23	 *  writes it (Bree's queue stays empty → never writes → no clobber); any bot
    24	 *  reads it via renderQueueView (platform, no kira import). */
    25	export const RENDER_QUEUE_STATE_FILE = join(tmpdir(), 'coffeeshop-render-queue.json');
    26	
    27	/** One deferred render. Receives the job's AbortSignal (aborted on queue
    28	 *  shutdown); MUST do its own delivery — the queue only serializes. */
    29	export type RenderTask = (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<void>;
    30	
    31	/**
    32	 * Three-tier floor (Todd: "24/7 content is ALWAYS lowest priority; any work /
    33	 * special request / Bree request / studio request is high").
    34	 * - 'high'   = user-initiated (gallery Motion/Repair/HQ, Bree/studio requests) —
    35	 *              jumps ahead of everything below.
    36	 * - 'normal' = default work.
    37	 * - 'low'    = autonomous 24/7 content — always sits at the back, behind any
    38	 *              queued 'normal'/'high'.
    39	 * Ordering is strictly high > normal > low, FIFO within a tier.
    40	 */
    41	export type RenderPriority = 'high' | 'normal' | 'low';
    42	
    43	/** Higher number = served first. Used to place a new job ahead of all
    44	 *  strictly-lower tiers while staying behind equal-or-higher (stable FIFO). */
    45	const PRIORITY_RANK: Record<RenderPriority, number> = { high: 3, normal: 2, low: 1 };
    46	
    47	export interface EnqueueOptions {
    48	    label?: string;
    49	    priority?: RenderPriority;
    50	    onCancel?: () => void;
    51	    /** Source image path (gallery actions) — lets /que + the gallery card show what’s pending on which image. */
    52	    sourceImage?: string;
    53	}
    54	
    55	interface RenderJob {
    56	    id: number;
    57	    kind: RenderKind;
    58	    label: string;
    59	    priority: RenderPriority;
    60	    sourceImage?: string;
    61	    task: RenderTask;
    62	    /** Fired when the job is dropped before it ever starts (queue shutdown/removal) —
    63	     *  the envelope's best-effort cancel-honesty seam (FDD Amendment AC15). */
    64	    onCancel?: () => void;
    65	}
    66	
    67	/** A queue row for the /que tool — id + human label + state + position. */
    68	export interface RenderQueueRow {
    69	    id: number;
    70	    kind: RenderKind;
    71	    label: string;
    72	    priority: RenderPriority;
    73	    sourceImage?: string;
    74	    state: 'running' | 'pending';
    75	    pos: number;
    76	}
    77	
    78	export interface RenderQueueStats {
    79	    /** Jobs waiting behind the running one. */
    80	    queued: number;
    81	    /** A job is currently running. */
    82	    draining: boolean;
    83	    enqueued: number;
    84	    completed: number;
    85	    failed: number;
    86	    cancelled: number;
    87	}
    88	
    89	let queue: RenderJob[] = [];
    90	let draining = false;
    91	let drainScheduled = false;
    92	let currentController: AbortController | null = null;
    93	let shutDown = false;
    94	let stats = { enqueued: 0, completed: 0, failed: 0, cancelled: 0 };
    95	let jobCounter = 0;
    96	let currentJob: RenderJob | null = null;
    97	
    98	/** Write the cross-daemon snapshot for the /que tool (best-effort). */
    99	function writeQueueState(): void {
   100	    try {
   101	        const rows: RenderQueueRow[] = [];
   102	        if (currentJob) rows.push({ id: currentJob.id, kind: currentJob.kind, label: currentJob.label, priority: currentJob.priority, sourceImage: currentJob.sourceImage, state: 'running', pos: 0 });
   103	        queue.forEach((j, i) => rows.push({ id: j.id, kind: j.kind, label: j.label, priority: j.priority, sourceImage: j.sourceImage, state: 'pending', pos: i + 1 }));
   104	        writeFileSync(RENDER_QUEUE_STATE_FILE, JSON.stringify({ ts: Date.now(), rows, ...stats, queued: queue.length, draining }));
   105	    } catch { /* best effort */ }
   106	}
   107	
   108	/**
   109	 * Enqueue a render job. Returns immediately — the caller is OFF the render's
   110	 * critical path (RC-1). Jobs run strictly one at a time in FIFO order; a
   111	 * throwing job is caught and counted, never wedging the queue.
   112	 */
   113	export function enqueueRender(kind: RenderKind, task: RenderTask, opts?: EnqueueOptions | (() => void)): number {
   114	    // Back-compat: a bare function 3rd arg is the legacy onCancel.
   115	    const o: EnqueueOptions = typeof opts === 'function' ? { onCancel: opts } : (opts ?? {});
   116	    const id = ++jobCounter;
   117	    if (shutDown) {
   118	        stats.cancelled++;
   119	        console.warn(`[render-queue] ${kind} job refused — queue is shut down`);
   120	        try { o.onCancel?.(); } catch (err) {
   121	            console.warn('[render-queue] onCancel threw:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
   122	        }
   123	        return id;
   124	    }
   125	    const priority: RenderPriority = o.priority ?? 'normal';
   126	    const job: RenderJob = { id, kind, label: o.label ?? kind, priority, sourceImage: o.sourceImage, task, onCancel: o.onCancel };
   127	    // Insert AHEAD of all jobs with a strictly-lower tier, BEHIND equal-or-higher
   128	    // (stable within a tier = FIFO). So high jumps normal+low, normal jumps low,
   129	    // and low always lands at the back. High-vs-normal behavior is unchanged.
   130	    const rank = PRIORITY_RANK[priority];
   131	    let i = queue.findIndex(j => PRIORITY_RANK[j.priority] < rank);
   132	    if (i < 0) i = queue.length;
   133	    queue.splice(i, 0, job);
   134	    stats.enqueued++;
   135	    console.log(`[render-queue] ${kind} #${id} "${job.label}" queued (${priority}, depth: ${queue.length}${draining ? ', one running' : ''})`);
   136	    writeQueueState();
   137	    // Deferred kick (#1175 review): enqueueRender is an ack-only boundary —
   138	    // an async fn runs synchronously until its first await, so an inline
   139	    // drain() would execute the first job's setup on the ENQUEUER's stack.
   140	    // queueMicrotask keeps the ack synchronous while the job starts strictly
   141	    // after return (same deferral async-envelope uses for its ready path).
   142	    if (!draining && !drainScheduled) {
   143	        drainScheduled = true;
   144	        queueMicrotask(() => {
   145	            drainScheduled = false;
   146	            if (!draining) void drain();
   147	        });
   148	    }
   149	    return id;
   150	}
   151	
   152	/**
   153	 * runThroughRenderQueue — await a render THROUGH the single-flight queue.
   154	 *
   155	 * Unlike enqueueRender (fire-and-forget; the task does its OWN delivery), this
   156	 * resolves with the task's RESULT when the queue runs it, so a producer that
   157	 * NEEDS the render output — the nightly full-night build (P2, FDD §5) — still
   158	 * serializes on the one Mac GPU with live-turn/muse renders instead of
   159	 * colliding at the ToolExecutor level. This is the "share its gate" seam.
   160	 *
   161	 * Rejects if the job is dropped before it ever starts (queue shutdown) or the
   162	 * task throws; a throw is ALSO re-raised into the queue so its failed-counter
   163	 * stays honest and the drain loop moves on cleanly.
   164	 */
   165	export function runThroughRenderQueue<T>(
   166	    kind: RenderKind,
   167	    task: (signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<T>,
   168	): Promise<T> {
   169	    return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
   170	        enqueueRender(
   171	            kind,
   172	            async (signal) => {
   173	                try {
   174	                    resolve(await task(signal));
   175	                } catch (err) {
   176	                    reject(err);
   177	                    throw err;   // keep the queue's failed-counter honest
   178	                }
   179	            },
   180	            () => reject(new Error(`${kind} render cancelled before start (render queue shut down)`)),
   181	        );
   182	    });
   183	}
   184	
   185	async function drain(): Promise<void> {
   186	    draining = true;
   187	    try {
   188	        while (queue.length > 0) {
   189	            const job = queue.shift()!;
   190	            currentJob = job;
   191	            currentController = new AbortController();
   192	            writeQueueState();
   193	            try {
   194	                await job.task(currentController.signal);
   195	                stats.completed++;
   196	            } catch (err) {
   197	                // Per-job containment (FDD §6.3(5)): a failed render never
   198	                // strands the daemon or the jobs behind it.
   199	                stats.failed++;
   200	                console.warn(`[render-queue] ${job.kind} #${job.id} "${job.label}" failed:`, err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
   201	            } finally {
   202	                currentController = null;
   203	                currentJob = null;
   204	                writeQueueState();
   205	            }
   206	        }
   207	    } finally {
   208	        draining = false;
   209	    }
   210	}
   211	
   212	/** Minimal observability for tests and logs (FDD AC13 names `draining`). */
   213	export function getRenderQueueStats(): RenderQueueStats {
   214	    return {
   215	        queued: queue.length,
   216	        draining,
   217	        enqueued: stats.enqueued,
   218	        completed: stats.completed,
   219	        failed: stats.failed,
   220	        cancelled: stats.cancelled,
   221	    };
   222	}
   223	
   224	// ---- Priority-queue tool ops (the /que command) ----
   225	
   226	/** Running + pending jobs (id/label/priority/pos) for the /que display. */
   227	export function listRenderQueue(): RenderQueueRow[] {
   228	    const rows: RenderQueueRow[] = [];
   229	    if (currentJob) rows.push({ id: currentJob.id, kind: currentJob.kind, label: currentJob.label, priority: currentJob.priority, sourceImage: currentJob.sourceImage, state: 'running', pos: 0 });
   230	    queue.forEach((j, i) => rows.push({ id: j.id, kind: j.kind, label: j.label, priority: j.priority, sourceImage: j.sourceImage, state: 'pending', pos: i + 1 }));
   231	    return rows;
   232	}
   233	
   234	/** Move a PENDING job to the front of the pending queue. False if not found (or it's the running job). */
   235	export function bumpRenderJob(id: number): boolean {
   236	    const i = queue.findIndex(j => j.id === id);
   237	    if (i < 0) return false;
   238	    const [job] = queue.splice(i, 1);
   239	    queue.unshift(job);
   240	    writeQueueState();
   241	    return true;
   242	}
   243	
   244	/** Nudge a PENDING job up (negative delta) or down (positive) by `delta` slots. */
   245	export function moveRenderJob(id: number, delta: number): boolean {
   246	    const i = queue.findIndex(j => j.id === id);
   247	    if (i < 0) return false;
   248	    const to = Math.max(0, Math.min(queue.length - 1, i + delta));
   249	    const [job] = queue.splice(i, 1);
   250	    queue.splice(to, 0, job);
   251	    writeQueueState();
   252	    return true;
   253	}
   254	
   255	/** Remove a PENDING job (fires its onCancel). The RUNNING job can't be removed
   256	 *  here (abort it via cancel/shutdown). False if not found. */
   257	export function removeRenderJob(id: number): boolean {
   258	    const i = queue.findIndex(j => j.id === id);
   259	    if (i < 0) return false;
   260	    const [job] = queue.splice(i, 1);
   261	    stats.cancelled++;
   262	    try { job.onCancel?.(); } catch { /* best effort */ }
   263	    writeQueueState();
   264	    return true;
   265	}
   266	
   267	/** Cross-daemon /que management: any bot appends bump/move/remove ops to this
   268	 *  file; the queue-owning daemon applies them on a poll (renderQueueView writes it). */
   269	export const RENDER_QUEUE_CMD_FILE = join(tmpdir(), 'coffeeshop-render-queue-cmd.jsonl');
   270	
   271	/** Apply + clear any pending /que commands. Called on a poll by the owning daemon. */
   272	export function applyQueueCommands(): void {
   273	    if (!existsSync(RENDER_QUEUE_CMD_FILE)) return;
   274	    let content = '';
   275	    try { content = readFileSync(RENDER_QUEUE_CMD_FILE, 'utf8'); unlinkSync(RENDER_QUEUE_CMD_FILE); } catch { return; }
   276	    for (const line of content.split('\n')) {
   277	        if (!line.trim()) continue;
   278	        try {
   279	            const c = JSON.parse(line) as { op: string; id: number; delta?: number };
   280	            if (c.op === 'bump') bumpRenderJob(c.id);
   281	            else if (c.op === 'remove') removeRenderJob(c.id);
   282	            else if (c.op === 'move') moveRenderJob(c.id, c.delta ?? 0);
   283	        } catch { /* skip malformed */ }
   284	    }
   285	}
   286	
   287	let cmdPollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
   288	/** Start polling the /que command file (idempotent). The queue-owning daemon calls this. */
   289	export function startQueueCommandPoll(intervalMs = 1500): void {
   290	    if (cmdPollTimer) return;
   291	    cmdPollTimer = setInterval(applyQueueCommands, intervalMs);
   292	    if (typeof cmdPollTimer.unref === 'function') cmdPollTimer.unref();
   293	}
   294	
   295	/**
   296	 * shutdownRenderQueue — best-effort cancel honesty (Amendment AC15). Drops
   297	 * every pending (not-yet-started) job, firing its onCancel so the enqueuer
   298	 * can surface an honest failure, and aborts the running job's signal.
   299	 *
   300	 * NOTE: KiraModule has no clean deinit seam today, so nothing calls this on
   301	 * daemon shutdown yet — a restart strands jobs and the orphan-scan adopts
   302	 * finished renders (Amendment AC15 accepted residual). Exported so a future
   303	 * deinit hook (or a test) can exercise the honest-cancel path.
   304	 */
   305	export function shutdownRenderQueue(): void {
   306	    shutDown = true;
   307	    const dropped = queue.splice(0, queue.length);
   308	    for (const job of dropped) {
   309	        stats.cancelled++;
   310	        console.warn(`[render-queue] ${job.kind} job cancelled by shutdown before start`);
   311	        try { job.onCancel?.(); } catch (err) {
   312	            console.warn('[render-queue] onCancel threw:', err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
   313	        }
   314	    }
   315	    currentController?.abort(new Error('render queue shut down'));
   316	}
   317	
   318	/** Test-only: reset all module state (queue, flags, counters). */
   319	export function __resetRenderQueueForTest(): void {
   320	    queue = [];
   321	    draining = false;
   322	    drainScheduled = false;
   323	    currentController = null;
   324	    shutDown = false;
   325	    stats = { enqueued: 0, completed: 0, failed: 0, cancelled: 0 };
   326	}

   109	    enabled: boolean;
   110	    intervalMinutes: number;
   111	    /** Honored again since 2026-07-28 ("unpin t2v"): 'i2v' | 't2v' | 'mixed'
   112	     *  (mixed coin-flips per video). Absent config renders i2v. */
   113	    videoMode: VideoMode;
   114	    clipSeconds: number;
   115	    /** v2 canonical: per-tier schedule + pacing (tiered scheduler, Todd
   116	     *  2026-07-27). GET always serves this; legacy flat config synthesizes it
   117	     *  behavior-preservingly (Codex B1). */
   118	    tiers: TierMap;
   119	    /** Round-robin open tiers per cycle instead of most-explicit-wins
   120	     *  (Todd 2026-07-28 "all modes all day"). */
   121	    tierRotation: boolean;
   122	    /** Flat AVOCADO projection for pre-v2 clients (GET only): mirrors
   123	     *  tiers.avocado, defaults when that tier is absent. */
   124	    imageCount: number;
   125	    videoCount: number;
   126	    unlimitedImages: boolean;
   127	    activeHours: ActiveHours | null;

   131	 * All external effects the route module performs, injectable for tests. `register`

   134	export interface KiraApiDeps {
   135	    /** BREE_HOME for this (Kira) daemon — the sentinel/config/studio root. */
   136	    breeHome(): string;
   137	    /** The consolidated, bounded per-turn read model. */
   138	    getKiraState(hints?: KiraStateTurnHints): KiraState;
   139	    /** The default content-mode store (get/set the global default). */
   140	    contentMode: { getDefault(): string; setDefault(mode: string): void };
   141	    /** The character whose studio gallery the media endpoints browse. */
   142	    character: string;
   143	    /** Enumerate gallery items (images/videos/scenes), newest-first. */
   144	    listGalleryItems(breeHome: string, character: string): GalleryItem[];
   145	
   146	    /** ComfyBox health/pool/stats triple (best-effort; fields may carry errors). */
   147	    comfyboxHealth(): Promise<{ server_health?: unknown; model_pool?: unknown; system_stats?: unknown }>;
   148	    /** Clean-slate compute-free flow. undefined ⇒ endpoint returns 501. */
   149	    computeFree?(): Promise<{ ok: boolean; detail?: string; error?: string }>;
   150	    /** Force a ComfyBox MCP reconnect. undefined ⇒ endpoint returns 501. */
   151	    comfyboxReconnect?(): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
   152	
   153	    /** Deliver a media file to the admin Telegram chat. */
   154	    deliverTelegram(mediaPath: string, kind: 'video' | 'document'): Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>;
   155	
   156	    /**
   157	     * Called after a policy PUT persists successfully. Default wiring notifies
   158	     * the live KiraContentScheduler so an `enabled` flip starts/stops the tick
   159	     * loop immediately (timer-start bug, 2026-07-25) — previously the timer
   160	     * only started at construction, so enabling a daemon that booted with
   161	     * enabled=false did nothing until a restart.
   162	     */
   163	    onPolicyChanged?(): void;
   164	
   165	    /** A6 (Todd 2026-07-27): editable character description. `set` persists
   166	     *  the override AND re-registers live; `reset` reverts to the code default. */
   167	    characterDescription: {
   168	        getDefault(): CharacterDescription;
   169	        getOverride(): CharacterDescription | null;
   170	        set(desc: CharacterDescription): void;
   171	        reset(): void;
   172	    };
   173	
   174	    /** Tiered scheduler v2: injectable legacy-migration tier (deterministic in
   175	     *  tests; production snapshots the content-mode store — Codex B1). */
   176	    legacyTierMode(): string;
   177	
   178	    /** Tiered scheduler v2: stream-mode override surface (the tab edits
   179	     *  Todd's slot; status is the shared Codex-W5 shape). */

   182	        setTodd(mode: SchedulerContentMode | null): void;

   204	function readBody(req: http.IncomingMessage): Promise<string> {
   205	    const MAX_BODY_SIZE = 1 * 1024 * 1024; // 1MB — these are tiny control payloads
   206	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   207	        const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
   208	        let total = 0;
   209	        req.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => {
   210	            total += chunk.length;
   260	    if (typeof requested !== 'string' || !requested) return null;
   261	    const studioRoot = path.resolve(deps.breeHome(), 'studio');
   262	    const resolved = path.resolve(studioRoot, requested);
   263	    if (resolved !== studioRoot && !resolved.startsWith(studioRoot + path.sep)) return null;
   264	    return resolved;
   265	}

   267	// ── Scheduler config read/write (A2) ─────────────────────────────────────────
   268	
   269	function schedulerConfigPath(deps: KiraApiDeps): string {
   270	    return path.join(deps.breeHome(), 'config.json');
   271	}
   272	
   273	function readFullConfig(deps: KiraApiDeps): Record<string, unknown> {
   274	    try {
   275	        const raw = fs.readFileSync(schedulerConfigPath(deps), 'utf-8');
   276	        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
   277	        return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {};
   278	    } catch {
   279	        return {};
   280	    }
   281	}
   282	
   283	function readSchedulerPolicy(deps: KiraApiDeps): ContentSchedulerPolicy {
   284	    const block = (readFullConfig(deps).contentScheduler ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   285	    const enabled = typeof block.enabled === 'boolean' ? block.enabled : DEFAULT_SCHEDULER_CONFIG.enabled;
   286	    const intervalMinutes = Number.isFinite(block.intervalMinutes)
   287	        ? Number(block.intervalMinutes) : DEFAULT_SCHEDULER_CONFIG.intervalMinutes;
   288	    const videoMode = (VIDEO_MODES as readonly string[]).includes(block.videoMode as string)
   289	        ? (block.videoMode as VideoMode) : DEFAULT_SCHEDULER_CONFIG.videoMode;
   290	    const clipSecondsRaw = Number(block.clipSeconds);
   291	    const clipSeconds = Number.isFinite(clipSecondsRaw)
   292	        ? Math.min(12, Math.max(4, Math.round(clipSecondsRaw)))
   293	        : DEFAULT_SCHEDULER_CONFIG.clipSeconds;
   294	    // Effective tiers: stored shape when present; legacy flat config
   295	    // synthesizes ONE tier keyed by the injectable legacy mode (deterministic
   296	    // in tests; the content-mode store in production — Codex B1).
   297	    const tiers = resolveTierMap(
   298	        block as Parameters<typeof resolveTierMap>[0],
   299	        deps.legacyTierMode(),
   300	    );
   301	    // Flat projection mirrors the LEGACY tier — avocado when present, else the
   302	    // (single) migrated tier — so a pre-v2 client sees and edits the tier that
   303	    // is actually running (Codex impl-review B1).
   304	    const legacy: TierConfig | undefined = tiers[legacyProjectionTier(tiers)];
   305	    return {
   306	        enabled,
   307	        intervalMinutes,
   308	        videoMode,
   309	        clipSeconds,
   310	        tiers,
   311	        tierRotation: block.tierRotation === true,
   312	        imageCount: Number.isInteger(legacy?.imageCount) ? Number(legacy?.imageCount) : 2,
   313	        videoCount: Number.isInteger(legacy?.videoCount) ? Number(legacy?.videoCount) : 1,
   314	        unlimitedImages: legacy?.unlimitedImages === true,
   315	        activeHours: legacy ? tierWindow(legacy) : null,
   316	    };
   317	}
   318	
   319	/** Validate + merge a partial policy update. Returns the merged block or an error. */
   320	function validatePolicyUpdate(
   321	    current: ContentSchedulerPolicy,
   322	    body: Record<string, unknown>,
   323	): { ok: true; policy: ContentSchedulerPolicy } | { ok: false; error: string } {
   324	    const next: ContentSchedulerPolicy = { ...current, tiers: { ...current.tiers } };
   325	
   326	    // Codex B2: flat pacing fields and `tiers` in one payload is ambiguous.
   327	    const FLAT_PACING = ['imageCount', 'videoCount', 'unlimitedImages', 'activeHours'] as const;
   328	    const flatKeysPresent = FLAT_PACING.filter((k) => k in body);
   329	    if ('tiers' in body && flatKeysPresent.length > 0) {
   330	        return { ok: false, error: `payload mixes tiers with flat fields (${flatKeysPresent.join(', ')}) — send one shape` };
   331	    }
   332	
   333	    if ('enabled' in body) {
   334	        if (typeof body.enabled !== 'boolean') return { ok: false, error: 'enabled must be a boolean' };
   335	        next.enabled = body.enabled;
   336	    }
   337	    if ('intervalMinutes' in body) {
   338	        const v = Number(body.intervalMinutes);
   339	        if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v <= 0) return { ok: false, error: 'intervalMinutes must be a positive number' };
   340	        next.intervalMinutes = v;
   341	    }
   342	    if ('clipSeconds' in body) {
   343	        const v = Number(body.clipSeconds);
   344	        if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v < 4 || v > 12) {
   345	            return { ok: false, error: 'clipSeconds must be a number between 4 and 12 (single-pass window; 12s = 289 frames)' };
   346	        }
   347	    }
   348	    if ('videoMode' in body) {
   349	        if (!(VIDEO_MODES as readonly string[]).includes(body.videoMode as string)) {
   350	            return { ok: false, error: `videoMode must be one of: ${VIDEO_MODES.join(', ')}` };
   351	        }
   352	        next.videoMode = body.videoMode as VideoMode;
   353	    }
   354	    if ('tierRotation' in body) {
   355	        if (typeof body.tierRotation !== 'boolean') return { ok: false, error: 'tierRotation must be a boolean' };
   356	        next.tierRotation = body.tierRotation;
   357	    }
   358	
   359	    if ('tiers' in body) {
   360	        // Full replacement (Codex B2): a tier omitted here is removed.
   361	        const raw = body.tiers;
   362	        if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(raw)) {
   363	            return { ok: false, error: 'tiers must be an object keyed by tier name' };
   364	        }
   365	        const cleaned: TierMap = {};
   366	        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(raw as Record<string, unknown>)) {
   367	            const mode = normalizeSchedulerContentMode(key);
   368	            if (!mode) return { ok: false, error: `unknown tier "${key}" — allowed: ${SCHEDULER_CONTENT_MODES.join(', ')}` };
   369	            const t = validateTier(mode, value);
   370	            if (typeof t === 'string') return { ok: false, error: t };
   371	            cleaned[mode] = t;
   372	        }
   373	        next.tiers = cleaned;
   374	    } else {
   375	        // Legacy flat pacing patches the LEGACY tier — avocado when present,
   376	        // else the migrated tier (Codex impl-review B1: an old client's
   377	        // stepper tap must not conjure a 24/7 avocado tier that wins every
   378	        // cycle). A tier created from scratch here starts on the overnight
   379	        // default window, v1's behavior — never all-hours.
   380	        const legacyTier = legacyProjectionTier(next.tiers);
   381	        const patch: TierConfig = {
   382	            ...(next.tiers[legacyTier] ?? { activeHours: DEFAULT_ACTIVE_HOURS }),
   383	        };
   384	        let touched = false;
   385	        if ('imageCount' in body) {
   386	            const v = Number(body.imageCount);
   387	            if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'imageCount must be a non-negative integer' };
   388	            patch.imageCount = v; touched = true;
   389	        }
   390	        if ('videoCount' in body) {
   391	            const v = Number(body.videoCount);
   392	            if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0) return { ok: false, error: 'videoCount must be a non-negative integer' };
   393	            patch.videoCount = v; touched = true;
   394	        }
   395	        if ('unlimitedImages' in body) {
   396	            if (typeof body.unlimitedImages !== 'boolean') {
   397	                return { ok: false, error: 'unlimitedImages must be a boolean' };
   398	            }
   399	            patch.unlimitedImages = body.unlimitedImages; touched = true;
   400	        }
   401	        if ('activeHours' in body) {
   402	            if (body.activeHours === null) {
   403	                patch.activeHours = null;   // 24/7
   404	            } else {
   405	                const hours = normalizeActiveHours(body.activeHours);
   406	                if (!hours) {
   407	                    return { ok: false, error: 'activeHours must be null (24/7) or { start, end } as "HH:MM" with start ≠ end' };
   408	                }
   409	                patch.activeHours = hours;
   410	            }
   411	            touched = true;
   412	        }
   413	        if (touched) next.tiers = { ...next.tiers, [legacyTier]: patch };
   414	    }
   415	    return { ok: true, policy: next };
   416	}
   417	
   418	/** One tier's PUT validation (Codex B3: activeHours key REQUIRED — null=24/7). */
   419	function validateTier(mode: SchedulerContentMode, value: unknown): TierConfig | string {
   420	    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
   421	        return `tier "${mode}" must be an object`;
   422	    }
   423	    const o = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   424	    if (!('activeHours' in o)) {
   425	        return `tier "${mode}" must carry an activeHours key — null for 24/7, or { start, end } "HH:MM"`;
   426	    }
   427	    const out: TierConfig = { activeHours: null };
   428	    if (o.activeHours !== null) {
   429	        const hours = normalizeActiveHours(o.activeHours);
   430	        if (!hours) return `tier "${mode}": activeHours must be null (24/7) or { start, end } as "HH:MM" with start ≠ end`;
   431	        out.activeHours = hours;
   432	    }
   433	    if ('imageCount' in o) {
   434	        const v = Number(o.imageCount);
   435	        if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0) return `tier "${mode}": imageCount must be a non-negative integer`;
   436	        out.imageCount = v;
   437	    }
   438	    if ('unlimitedImages' in o) {
   439	        if (typeof o.unlimitedImages !== 'boolean') return `tier "${mode}": unlimitedImages must be a boolean`;
   440	        out.unlimitedImages = o.unlimitedImages;
   441	    }
   442	    if ('videoCount' in o) {
   443	        const v = Number(o.videoCount);
   444	        if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0) return `tier "${mode}": videoCount must be a non-negative integer`;
   445	        if (mode === 'neutral' && v > 0) {
   446	            return 'tier "neutral" cannot render video (the Autocord shoots stills) — omit videoCount or set 0';
   447	        }
   448	        out.videoCount = v;
   449	    }
   450	    return out;
   451	}
   452	
   453	function writeSchedulerPolicy(deps: KiraApiDeps, policy: ContentSchedulerPolicy): void {
   454	    const full = readFullConfig(deps);
   455	    // Canonical tiers-only persist (Codex B2) — stale flat pacing keys are
   456	    // dropped, so the stored block has exactly one source of truth.
   457	    const prior = (full.contentScheduler ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   458	    full.contentScheduler = {
   459	        enabled: policy.enabled,
   460	        intervalMinutes: policy.intervalMinutes,
   461	        videoMode: policy.videoMode,
   462	        tiers: policy.tiers,
   463	        tierRotation: policy.tierRotation,
   464	        // arcLifecycle (Inner Loop F5) is hand-edited config outside the
   465	        // policy surface — carry it through the canonical rewrite or a
   466	        // Kira-tab policy save would silently wipe it (the config-passthrough
   467	        // gotcha that once dropped unknown keys in loadConfig).
   468	        ...('arcLifecycle' in prior ? { arcLifecycle: prior.arcLifecycle } : {}),
   469	    };
   470	    const dir = deps.breeHome();
   471	    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   472	    fs.writeFileSync(schedulerConfigPath(deps), JSON.stringify(full, null, 2));
   473	}
   474	
   475	// ── Default deps (production wiring from ctx) ────────────────────────────────
   476	
   477	function resolveDeps(ctx: DaemonContext): KiraApiDeps {
   478	    const override = ((ctx as unknown as { kiraApiDeps?: Partial<KiraApiDeps> }).kiraApiDeps) ?? {};
   479	
   480	    const defaults: KiraApiDeps = {
   580	        try {
   581	            const state = deps.getKiraState();
   582	            json(res, 200, { ok: true, state });
   583	        } catch (err) {
   584	            json(res, 500, { error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
   585	        }
   586	    });
   587	
   588	    // ── A2: content scheduler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   589	    router.get('/v1/kira/content-scheduler/status', (req, res) => {
   590	        if (denied(ctx, req, res)) return;
   591	        let paused = false;
   592	        try {
   593	            paused = fs.existsSync(path.join(deps.breeHome(), SCHEDULER_PAUSED_FILE));
   594	        } catch { /* fs unavailable → treat as not paused */ }
   595	        json(res, 200, { ok: true, paused, config: readSchedulerPolicy(deps) });
   596	    });
   597	
   598	    router.post('/v1/kira/content-scheduler/pause', (req, res) => {
   599	        if (denied(ctx, req, res)) return;
   600	        try {
   601	            const dir = deps.breeHome();
   602	            if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   603	            fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, SCHEDULER_PAUSED_FILE), `paused ${new Date().toISOString()}\n`);
   604	            json(res, 200, { ok: true, paused: true });
   605	        } catch (err) {
   606	            json(res, 500, { error: `failed to pause: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}` });
   607	        }
   608	    });
   609	
   610	    router.post('/v1/kira/content-scheduler/resume', (req, res) => {
   611	        if (denied(ctx, req, res)) return;
   612	        try {

   627	        if (denied(ctx, req, res)) return;
   628	        return (async () => {
   629	            let body: unknown;
   630	            try {
   631	                body = await parseJsonBody(req);
   632	            } catch {
   633	                json(res, 400, { error: 'Invalid JSON' });
   634	                return;
   635	            }
   636	            if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {
   637	                json(res, 400, { error: 'body must be an object' });
   638	                return;
   639	            }
   640	            const result = validatePolicyUpdate(readSchedulerPolicy(deps), body as Record<string, unknown>);
   641	            if (!result.ok) {
   642	                json(res, 400, { error: result.error });
   643	                return;
   644	            }
   645	            try {
   646	                writeSchedulerPolicy(deps, result.policy);
   647	            } catch (err) {
   648	                json(res, 500, { error: `failed to persist policy: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}` });
   649	                return;
   650	            }
   651	            // Best-effort live apply: the policy IS persisted at this point, so
   652	            // a notifier failure must not turn the response into an error.
   653	            try {
   654	                deps.onPolicyChanged?.();
   655	            } catch (err) {
   656	                console.warn(`[kira-api] policy live-apply failed (persisted anyway): ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
   657	            }
   658	            json(res, 200, { ok: true, policy: result.policy });
   659	        })().catch((err) => finalizeResponseError(res, err));
   660	    });

  1407	            "path": url.path,
  1408	            "filename": url.lastPathComponent,
  1409	            "kind": isVideo ? "video" : "image",
  1410	            "size": vals?.fileSize ?? 0,
  1411	            "modified": (vals?.contentModificationDate.map { ISO8601DateFormatter().string(from: $0) }) ?? "",
  1412	          ])
  1413	        }
  1414	      }
  1415	      items.sort { (($0["modified"] as? String) ?? "") > (($1["modified"] as? String) ?? "") }
  1416	      if items.count > limit { items = Array(items.prefix(limit)) }
  1417	      guard let data = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: ["items": items]) else {
  1418	        return .error(.error(status: 500, message: "Failed to serialize gallery list"))

  1659	  // Local video (LTX-2) ---------------------------------------------------------
  1660	
  1661	  /// Body for the local LTX-2 video route (snake_case over the wire).
  1662	  /// Decode a base64 image (image_base64) to a temp PNG and return its path, so
  1663	  /// remote clients can send an init image without a pre-existing server file.
  1664	  /// Returns nil when the string is absent/undecodable.
  1665	  private static func writeTempImage(base64: String?) -> String? {
  1666	    guard let base64, let data = Data(base64Encoded: base64) else { return nil }
  1667	    let path = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-vidinit-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  1668	    return (try? data.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: path))) != nil ? path : nil
  1669	  }
  1670	
  1671	  private struct LocalVideoRequest: Decodable {
  1672	    let prompt: String
  1673	    let negativePrompt: String?
  1674	    let imagePath: String?
  1675	    /// I2V init image sent as base64 (image_base64) for remote clients.
  1676	    let imageBase64: String?
  1677	    let width: Int?
  1678	    let height: Int?
  1679	    let frames: Int?
  1680	    let steps: Int?
  1681	    let seed: UInt64?
  1682	    let strength: Float?
  1683	    /// Conditioning compression (libx264 CRF) override for THIS render — the
  1684	    /// daemon sends a higher value (more motion) for partnered-action prompts
  1685	    /// and a low value (fidelity) for solo/portrait. nil = env default.
  1686	    let imgCompression: Int?
  1687	    /// CFG guidance override (>1 amplifies action; motion recipe sends 2.0 for
  1688	    /// partnered-action, omits for solo=fidelity). nil = env/config default.
  1689	    let guidance: Float?
  1690	    let extendToSeconds: Float?
  1691	    /// Target duration in seconds — the daemon/MCP vocabulary. For local
  1692	    /// renders this maps onto `extendToSeconds` (chunked continuation, each
  1693	    /// chunk re-anchored on the previous chunk\u{27}s last frame) when it
  1694	    /// exceeds one chunk. `extend_to_seconds` still wins when both are set.
  1695	    let duration: Float?
  1696	    /// Identity re-anchor strength for continuation chunks (0 disables).
  1697	    /// Default 0.5 for extended renders \u{2014} counters per-chunk subject drift.
  1698	    let identityAnchorStrength: Float?
  1699	    let fps: Int?
  1700	    let loraPath: String?
  1701	    let loraStrength: Float?
  1702	    /// Multiple LoRAs, applied in order — same {path, scale} shape as image
  1703	    /// LoRA requests. `loraPath`/`loraStrength` still work for a single LoRA.
  1704	    let loras: [LoRAEntry]?
  1705	    let outputPath: String?
  1706	    /// Which client/app submitted this job (desktop, bree, api…) — surfaced in
  1707	    /// the async job status and /health, same as image `GeneratePayload.source`.
  1708	    let source: String?
  1709	    /// Tier A tuning overrides (snake_case JSON via decoder strategy).
  1710	    let tuning: LTX2VideoTuning?
  1711	    /// Server-minted id from /v1/enhance binding this render to its
  1712	    /// optimization lineage (task #19, finding #6).
  1713	    let optimizationAttemptId: String?
  1714	    /// Optional preset id resolved from the shared PresetStore (mediaKind
  1715	    /// "video"): LoRAs, prompt prefix/suffix, negative prompt, dims budget,
  1716	    /// steps, seed. Explicit request fields always override preset values.
  1717	    let preset: String?
  1718	    /// Character whose canonical description is prepended to the prompt so the
  1719	    /// subject renders on-model. For T2V (no init image) this is the ONLY
  1720	    /// identity source; defaults to "kira" when unset. For I2V the init image
  1721	    /// already carries identity, so it's injected only when explicitly named.
  1722	    let character: String?
  1723	    /// Content mode (neutral/apple/banana/avocado) gating the character's
  1724	    /// mode-specific description addendum. Defaults to the server's current mode.
  1725	    let contentMode: String?
  1726	    /// Auto-enhance the prompt through the configured prompt-optimization
  1727	    /// provider (Dan's-PE via LM Studio) before encoding. Default on when a
  1728	    /// provider is configured; set false to send the raw prompt.
  1729	    let enhance: Bool?
  1730	    /// Named resolution budget: "480p" | "720p" | "1080p". Maps to a
  1731	    /// width x height pixel budget when explicit width/height are absent
  1732	    /// (previously this key was silently DROPPED on the local path).
  1733	    let resolution: String?
  1734	    /// Aspect ratio for the resolution budget: "16:9" (default) or "9:16".
  1735	    /// For I2V the source image's aspect still wins (budget only).
  1736	    let aspectRatio: String?
  1737	  }
  1738	
  1739	  /// Map a named resolution + aspect to a width x height budget. Dims are
  1740	  /// budgets, not finals — the existing /64 snapping and I2V source-aspect
  1741	  /// derivation still apply downstream.
  1742	  private static func videoDims(resolution: String?, aspectRatio: String?) -> (width: Int, height: Int)? {
  1743	    guard let res = resolution?.lowercased() else { return nil }
  1744	    let landscape: (Int, Int)
  1745	    switch res {
  1746	    case "480p": landscape = (832, 480)
  1747	    case "720p": landscape = (1280, 720)
  1748	    case "1080p": landscape = (1920, 1080)
  1749	    default: return nil
  1750	    }
  1751	    let portrait = (aspectRatio ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
  1752	    return portrait ? (landscape.1, landscape.0) : landscape
  1753	  }
  1754	
  1755	  private struct LocalVideoResponse: Encodable {
  1756	    let success: Bool
  1757	    let outputPath: String
  1758	    let frameCount: Int
  1759	    let durationSeconds: Float
  1760	    let elapsedSeconds: Double
  1761	    let backend: String
  1762	  }
