  1764	  /// Submit a video render to the paid Replicate cloud proxy and return its 202
  1765	  /// job status (already submit-and-poll). Shared by the sync and async video
  1766	  /// routes for the cloud / unspecified-fallback case.
  1767	  private func submitReplicateVideo(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse {
  1768	    guard let proxy = replicateVideoProxy else {
  1769	      return .error(.error(status: 503, message: "Video generation not available: configure LTX-2 (--ltx2-weights) for local video, or a Replicate API key for cloud"))
  1770	    }
  1771	    logger.info("video: routing to Replicate cloud (\(ReplicateVideoProxy.i2vModel))")
  1772	    do {
  1773	      var videoRequest = try decode(VideoGenerateRequest.self, from: body)
  1774	      // Accept a bytes-uploaded init image (image_base64) when no path is given.
  1775	      if videoRequest.imagePath == nil, let tempPath = Self.writeTempImage(base64: videoRequest.imageBase64) {
  1776	        videoRequest.imagePath = tempPath
  1777	      }
  1778	      if let validationError = videoRequest.validate() {
  1779	        return .error(.error(status: 400, message: validationError))
  1780	      }
  1781	      // Enforce output path containment within the allowed output directory
  1782	      // (throws WarmServerError.invalidOutputPath -> 400 via response(for:)).
  1783	      if let outputPath = videoRequest.outputPath, !outputPath.isEmpty {
  1784	        _ = try WarmServerOutputPathValidator.resolveOutputPath(
  1785	          outputPath,
  1786	          allowedOutputDirectory: configuration.allowedOutputDirectory
  1787	        )
  1788	      }
  1789	      // I2V: verify image_path exists, is a regular file, and has PNG/JPEG
  1790	      // magic bytes before it gets base64-uploaded to Replicate.
  1791	      if let imagePath = videoRequest.imagePath {
  1792	        if let imageError = ReplicateVideoProxy.validateSourceImage(atPath: imagePath) {
  1793	          return .error(.error(status: 400, message: imageError))
  1794	        }
  1795	      }
  1796	      let jobStatus = await proxy.submit(videoRequest)
  1797	      let encoder = JSONEncoder()
  1798	      encoder.keyEncodingStrategy = .convertToSnakeCase
  1799	      let data = try encoder.encode(jobStatus)
  1800	      return .json(.rawJSON(status: 202, data: data))
  1801	    } catch {
  1802	      return .error(response(for: error))
  1803	    }
  1804	  }
  1805	
  1806	  /// A LTX-2 generator + validated request, ready to enqueue. Shared by the
  1807	  /// synchronous (`/v1/video/generate`) and async (`/v1/video/generate/async`)
  1808	  /// local video paths so they build the render identically.
  1809	  private struct PreparedLocalVideo {
  1810	    let generator: LTX2VideoGenerator
  1811	    let request: LTX2VideoRequest
  1812	    /// t2v when there's no init image, i2v otherwise.
  1813	    let mode: VideoMode
  1814	    let source: String
  1815	    /// Lineage reference from /v1/enhance, if the caller optimized first.
  1816	    let optimizationAttemptId: String?
  1817	  }
  1818	
  1819	  /// Map the daemon/MCP `duration` field onto chunked continuation: 0 when
  1820	  /// the request fits one chunk (single-chunk render, no continuation cost),
  1821	  /// else the requested seconds. Pure for unit testing.
  1822	  static func extendSecondsFromDuration(_ duration: Float?, framesPerChunk: Int, fps: Int) -> Float {
  1823	    guard let seconds = duration, seconds > 0, fps > 0 else { return 0 }
  1824	    let singleChunkSeconds = Float(framesPerChunk) / Float(fps)
  1825	    return seconds > singleChunkSeconds ? seconds : 0
  1826	  }
  1827	
  1828	  /// Whether a video request renders more than one chunk (continuation path).
  1829	  /// Mirrors the extendToSeconds resolution above — the identity anchor
  1830	  /// defaults on only for these (#231).
  1831	  static func isExtendedRender(
  1832	    extendToSeconds: Float?, duration: Float?, framesPerChunk: Int, fps: Int
  1833	  ) -> Bool {
  1834	    if let explicit = extendToSeconds { return explicit > 0 }
  1835	    return extendSecondsFromDuration(
  1836	      duration, framesPerChunk: framesPerChunk, fps: fps) > 0
  1837	  }
  1838	
  1839	  /// Snap a render dimension to the nearest multiple of 64 (floor 256).
  1840	  /// LTX-2 renders at dims that are 32-multiples but NOT 64-multiples (e.g.
  1841	  /// 480) exhibit progressive haze (#219) — every clean render in the 07-13
  1842	  /// bisect used /64 dims, every hazy one used 480.
  1843	  /// Resolve the stage-1 (painted) dims for a two-stage render whose request
  1844	  /// dims mean the FINAL output size. Pure, so it can be tested at the sizes
  1845	  /// callers actually send rather than only the ones convenient to validate.
  1846	  ///
  1847	  /// The refine SHARPENS what stage 1 painted; it cannot invent detail that was
  1848	  /// never generated. Halving a request sized for the old single-pass
  1849	  /// convention therefore degrades output silently — Kira's 704x448 became a
  1850	  /// 384x256 base pass (a third of her previous pixels) and went visibly
  1851	  /// diffuse, while every render validated that day asked for 960x576 and
  1852	  /// halved comfortably to 512x320 (2026-08-02).
  1853	  ///
  1854	  /// Below the floor the request is treated as STAGE-1 dims (pre-halving
  1855	  /// behaviour): the clip finishes at 2x the requested size. A sharp surprise
  1856	  /// beats a soft silent degradation.
  1857	  ///
  1858	  /// - Returns: the dims to paint at, and whether halving was applied.
  1859	  static func stageOneDims(
  1860	    finalWidth: Int, finalHeight: Int, floorPixels: Int = 512 * 320
  1861	  ) -> (width: Int, height: Int, halved: Bool) {
  1862	    let w = snapDim64(finalWidth / 2)
  1863	    let h = snapDim64(finalHeight / 2)
  1864	    guard w * h >= floorPixels else {
  1865	      return (finalWidth, finalHeight, false)
  1866	    }
  1867	    return (w, h, true)
  1868	  }
  1869	
  1870	  static func snapDim64(_ value: Int) -> Int {
  1871	    max(256, Int((Double(value) / 64.0).rounded()) * 64)
  1872	  }
  1873	
  1874	  /// Derive I2V render dims matching the source image aspect within the
  1875	  /// requested pixel-area budget, both dims /64. Pure for unit testing.
  1876	  ///
  1877	  /// Rounding each axis to /64 independently compounds error in opposite
  1878	  /// directions: a 1664x896 source (aspect 1.857) at a 448x704 budget produced
  1879	  /// 768x384 (aspect 2.000) — the height's ideal 412.1 sat almost exactly on a
  1880	  /// 64-boundary midpoint and rounded DOWN while the width rounded up, a 7.7%
  1881	  /// distortion that visibly squashes the subject (2026-08-01). Search the /64
  1882	  /// neighbourhood instead and keep the pair whose aspect is closest to the
  1883	  /// source, breaking ties toward the pixel budget.
  1884	  static func deriveVideoDims(
  1885	    sourceWidth: Int, sourceHeight: Int, budgetWidth: Int, budgetHeight: Int
  1886	  ) -> (width: Int, height: Int) {
  1887	    guard sourceWidth > 0, sourceHeight > 0 else {
  1888	      return (snapDim64(budgetWidth), snapDim64(budgetHeight))
  1889	    }
  1890	    let aspect = Double(sourceWidth) / Double(sourceHeight)
  1891	    let budget = Double(max(budgetWidth, 64) * max(budgetHeight, 64))
  1892	    let idealW = (budget * aspect).squareRoot()
  1893	    let idealH = idealW / aspect
  1894	
  1895	    let baseW = Int((idealW / 64.0).rounded())
  1896	    let baseH = Int((idealH / 64.0).rounded())
  1897	
  1898	    func search(areaCap: Double) -> (w: Int, h: Int, aspectErr: Double, areaErr: Double)? {
  1899	      var best: (w: Int, h: Int, aspectErr: Double, areaErr: Double)?
  1900	      for dw in -1...1 {
  2180	        foldedExtendSeconds = 0  // 0 = no continuation chunks
  2181	      }
  2182	    }
  2183	
  2184	    let videoRequest = LTX2VideoRequest(
  2185	      prompt: effectivePrompt,
  2186	      negativePrompt: req.negativePrompt ?? videoPreset?.negativePrompt,
  2187	      initImagePath: effectiveInitImage,
  2188	      width: renderWidth,
  2189	      height: renderHeight,
  2190	      framesPerChunk: foldedFramesPerChunk,
  2191	      steps: req.steps ?? videoPreset?.steps ?? 8,
  2192	      seed: req.seed ?? videoPreset?.seed.map(UInt64.init) ?? 42,
  2193	      strength: req.strength ?? 1.0,
  2194	      imgCompression: req.imgCompression,
  2195	      guidance: req.guidance,
  2196	      // Re-enabled by default for EXTENDED renders (#231, 2026-07-16): the
  2197	      // 2026-07-13 MLX mutex crash on this path was memory pressure — with
  2198	      // the int8 stack (#230) a 12s/3-chunk anchored render completed clean
  2199	      // (289f, no crash). Single-chunk renders don't anchor (nothing to
  2200	      // drift); callers can still pass 0 to disable.
  2201	      // Mid-pass identity re-anchor is OPT-IN and default OFF — it was superseded
  2202	      // by the face-region anchor (LTX2_FACE_ANCHOR_STRENGTH), which holds partner
  2203	      // faces without the multi-keyframe gap-collapse. Enable explicitly via
  2204	      // LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL>0 (+ _STRENGTH); a standard 97f/4s render NEVER takes
  2205	      // it unless the interval is set below the frame count. Only the pre-existing
  2206	      // extended/chunked anchor stays on by default (unchanged behavior).
  2207	      identityAnchorStrength: req.identityAnchorStrength
  2208	        ?? (Self.isExtendedRender(
  2209	              extendToSeconds: req.extendToSeconds, duration: req.duration,
  2210	              framesPerChunk: req.frames ?? 97, fps: req.fps ?? 24)
  2211	            ? 0.5
  2212	            : ((effectiveInitImage != nil
  2213	                && (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0) > 0
  2214	                && (req.frames ?? 97) > (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0))
  2215	               ? (Float(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_STRENGTH"] ?? "") ?? 0.4) : 0)),
  2216	      identityReAnchorInterval: (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0),
  2217	      extendToSeconds: foldedExtendSeconds,
  2218	      fps: req.fps ?? 24,
  2219	      loraPath: req.loraPath,
  2220	      loraStrength: req.loraStrength ?? 1.0,
  2221	      loras: resolvedLoRAs,
  2222	      outputPath: resolvedOutput
  2223	,
  2224	      tuning: req.tuning,
  2225	      presetTuning: videoPreset?.videoTuning
  2226	    )
  2227	    // Validate before enqueuing so bad frames/dims fail fast.
  2228	    try generator.validate(videoRequest)
  2229	
  2230	    return PreparedLocalVideo(
  2231	      generator: generator,
  2232	      request: videoRequest,
  2233	      mode: (effectiveInitImage?.isEmpty == false) ? .i2v : .t2v,
  2234	      source: req.source ?? "api",
  2235	      optimizationAttemptId: req.optimizationAttemptId)
  2236	  }
  2237	
  2238	  /// If LTX-2 is configured, ASYNC-submit the local render and return 202 + a
  2239	  /// job status immediately; otherwise nil so the caller falls through to the
  2240	  /// Replicate proxy. Poll GET /v1/video/status/{id} for completion. This is the
  2241	  /// path a long (multi-minute / multi-chunk) render must take — it never holds
  2242	  /// the HTTP connection open for the whole denoise.
  2243	  private func localVideoAsyncResponseIfConfigured(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse? {
  2244	    do {
  2245	      guard let prep = try await prepareLocalVideo(body: body) else { return nil }
  2246	      logger.info("LTX-2: local video job submitted (\(prep.request.width)x\(prep.request.height), \(prep.request.framesPerChunk)f)")
  2247	      var tracePayload: [String: String] = ["prompt": prep.request.prompt]
  2248	      if let attemptId = prep.optimizationAttemptId {
  2249	        tracePayload["optimization_attempt_id"] = attemptId
  2250	      }
  2251	      let status = videoJobTracker.submit(
  2252	        source: prep.source, mode: prep.mode, coordinator: coordinator,
  2253	        // Snapshot at SUBMIT time (finding #15): the authoritative resolution
  2254	        // this render will use, durable on the job status.
  2255	        resolvedConfig: LTX2ConfigResolver.resolveTyped(
  2256	          request: prep.request.tuning, preset: prep.request.presetTuning).params,
  2257	        tracePayload: tracePayload
  2258	      ) { report in
  2259	        try prep.generator.generate(prep.request) { chunk, totalChunks, step, totalSteps in
  2260	          report(Self.localVideoProgressPercent(
  2261	            chunk: chunk, totalChunks: totalChunks, step: step, totalSteps: totalSteps))
  2262	        }
  2263	      }
  2264	      let encoder = JSONEncoder()
  2265	      encoder.keyEncodingStrategy = .convertToSnakeCase
  2266	      let data = try encoder.encode(status)
  2267	      return .json(.rawJSON(status: 202, data: data))
  2268	    } catch let error as LTX2VideoError {
  2269	      return .error(.error(status: 400, message: error.localizedDescription))
  2270	    } catch {
  2271	      return .error(response(for: error))
  2272	    }
  2273	  }
  2274	
  2275	  /// If LTX-2 is configured, generate the video locally and return the result
  2276	  /// SYNCHRONOUSLY (blocks the HTTP connection for the whole render); otherwise
  2277	  /// nil so the caller falls through to the Replicate proxy. Kept for backward
  2278	  /// compatibility — new/long renders should use the async path above.
  2279	  private func localVideoResponseIfConfigured(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse? {
  2280	    guard configuration.ltx2WeightsPath != nil, configuration.ltx2GemmaPath != nil else {
  2281	      return nil
  2282	    }
  2283	    do {
  2284	      guard let prep = try await prepareLocalVideo(body: body) else { return nil }
  2285	      let generator = prep.generator
  2286	      let videoRequest = prep.request
  2287	
  2288	      logger.info("LTX-2: local video request queued (\(videoRequest.width)x\(videoRequest.height), \(videoRequest.framesPerChunk)f)")
  2289	      let result = try await coordinator.enqueueLocalVideo { report in
  2290	        try generator.generate(videoRequest) { chunk, totalChunks, step, totalSteps in
  2291	          report(Self.localVideoProgressPercent(
  2292	            chunk: chunk, totalChunks: totalChunks, step: step, totalSteps: totalSteps))
  2293	        }
  2294	      }
  2295	      auditLog.append(kind: "video.local", message: "LTX-2 video \(result.frameCount)f -> \(result.outputPath)")
  2296	      return .json(status: 200, payload: LocalVideoResponse(
  2297	        success: true,
  2298	        outputPath: result.outputPath,
  2299	        frameCount: result.frameCount,
  2300	        durationSeconds: result.durationSeconds,
  2301	        elapsedSeconds: result.elapsedSeconds,
  2302	        backend: "ltx2-local"
  2303	      ))
  2304	    } catch let error as LTX2VideoError {
  2305	      return .error(.error(status: 400, message: error.localizedDescription))
  2306	    } catch {
  2307	      return .error(.error(status: 500, message: "LTX-2 video failed: \(error.localizedDescription)"))
  2308	    }
  2309	  }

  2311	  // Queue ----------------------------------------------------------------------
  2312	
  2313	  /// GET /v1/queue: the active operation + every pending job (cancellable by id).
  2314	  /// GET /v1/queue — served from the lock-based ``LiveHealthState`` snapshot
  2315	  /// instead of `await coordinator.queueSnapshot()` so the Queue tab stays
  2316	  /// responsive during a render, matching the /health fix (#217). Hopping
  2317	  /// onto the actor here queued this request behind the whole render and
  2318	  /// also read `isRendering` off a stale field, so the tab showed an empty,
  2319	  /// not-rendering queue while a job was actually active.
  2320	  private func queueListResponse() async -> RoutedResponse {
  2321	    let (snap, progress) = liveHealth.read()
  2322	    let iso = ISO8601DateFormatter()
  2323	    var payload: [String: Any] = [
  2324	      "is_rendering": snap.isRendering,
  2325	      "is_paused": snap.isPaused,
  2326	      "max_pending": snap.maxPending,
  2327	      "render_count": snap.renderCount,
  2328	      "failed_count": snap.failedRenderCount,
  2329	      "pending": snap.pending.map { job in
  2330	        [
  2331	          "id": job.id,
  2332	          "kind": job.kind,
  2333	          "summary": job.summary,
  2334	          "source": job.source,
  2335	          "enqueued_at": iso.string(from: job.enqueuedAt),
  2336	        ] as [String: Any]
  2337	      },
  2338	    ]
  2339	    if let id = snap.activeJobId { payload["active_job_id"] = id }
  2340	    if let summary = snap.activeSummary { payload["active_summary"] = summary }
  2341	    if let source = snap.activeSource { payload["active_source"] = source }
  2342	    if let started = snap.activeRenderStartedAt { payload["active_started_at"] = iso.string(from: started) }
  2343	    if let pct = progress { payload["progress_percent"] = pct }
  2344	    guard let data = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: payload) else {
  2345	      return .error(.error(status: 500, message: "Failed to serialize queue snapshot"))
  2346	    }
  2347	    return .json(.rawJSON(status: 200, data: data))
  2348	  }

  1659	  // Local video (LTX-2) ---------------------------------------------------------
  1660	
  1661	  /// Body for the local LTX-2 video route (snake_case over the wire).
  1662	  /// Decode a base64 image (image_base64) to a temp PNG and return its path, so
  1663	  /// remote clients can send an init image without a pre-existing server file.
  1664	  /// Returns nil when the string is absent/undecodable.
  1665	  private static func writeTempImage(base64: String?) -> String? {
  1666	    guard let base64, let data = Data(base64Encoded: base64) else { return nil }
  1667	    let path = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-vidinit-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  1668	    return (try? data.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: path))) != nil ? path : nil
  1669	  }

  1671	  private struct LocalVideoRequest: Decodable {
  1672	    let prompt: String
  1673	    let negativePrompt: String?
  1674	    let imagePath: String?
  1675	    /// I2V init image sent as base64 (image_base64) for remote clients.
  1676	    let imageBase64: String?
  1677	    let width: Int?
  1678	    let height: Int?
  1679	    let frames: Int?
  1680	    let steps: Int?
  1681	    let seed: UInt64?
  1682	    let strength: Float?
  1683	    /// Conditioning compression (libx264 CRF) override for THIS render — the
  1684	    /// daemon sends a higher value (more motion) for partnered-action prompts
  1685	    /// and a low value (fidelity) for solo/portrait. nil = env default.
  1686	    let imgCompression: Int?
  1687	    /// CFG guidance override (>1 amplifies action; motion recipe sends 2.0 for
  1688	    /// partnered-action, omits for solo=fidelity). nil = env/config default.
  1689	    let guidance: Float?
  1690	    let extendToSeconds: Float?
  1691	    /// Target duration in seconds — the daemon/MCP vocabulary. For local
  1692	    /// renders this maps onto `extendToSeconds` (chunked continuation, each
  1693	    /// chunk re-anchored on the previous chunk\u{27}s last frame) when it
  1694	    /// exceeds one chunk. `extend_to_seconds` still wins when both are set.
  1695	    let duration: Float?
  1696	    /// Identity re-anchor strength for continuation chunks (0 disables).
  1697	    /// Default 0.5 for extended renders \u{2014} counters per-chunk subject drift.
  1698	    let identityAnchorStrength: Float?
  1699	    let fps: Int?
  1700	    let loraPath: String?
  1701	    let loraStrength: Float?
  1702	    /// Multiple LoRAs, applied in order — same {path, scale} shape as image
  1703	    /// LoRA requests. `loraPath`/`loraStrength` still work for a single LoRA.
  1704	    let loras: [LoRAEntry]?
  1705	    let outputPath: String?
  1706	    /// Which client/app submitted this job (desktop, bree, api…) — surfaced in
  1707	    /// the async job status and /health, same as image `GeneratePayload.source`.
  1708	    let source: String?
  1709	    /// Tier A tuning overrides (snake_case JSON via decoder strategy).
  1710	    let tuning: LTX2VideoTuning?
  1711	    /// Server-minted id from /v1/enhance binding this render to its
  1712	    /// optimization lineage (task #19, finding #6).
  1713	    let optimizationAttemptId: String?
  1714	    /// Optional preset id resolved from the shared PresetStore (mediaKind
  1715	    /// "video"): LoRAs, prompt prefix/suffix, negative prompt, dims budget,
  1716	    /// steps, seed. Explicit request fields always override preset values.
  1717	    let preset: String?
  1718	    /// Character whose canonical description is prepended to the prompt so the
  1719	    /// subject renders on-model. For T2V (no init image) this is the ONLY
  1720	    /// identity source; defaults to "kira" when unset. For I2V the init image
  1721	    /// already carries identity, so it's injected only when explicitly named.
  1722	    let character: String?
  1723	    /// Content mode (neutral/apple/banana/avocado) gating the character's
  1724	    /// mode-specific description addendum. Defaults to the server's current mode.
  1725	    let contentMode: String?
  1726	    /// Auto-enhance the prompt through the configured prompt-optimization
  1727	    /// provider (Dan's-PE via LM Studio) before encoding. Default on when a
  1728	    /// provider is configured; set false to send the raw prompt.
  1729	    let enhance: Bool?
  1730	    /// Named resolution budget: "480p" | "720p" | "1080p". Maps to a
  1731	    /// width x height pixel budget when explicit width/height are absent
  1732	    /// (previously this key was silently DROPPED on the local path).
  1733	    let resolution: String?
  1734	    /// Aspect ratio for the resolution budget: "16:9" (default) or "9:16".
  1735	    /// For I2V the source image's aspect still wins (budget only).
  1736	    let aspectRatio: String?
  1737	  }
  1738	
  1739	  /// Map a named resolution + aspect to a width x height budget. Dims are
  1740	  /// budgets, not finals — the existing /64 snapping and I2V source-aspect
  1741	  /// derivation still apply downstream.
  1742	  private static func videoDims(resolution: String?, aspectRatio: String?) -> (width: Int, height: Int)? {
  1743	    guard let res = resolution?.lowercased() else { return nil }
  1744	    let landscape: (Int, Int)
  1745	    switch res {
  1746	    case "480p": landscape = (832, 480)
  1747	    case "720p": landscape = (1280, 720)
  1748	    case "1080p": landscape = (1920, 1080)
  1749	    default: return nil
  1750	    }
  1751	    let portrait = (aspectRatio ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
  1752	    return portrait ? (landscape.1, landscape.0) : landscape
  1753	  }
  1754	
  1755	  private struct LocalVideoResponse: Encodable {
  1756	    let success: Bool
  1757	    let outputPath: String
  1758	    let frameCount: Int
  1759	    let durationSeconds: Float
  1760	    let elapsedSeconds: Double
  1950	    let done = max(0, chunk) * steps + max(0, step)
  1951	    let total = chunks * steps
  1952	    return min(100, max(0, Int((Double(done) / Double(total)) * 100.0)))
  1953	  }
  1954	
  1955	  /// Resolve LTX-2 weights, build + validate the render request. Returns nil when
  1956	  /// local LTX-2 isn't configured (caller falls through to Replicate); throws for
  1957	  /// a malformed request or invalid output path.
  1958	  private func prepareLocalVideo(body: Data) async throws -> PreparedLocalVideo? {
  1959	    guard let weights = configuration.ltx2WeightsPath, let gemma = configuration.ltx2GemmaPath else {
  1960	      return nil
  1961	    }
  1962	    let req = try decode(LocalVideoRequest.self, from: body)
  1963	
  1964	    // Video presets — same PresetStore as images (mediaKind "video"). A
  1965	    // preset is a named bundle: LoRAs (bare filenames resolve through the
  1966	    // LoRA library search roots), prompt shaping, negative prompt, dims
  1967	    // budget, steps, seed. Explicit request fields win over the preset.
  1968	    var videoPreset: ImagePreset? = nil
  1969	    if let presetId = req.preset, !presetId.isEmpty {
  1970	      guard let found = presetStore.get(presetId) else {
  1971	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: "Unknown preset \u{27}\(presetId)\u{27} — see /v1/presets")
  1972	      }
  1973	      videoPreset = found
  1974	      logger.info("LTX-2: applying video preset \u{27}\(presetId)\u{27} (\(found.loras.count) LoRA(s))")
  1975	    }
  1976	
  1977	    // Contain the output within the allowed directory. A relative (or absent)
  1978	    // output path is resolved against the ALLOWED directory, not the process
  1979	    // CWD — under launchd the CWD is the repo checkout, so the old bare-name
  1980	    // default failed its own containment check on every submit (#219).
  1981	    let requestedOutputRaw = req.outputPath ?? "ltx2-\(UUID().uuidString).mp4"
  1982	    let requestedOutput: String
  1983	    if requestedOutputRaw.hasPrefix("/") || requestedOutputRaw.hasPrefix("~") {
  1984	      requestedOutput = requestedOutputRaw
  1985	    } else {
  1986	      requestedOutput = (configuration.allowedOutputDirectory as NSString)
  1987	        .appendingPathComponent(requestedOutputRaw)
  1988	    }
  1989	    let resolvedOutput = try WarmServerOutputPathValidator.resolveOutputPath(
  1990	      requestedOutput, allowedOutputDirectory: configuration.allowedOutputDirectory).path
  1991	
  1992	    let generator: LTX2VideoGenerator
  1993	    if let existing = videoHolder.get() {
  1994	      generator = existing
  1995	    } else {
  1996	      logger.info("LTX-2: resolving weights/text-encoder (downloads on first use if not cached)…")
  1997	      let weightsURL = try await ModelResolution.resolve(
  1998	        modelSpec: weights,
  1999	        filePatterns: ["transformer-distilled.safetensors", "connector.safetensors",
  2000	                        "vae_decoder.safetensors", "vae_encoder.safetensors", "config.json"]
  2001	      )
  2002	      let gemmaURL = try await ModelResolution.resolve(
  2003	        modelSpec: gemma,
  2004	        filePatterns: ["*.safetensors", "*.json", "tokenizer/*", "*.model"]
  2005	      )
  2006	      generator = LTX2VideoGenerator(
  2007	        config: .init(weightsDir: weightsURL.path, gemmaPath: gemmaURL.path), logger: logger)
  2008	    }
  2009	    // Publish into the shared holder so the coordinator can evict it before an
  2010	    // image load, and so the render queue evicts image models before this one
  2011	    // actually loads its ~65GB of weights inside generate() (#218).
  2012	    videoHolder.set(generator)
  2013	
  2014	    // Accept an init image as bytes (image_base64) when no server path is given.
  2015	    let effectiveInitImage = req.imagePath ?? Self.writeTempImage(base64: req.imageBase64)
  2016	
  2017	    var loraEntries: [LoRAEntry] = req.loras ?? []
  2018	    if loraEntries.isEmpty, req.loraPath == nil, let preset = videoPreset, !preset.loras.isEmpty {
  2019	      loraEntries = preset.loras.map { LoRAEntry(path: $0.filename, scale: Float($0.scale)) }
  2020	    }
  2021	    if loraEntries.isEmpty, req.loraPath == nil,
  2022	       let defaultLoRA = configuration.ltx2DefaultLoRA, !defaultLoRA.isEmpty {
  2023	      // "path" or "path@scale"
  2024	      let parts = defaultLoRA.split(separator: "@", maxSplits: 1).map(String.init)
  2025	      let scale = parts.count == 2 ? Float(parts[1]) ?? 1.0 : 1.0
  2026	      loraEntries = [LoRAEntry(path: parts[0], scale: scale)]
  2027	      logger.info("LTX-2: applying default video LoRA \(parts[0]) @ \(scale) (--ltx2-lora)")
  2028	    }
  2029	    let resolvedLoRAs: [LTX2LoRAReference] = try loraEntries.map { entry in
  2030	      let config = try entry.makeConfiguration()
  2031	      guard case .local(let url) = config.source else {
  2032	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  2033	          message: "LTX-2 video LoRAs must be local files (got a HuggingFace reference for '\(entry.path)')")
  2034	      }
  2035	      return LTX2LoRAReference(path: url.path, scale: config.scale)
  2036	    }
  2037	
  2038	    // LTX-2 render dims must be divisible by 64: 32-multiples that are not
  2039	    // 64-multiples (e.g. 480) produce progressive haze/ghosting (#219). I2V
  2040	    // output must additionally match the SOURCE image aspect ratio — a fixed
  2041	    // preset like 704x448 applied to a portrait source distorts the
  2042	    // conditioning frame and the render drifts off the image. The requested
  2043	    // width x height is kept only as a pixel-area budget for I2V.
  2044	    // Priority: explicit width/height > named resolution ("720p" etc., FIXED:
  2045	    // previously silently dropped) > preset dims > 704x448 default.
  2046	    let namedDims = Self.videoDims(resolution: req.resolution, aspectRatio: req.aspectRatio)
  2047	    var renderWidth = req.width ?? namedDims?.width ?? videoPreset?.width ?? 704
  2048	    var renderHeight = req.height ?? namedDims?.height ?? videoPreset?.height ?? 448
  2049	    if req.width == nil, let nd = namedDims {
  2050	      logger.info("LTX-2: resolution '\(req.resolution ?? "")' -> \(nd.width)x\(nd.height) budget")
  2051	    }
  2052	    if let initPath = effectiveInitImage,
  2053	       let sourceSize = Self.imagePixelSize(atPath: initPath) {
  2054	      let derived = Self.deriveVideoDims(
  2055	        sourceWidth: sourceSize.width, sourceHeight: sourceSize.height,
  2056	        budgetWidth: renderWidth, budgetHeight: renderHeight)
  2057	      if derived.width != renderWidth || derived.height != renderHeight {
  2058	        logger.info(
  2059	          "LTX-2 I2V: adjusted \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) -> \(derived.width)x\(derived.height) (source \(sourceSize.width)x\(sourceSize.height), aspect-matched, /64)")
  2060	        renderWidth = derived.width
  2061	        renderHeight = derived.height
  2062	      }
  2063	    } else {
  2064	      let snappedW = Self.snapDim64(renderWidth)
  2065	      let snappedH = Self.snapDim64(renderHeight)
  2066	      if snappedW != renderWidth || snappedH != renderHeight {
  2067	        logger.info("LTX-2: snapped \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) -> \(snappedW)x\(snappedH) (dims must be /64, #219)")
  2068	        renderWidth = snappedW
  2069	        renderHeight = snappedH
  2070	      }
  2071	    }
  2072	    // Two-stage dims convention (2026-08-02): with LTX2_TWO_STAGE=1 the request
  2073	    // dims are the FINAL output size (matching ComfyUI and every caller's
  2074	    // intuition). ComfyBox's pipeline doubles stage-1 dims through the refine,
  2075	    // so hand it the /64-snapped HALF. Without this, enabling two-stage doubled
  2076	    // every clip's output size and the refine-volume gate silently skipped the
  2077	    // refine — the worst of both worlds. Stage-1 /64 keeps the final /128-ish
  2078	    // and matches all validated two-stage renders (stage 1 at 448x256 etc.).
  2079	    // Typed resolution honors request/preset tuning overrides (finding #18):
  2080	    // a request can enable two-stage without the plist knowing.
  2081	    if LTX2ConfigResolver.resolveTyped(request: req.tuning, preset: videoPreset?.videoTuning).twoStage {
  2082	      let s1 = Self.stageOneDims(finalWidth: renderWidth, finalHeight: renderHeight)
  2083	      if s1.halved {
  2084	        logger.info(
  2085	          "LTX-2 two-stage: request dims \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) = FINAL; stage 1 paints \(s1.width)x\(s1.height), refine doubles to \(s1.width * 2)x\(s1.height * 2)")
  2086	        renderWidth = s1.width
  2087	        renderHeight = s1.height
  2088	      } else {
  2089	        logger.warning("""
  2090	          LTX-2 two-stage: request \(renderWidth)x\(renderHeight) would paint stage 1 at \
  2091	          \(Self.snapDim64(renderWidth / 2))x\(Self.snapDim64(renderHeight / 2)) — below the \
  2092	          stage-1 floor, which renders SOFT (the refine sharpens, it cannot invent detail). \
  2093	          Treating the request as stage-1 dims instead; output will be \
  2094	          \(renderWidth * 2)x\(renderHeight * 2). Send ~2x larger dims for the intended size.
  2095	          """)
  2096	      }
  2097	    }
  2098	    if let requestedSteps = req.steps, requestedSteps != 8 {
  2099	      logger.warning(
  2100	        "LTX-2: steps=\(requestedSteps) requested, but the distilled pipeline uses a fixed 8-step sigma schedule — the value is currently ignored (#219)")
  2101	    }
  2102	
  2103	    var effectivePrompt = req.prompt
  2104	
  2105	    // Character identity + optional prompt enhancement. For T2V (no init image)
  2106	    // there is no other identity source, so default to "kira" when the caller
  2107	    // names no character. For I2V the init image already carries identity.
  2108	    let isT2V = (effectiveInitImage == nil)
  2109	    let characterName = req.character ?? (isT2V ? "kira" : nil)
  2110	    let charMode = req.contentMode.flatMap { ContentModeManager.Mode(rawValue: $0) } ?? .neutral
  2111	    var characterDesc: String? = nil
  2112	    if let name = characterName,
  2113	       let entry = await characterStore.get(CharacterEntry.slug(name)) {
  2114	      characterDesc = entry.resolvedDescription(for: charMode)
  2115	    }
  2116	
  2117	    // Auto-enhance the video prompt through the configured prompt-optimization
  2118	    // provider (Dan's-PE via LM Studio). The optimizer weaves in the character
  2119	    // description AND enriches the scene, so it replaces the manual character
  2120	    // prepend. Opt out per request with enhance:false; falls back to the manual
  2121	    // prepend when no provider is configured or enhancement fails.
  2122	    var enhancedApplied = false
  2123	    let aiProviderConfig = ComfyBoxServerConfig.loadOrMigrate()
  2124	    if req.enhance != false, let endpoint = aiProviderConfig.providers.promptOptimization {
  2125	      var base = endpoint.baseUrl
  2126	      while base.hasSuffix("/") { base.removeLast() }
  2127	      if base.hasSuffix("/v1") { base = String(base.dropLast(3)) }
  2128	      while base.hasSuffix("/") { base.removeLast() }
  2129	      let optimizer = PromptOptimizer(
  2130	        configuration: PromptOptimizer.Configuration(
  2131	          ollamaBaseURL: base, lmStudioBaseURL: nil, model: endpoint.model,
  2132	          timeoutSeconds: 90, enabled: true),
  2133	        logger: logger)
  2134	      // i2v: motion-only enhancement (the init image fixes subject/scene); t2v: full scene.
  2135	      let result = await optimizer.optimize(
  2136	        prompt: req.prompt, character: characterName,
  2137	        characterDescription: characterDesc, contentMode: charMode.rawValue,
  2138	        mediaKind: isT2V ? "video" : "video-i2v")
  2139	      if result.enhanced {
  2140	        effectivePrompt = result.prompt
  2141	        enhancedApplied = true
  2142	        logger.info("Video: enhanced prompt via \(endpoint.model)\(characterName.map { " (character \($0))" } ?? "").")
  2143	      }
  2144	    }
  2145	
  2146	    // Fallback: manual character prepend when enhancement didn't run/apply.
  2147	    if !enhancedApplied, let name = characterName, let desc = characterDesc, !desc.isEmpty {
  2148	      // Idempotency: skip if the caller already wrote the description in.
  2149	      let alreadyPresent = desc.split(separator: " ").prefix(4).allSatisfy {
  2150	        effectivePrompt.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains($0)
  2151	      }
  2152	      if !alreadyPresent {
  2153	        effectivePrompt = desc + " " + effectivePrompt
  2154	        logger.info("Video: prepended character '\(name)' (mode \(charMode.rawValue)) to prompt.")
  2155	      }
  2156	    }
  2157	
  2158	    if let preset = videoPreset {
  2159	      if let prefix = preset.promptPrefix, !prefix.isEmpty { effectivePrompt = prefix + ", " + effectivePrompt }
  2160	      if let suffix = preset.promptSuffix, !suffix.isEmpty { effectivePrompt = effectivePrompt + ", " + suffix }
  2161	    }
  2162	
  2163	    // Single-pass fold (2026-08-02): continuation chunks DEGENERATE — chunk 2
  2164	    // collapses into fragments (long-known for i2v, which is why the daemon
  2165	    // sends explicit single-pass frames there; observed for T2V today the
  2166	    // moment two-stage went live: chunk 1 clean, chunk 2 psychedelic). The
  2167	    // daemon's rule only covers i2v, so fold ANY duration that fits the
  2168	    // trained window (289f = 12s) into ONE chunk here, t2v included.
  2169	    var foldedFramesPerChunk = req.frames ?? 97
  2170	    var foldedExtendSeconds = req.extendToSeconds
  2171	      ?? Self.extendSecondsFromDuration(req.duration, framesPerChunk: foldedFramesPerChunk, fps: req.fps ?? 24)
  2172	    if foldedExtendSeconds > 0 {
  2173	      let fps = req.fps ?? 24
  2174	      let targetFrames = Int((foldedExtendSeconds * Float(fps)).rounded())
  2175	      if targetFrames <= 289 {
  2176	        let singleFrames = min(289, ((max(targetFrames, 9) - 2) / 8) * 8 + 9)  // 1+8k covering target
  2177	        foldedFramesPerChunk = max(foldedFramesPerChunk, singleFrames)
  2178	        logger.info(
  2179	          "LTX-2: folded \(foldedExtendSeconds)s request into a single \(foldedFramesPerChunk)f chunk (continuation chunks degenerate; ≤289f renders single-pass)")
  2180	        foldedExtendSeconds = 0  // 0 = no continuation chunks
  2181	      }
  2182	    }
  2183	
  2184	    let videoRequest = LTX2VideoRequest(
  2185	      prompt: effectivePrompt,
  2186	      negativePrompt: req.negativePrompt ?? videoPreset?.negativePrompt,
  2187	      initImagePath: effectiveInitImage,
  2188	      width: renderWidth,
  2189	      height: renderHeight,
  2190	      framesPerChunk: foldedFramesPerChunk,
  2191	      steps: req.steps ?? videoPreset?.steps ?? 8,
  2192	      seed: req.seed ?? videoPreset?.seed.map(UInt64.init) ?? 42,
  2193	      strength: req.strength ?? 1.0,
  2194	      imgCompression: req.imgCompression,
  2195	      guidance: req.guidance,
  2196	      // Re-enabled by default for EXTENDED renders (#231, 2026-07-16): the
  2197	      // 2026-07-13 MLX mutex crash on this path was memory pressure — with
  2198	      // the int8 stack (#230) a 12s/3-chunk anchored render completed clean
  2199	      // (289f, no crash). Single-chunk renders don't anchor (nothing to
  2200	      // drift); callers can still pass 0 to disable.
  2201	      // Mid-pass identity re-anchor is OPT-IN and default OFF — it was superseded
  2202	      // by the face-region anchor (LTX2_FACE_ANCHOR_STRENGTH), which holds partner
  2203	      // faces without the multi-keyframe gap-collapse. Enable explicitly via
  2204	      // LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL>0 (+ _STRENGTH); a standard 97f/4s render NEVER takes
  2205	      // it unless the interval is set below the frame count. Only the pre-existing
  2206	      // extended/chunked anchor stays on by default (unchanged behavior).
  2207	      identityAnchorStrength: req.identityAnchorStrength
  2208	        ?? (Self.isExtendedRender(
  2209	              extendToSeconds: req.extendToSeconds, duration: req.duration,
  2210	              framesPerChunk: req.frames ?? 97, fps: req.fps ?? 24)
  2211	            ? 0.5
  2212	            : ((effectiveInitImage != nil
  2213	                && (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0) > 0
  2214	                && (req.frames ?? 97) > (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0))
  2215	               ? (Float(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_STRENGTH"] ?? "") ?? 0.4) : 0)),
  2216	      identityReAnchorInterval: (Int(ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REANCHOR_INTERVAL"] ?? "") ?? 0),
  2217	      extendToSeconds: foldedExtendSeconds,
  2218	      fps: req.fps ?? 24,
  2219	      loraPath: req.loraPath,
  2220	      loraStrength: req.loraStrength ?? 1.0,
  2221	      loras: resolvedLoRAs,
  2222	      outputPath: resolvedOutput
  2223	,
  2224	      tuning: req.tuning,
  2225	      presetTuning: videoPreset?.videoTuning
  2226	    )
  2227	    // Validate before enqueuing so bad frames/dims fail fast.
  2228	    try generator.validate(videoRequest)
  2229	
  2230	    return PreparedLocalVideo(
  2231	      generator: generator,
  2232	      request: videoRequest,
  2233	      mode: (effectiveInitImage?.isEmpty == false) ? .i2v : .t2v,
  2234	      source: req.source ?? "api",
  2235	      optimizationAttemptId: req.optimizationAttemptId)
  2236	  }
  2237

858:  private func denoisingLoop(
870:    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)?
874:    // Per-step CFG schedule (community "CFG ramp": e.g. LTX2_CFG_SCHEDULE=3,2,1 —
875:    // CFG only on the first steps, where broad motion forms, then back to 1;
876:    // 10Eros per-step guider pattern). Missing entries extend the last value.
878:    func cfgAt(_ step: Int) -> Float {
880:      return sch[min(step, sch.count - 1)]
885:    // Set LTX2_TRAJ_DUMP=<path-prefix> to append one JSON line per step to
911:    // PinkCherry recipe: base=euler_ancestral_cfg_pp, refine=euler_cfg_pp), so
915:    // every step regardless of cfgScale.
925:      // clean latent every step, consistent with their per-token timestep of
926:      // ~0. Without this the step update re-mixes noise into conditioned
928:      // misreading the reference each step (appearance divergence, i2v
935:      // Snapshot pre-step latents for delta_norm (post input-clamp so the
936:      // delta measures the denoise update; no compute or eval unless
953:      // Compute per-token timesteps
954:      let timesteps: MLXArray
956:        // I2V: per-token timesteps based on denoise mask
962:        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).asType(dtype) * maskFlat.asType(dtype)
964:        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).reshaped(1, 1).asType(dtype)
970:      // Pass PER-TOKEN timesteps so the model sees conditioned frames (mask=0)
971:      // at timestep 0 (clean) and generated frames at `sigma`. Without this the
975:      // passes `video_timesteps = denoise_mask * sigma` when the mask is
976:      // non-uniform. For T2V, `timesteps` is a scalar (1,1) → broadcast, so this
980:      // the unconditional pass too would guide the baseline that CFG++ steps
984:        timestep: timesteps,
993:      // Compute x0 (denoised) from velocity using per-token timesteps
997:      let timestepsF32 = timesteps.asType(.float32).expandedDimensions(axis: -1)
998:      var x0GuidedF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
1002:      // step, even at cfg=1 — the CFG++ update steps along the UNCONDITIONAL
1004:      // disable_cfg1_optimization=True for exactly this reason).
1009:          timestep: timesteps,
1015:        eval(velocityNeg)
1017:        let x0NegF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityNeg.asType(.float32)
1049:          timestep: timesteps,
1056:        eval(velocitySTG)
1057:        let x0STGF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocitySTG.asType(.float32)
1075:      // toward the source face, softly + every step. No full-frame splicing, so
1083:      // Euler step (ancestral/SDE when LTX2_SAMPLER=euler_ancestral) in float32
1086:          // CFG++ step (ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp, flow/CONST model:
1094:          // Guard the σ=1.0 first step: alphaS→0 makes sf=σ/alphaS blow up to inf
1102:            // get_ancestral_step(σ/alpha_s, σ_next/alpha_t, eta=1), then
1134:      // CFG++ steps along the uncond direction, which drifts frames the denoise
1135:      // mask pins at timestep 0 (I2V conditioning / refine re-inject); re-snap
1136:      // them to the clean latent after every step (ComfyUI does the equivalent
1146:      eval(currentLatents)
1148:      // --- Trajectory dump (after step update) ---
1150:        // Scalar stats via small MLX reductions; only scalars are evaluated.
1163:          // Plain Euler step: no ancestral noise injection (sigmaUp == 0).
1194:        // Serialize one JSON line and append (create file on first step).
1195:        var jsonParts = ["\"step\":\(i)"]
1204:          // so a two-stage render's refine pass doesn't clobber the stage-1 dump.
1220:          try? handle.synchronize()
1224:      progressCallback?(i + 1, numSteps)
1238:    steps: Int, latF: Int, latH: Int, latW: Int
1246:        steps: steps,
1258:  /// Adaptive VAE decode. Plain single-pass decode is clean (no spatial-tile
1259:  /// mosaic, no temporal-window jitter) but memory-heavy; tiled decode is
1261:  /// normal clips, exact streamed decode above the safety gate. Mode switch:
1264:  private func decodeAdaptive(_ latents: MLXArray) -> MLXArray {
1269:    // latF 13 (24x40 spatial) is 3x the volume of 448x704 and plain decode at
1270:    // that size drove 40GB+ of swap with critical memory pressure mid-decode,
1274:    // Default budget derived from the Metal max-buffer ceiling, measured
1277:    // no error, just garbage — while tiled decode of the same tensor is clean.
1280:    // LTX2_DUMP_LATENT=<path>: save the pre-decode latent for offline decode
1284:      logger.info("VAE decode: dumped pre-decode latent to \(dumpPath)")
1286:    // LTX2_DECODE_F32=1: decode in float32 instead of bf16 (debug probe).
1287:    let decodeF32 = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_DECODE_F32"] == "1"
1288:    let bf = latents.asType(decodeF32 ? .float32 : .bfloat16)
1294:    //   plain: force single-pass — EXPERT/DEBUG ONLY: above the gate, MLX Metal
1299:    // constructed config could force a corrupting plain decode).
1305:        logger.warning("VAE decode: FORCED plain above the safety gate (volume \(volume) > \(plainMaxVolume)) — output may be silently corrupt (mlx #3836).")
1307:      logger.info("VAE decode: plain single-pass (forced; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]).")
1308:      return vae.decode(bf)
1311:      logger.info("VAE decode: tiled (mode=tile; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — tile \(tile).")
1312:      return vae.decodeTiled(bf, tileSize: tile, tileStride: tile - 2)
1314:      logger.info("VAE decode: streamed exact chunked-io (mode=stream; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — zero seams.")
1315:      return vae.decodeStreamed(bf)
1318:        logger.info("VAE decode: streamed exact chunked-io (volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)] > \(plainMaxVolume)) — zero seams.")
1319:        return vae.decodeStreamed(bf)
1321:      logger.info("VAE decode: plain single-pass (volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — under the safety gate.")
1322:      return vae.decode(bf)
1326:  /// Shared two-stage refine (Phase 3): latent upsample x2 -> re-noise ->
1327:  /// short refine denoise at high res, with the refine-volume OOM gate and
1330:  /// multi-chunk render gets identical refine treatment — previously chunks
1331:  /// 2+ skipped refine entirely, dropping to base resolution mid-video.
1332:  /// Returns the refined (or gated/upsampled) latents; caller decodes.
1340:    refineAnchorImage: MLXArray?,
1341:    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)?
1344:          resolvedConfig.twoStage else {
1347:    logger.info("Two-stage refine: upsampling latents 2x...")
1348:    // The latent upsampler is trained on UN-normalized (VAE-scale) latents.
1349:    let stats = vae.decoder.perChannelStatistics
1353:    eval(upLatent)
1355:    // LTX2_REFINE_ROWSTATS=1 logs per-latent-row energy at each refine boundary
1357:    // vs denoise vs decode) from a single short render.
1359:    // .npy for offline decode/parity tests.
1366:      eval(energy)
1375:    // Refine-volume OOM gate (2026-07-25 23:24 crash): above it, decode the
1376:    // upsampled latent directly — upscaled-but-unrefined beats a dead server.
1377:    let refineVolume = upLatent.dim(2) * (latH * 2) * (latW * 2)
1378:    let refineMaxVolume = resolvedConfig.refineMaxVol
1380:      logger.info("Two-stage refine: DECODE_ONLY (skipped denoise) — decoding upsampled latent directly.")
1383:    if refineVolume > refineMaxVolume {
1384:      logger.info("Two-stage refine: SKIPPED denoise (refine latent volume \(refineVolume) > \(refineMaxVolume)) — decoding upsampled latent directly (OOM guard).")
1389:    let refineSigmas: [Float] = resolvedConfig.refineSigmasEffective
1393:    let sigma0 = refineSigmas[0]
1395:    // Frame-0 re-anchor: raw source re-encoded at refine res when available
1399:    if var refImg = refineAnchorImage {
1401:      anchorFrame = vae.encode(refImg).asType(.float32)
1402:      eval(anchorFrame)
1403:      logger.info("Two-stage refine: frame 0 re-anchored to source re-encoded at \(rLatW * spatialCompression)x\(rLatH * spatialCompression).")
1406:    let refineInit = MLX.concatenated(
1414:      latent: refineInit, cleanLatent: refClean, denoiseMask: refMask)
1415:    let refinePos = createPositionGrid(batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: rLatH, latW: rLatW)
1416:    let refinePE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
1417:      indicesGrid: refinePos, dim: innerDim,
1424:    eval(refinePE.cos, refinePE.sin)
1425:    logger.info("Two-stage refine: denoising at \(rLatW * spatialCompression)x\(rLatH * spatialCompression), \(refineSigmas.count - 1) steps...")
1426:    // base=euler_ancestral_cfg_pp -> refine=euler_cfg_pp (the author's pairing).
1427:    var refined = denoisingLoop(
1428:      latents: refineInit, positions: refinePos, precomputedPE: refinePE,
1430:      sigmas: refineSigmas, cfgScale: cfgScale, state: refState,
1432:      progressCallback: progressCallback)
1433:    eval(refined)
1434:    rowStats("refined", refined)
1436:    logger.info("Two-stage refine complete.")
1437:    return refined
1446:    spatialScaleMul: Float = 1.0   // refine: scale spatial positions to keep RoPE in-distribution at 2x
1525:    eval(bf16)
