   854	
   855	  // MARK: - Internal: Denoising Loop
   856	
   857	  /// Core denoising loop shared by T2V and I2V.
   858	  private func denoisingLoop(
   859	    latents: MLXArray,
   860	    positions: MLXArray,
   861	    precomputedPE: (cos: MLXArray, sin: MLXArray),
   862	    textEmbeddings: MLXArray,
   863	    negativeEmbeddings: MLXArray?,
   864	    sigmas: [Float],
   865	    cfgScale: Float,
   866	    state: LTX2LatentState?,
   867	    nagEmbeddings: MLXArray? = nil,
   868	    nag: LTX2NAGConfig = .disabled,
   869	    forceDeterministic: Bool = false,
   870	    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)?
   871	  ) -> MLXArray {
   872	    let dtype: DType = .bfloat16
   873	    let numSteps = sigmas.count - 1
   874	    // Per-step CFG schedule (community "CFG ramp": e.g. LTX2_CFG_SCHEDULE=3,2,1 —
   875	    // CFG only on the first steps, where broad motion forms, then back to 1;
   876	    // 10Eros per-step guider pattern). Missing entries extend the last value.
   877	    let cfgSchedule: [Float]? = resolvedConfig.cfgSchedule.isEmpty ? nil : resolvedConfig.cfgSchedule
   878	    func cfgAt(_ step: Int) -> Float {
   879	      guard let sch = cfgSchedule else { return cfgScale }
   880	      return sch[min(step, sch.count - 1)]
   881	    }
   882	    let useCFG = (cfgScale > 1.0 || (cfgSchedule?.contains { $0 > 1.0 } ?? false)) && negativeEmbeddings != nil
   883	
   884	    // --- Denoise-trajectory instrumentation (env-gated, off by default) ---
   885	    // Set LTX2_TRAJ_DUMP=<path-prefix> to append one JSON line per step to
   886	    // <path-prefix>.jsonl with latent/x0/velocity statistics. Used to diff
   887	    // sampler behavior numerically against reference implementations
   888	    // (e.g. ComfyUI). Zero overhead when the env var is unset.
   889	    let trajPrefix = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_TRAJ_DUMP"]
   890	    var trajHandle: FileHandle? = nil
   891	    // Mean over all elements.
   892	    func trajMean(_ x: MLXArray) -> MLXArray { x.mean() }
   893	    // Std over all elements (population).
   894	    func trajStd(_ x: MLXArray) -> MLXArray {
   895	      let m = x.mean()
   896	      return MLX.sqrt(((x - m) * (x - m)).mean())
   897	    }
   898	    // Weighted mean/std over token subset. x: (B, T, C) f32, w: (B, T) f32 0/1.
   899	    func trajMaskedStats(_ x: MLXArray, _ w: MLXArray) -> (mean: MLXArray, std: MLXArray) {
   900	      let w3 = w.expandedDimensions(axis: -1)
   901	      let channels = Float(x.dim(-1))
   902	      let count = w.sum() * channels + 1e-6
   903	      let m = (x * w3).sum() / count
   904	      let v = (((x - m) * (x - m)) * w3).sum() / count
   905	      return (m, MLX.sqrt(v))
   906	    }
   907	
   908	    // Keep latents in float32 throughout for precision
   909	    var currentLatents = latents.asType(.float32)
   910	    // Refine pass uses the deterministic member of the sampler family (validated
   911	    // PinkCherry recipe: base=euler_ancestral_cfg_pp, refine=euler_cfg_pp), so
   912	    // forceDeterministic drops the ancestral/SDE noise but keeps CFG++ mode.
   913	    let useSDE = resolvedConfig.samplerIsAncestral && !forceDeterministic
   914	    // CFG++ (euler[_ancestral]_cfg_pp): needs an unconditional (negative) pass
   915	    // every step regardless of cfgScale.
   916	    let useCfgPP = resolvedConfig.samplerIsCfgPP && negativeEmbeddings != nil
   917	
   918	    for i in 0..<numSteps {
   919	      let sigma = sigmas[i]
   920	      let sigmaNext = sigmas[i + 1]
   921	
   922	      // INPUT-side conditioning clamp (matches ComfyUI KSamplerX0Inpaint +
   923	      // LTXV.scale_latent_inpaint which returns the CLEAN latent, never
   924	      // re-noised): conditioned tokens (denoiseMask < 1) enter the model as
   925	      // clean latent every step, consistent with their per-token timestep of
   926	      // ~0. Without this the step update re-mixes noise into conditioned
   927	      // tokens, so the model reads a NOISY frame while told it's clean —
   928	      // misreading the reference each step (appearance divergence, i2v
   929	      // transition pop). Output-side x0 clamping alone is insufficient.
   930	      if let s = state {
   931	        let m = s.denoiseMask.asType(.float32)
   932	        currentLatents = currentLatents * m + s.cleanLatent.asType(currentLatents.dtype) * (MLXArray(Float(1)) - m)
   933	      }
   934	
   935	      // Snapshot pre-step latents for delta_norm (post input-clamp so the
   936	      // delta measures the denoise update; no compute or eval unless
   937	      // trajectory dumping is enabled).
   938	      let trajLatentsBefore: MLXArray? = trajPrefix != nil ? currentLatents : nil
   939	
   940	      let b = currentLatents.dim(0)
   941	      let c = currentLatents.dim(1)
   942	      let f = currentLatents.dim(2)
   943	      let h = currentLatents.dim(3)
   944	      let w = currentLatents.dim(4)
   945	      let numTokens = f * h * w
   946	
   947	      // Flatten latents to token space: (B, C, F, H, W) -> (B, T, C)
   948	      let latentsFlat = currentLatents
   949	        .reshaped(b, c, -1)
   950	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
   951	        .asType(dtype)
   952	
   953	      // Compute per-token timesteps
   954	      let timesteps: MLXArray
   955	      if let s = state {
   956	        // I2V: per-token timesteps based on denoise mask
   957	        let maskBroadcast = MLX.broadcast(
   958	          s.denoiseMask.reshaped(b, 1, f, 1, 1),
   959	          to: [b, 1, f, h, w]
   960	        )
   961	        let maskFlat = maskBroadcast.reshaped(b, numTokens)
   962	        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).asType(dtype) * maskFlat.asType(dtype)
   963	      } else {
   964	        timesteps = MLXArray(sigma).reshaped(1, 1).asType(dtype)
   965	      }
   966	
   967	      let sigmaArray = MLXArray([sigma]).asType(dtype)
   968	
   969	      // Positive pass through transformer.
   970	      // Pass PER-TOKEN timesteps so the model sees conditioned frames (mask=0)
   971	      // at timestep 0 (clean) and generated frames at `sigma`. Without this the
   972	      // transformer treats every token as equally noisy and cannot tell which
   973	      // frame is the I2V conditioning frame — it discounts the image and
   974	      // produces T2V-like output. Matches reference guided_denoise_loop which
   975	      // passes `video_timesteps = denoise_mask * sigma` when the mask is
   976	      // non-uniform. For T2V, `timesteps` is a scalar (1,1) → broadcast, so this
   977	      // is a no-op there.
   978	      // NAG rides ONLY the positive pass: it steers the conditional prediction
   979	      // away from the negative concept inside cross-attention. Applying it to
   980	      // the unconditional pass too would guide the baseline that CFG++ steps
   981	      // along, double-counting the guidance.
   982	      let velocityPos = transformer(
   983	        latent: latentsFlat,
   984	        timestep: timesteps,
   985	        context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
   986	        positions: positions,
   987	        sigma: sigmaArray,
   988	        precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
   989	        nagContext: nagEmbeddings?.asType(dtype),
   990	        nag: nag
   991	      )
   992	
   993	      // Compute x0 (denoised) from velocity using per-token timesteps
   994	      let latentsFlatF32 = currentLatents
   995	        .reshaped(b, c, -1)
   996	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
   997	      let timestepsF32 = timesteps.asType(.float32).expandedDimensions(axis: -1)
   998	      var x0GuidedF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityPos.asType(.float32)
   999	      let x0CondF32 = x0GuidedF32  // pure conditional x0, saved for the STG delta
  1000	
  1001	      // Negative pass: needed for classic CFG (scale>1) and for CFG++ (every
  1002	      // step, even at cfg=1 — the CFG++ update steps along the UNCONDITIONAL
  1003	      // noise direction; see ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp which sets
  1004	      // disable_cfg1_optimization=True for exactly this reason).
  1005	      var x0UncondF32: MLXArray? = nil
  1006	      if useCFG || useCfgPP, let negEmb = negativeEmbeddings {
  1007	        let velocityNeg = transformer(
  1008	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1009	          timestep: timesteps,
  1010	          context: negEmb.asType(dtype),
  1011	          positions: positions,
  1012	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1013	          precomputedPE: precomputedPE
  1014	        )
  1015	        eval(velocityNeg)
  1016	
  1017	        let x0NegF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocityNeg.asType(.float32)
  1018	        x0UncondF32 = x0NegF32
  1019	        if useCFG && cfgAt(i) > 1.0 {
  1020	          x0GuidedF32 = LTX2Guidance.applyCFG(
  1021	            conditioned: x0GuidedF32,
  1022	            unconditioned: x0NegF32,
  1023	            scale: cfgAt(i)
  1024	          )
  1025	          // Guidance rescale (Lin et al.; counters CFG over-saturation /
  1026	          // shadow-crush). High CFG inflates the prediction's std -> boosted
  1027	          // color/contrast (measured: cfg2 saturation +19% vs seed). Rescale
  1028	          // the guided x0 back toward the CONDITIONAL prediction's std, then
  1029	          // blend by phi — keeps the action-motion boost, restores seed color.
  1030	          // resolvedConfig.guidanceRescale (0 = off) — request/preset capable.
  1031	          let phi = resolvedConfig.guidanceRescale
  1032	          if phi > 0 {
  1033	            let stdCond = MLX.sqrt(((x0CondF32 - x0CondF32.mean()) * (x0CondF32 - x0CondF32.mean())).mean())
  1034	            let stdGuided = MLX.sqrt(((x0GuidedF32 - x0GuidedF32.mean()) * (x0GuidedF32 - x0GuidedF32.mean())).mean())
  1035	            let rescaled = x0GuidedF32 * (stdCond / (stdGuided + 1e-6))
  1036	            x0GuidedF32 = MLXArray(phi) * rescaled + MLXArray(1 - phi) * x0GuidedF32
  1037	          }
  1038	        }
  1039	      }
  1040	
  1041	      // STG: spatiotemporal guidance. A perturbed pass skips self-attention in a
  1042	      // block subset; steer away from it to restore high-frequency motion detail.
  1043	      // For distilled cfg=1 this is the primary guidance lever (anti-haze).
  1044	      let stgBase = resolvedConfig.stgScale
  1045	      if stgBase > 0 {
  1046	        let stgScale = LTX2PipelineConfig.stgScaleForStep(i, base: stgBase)
  1047	        let velocitySTG = transformer(
  1048	          latent: latentsFlat,
  1049	          timestep: timesteps,
  1050	          context: textEmbeddings.asType(dtype),
  1051	          positions: positions,
  1052	          sigma: sigmaArray,
  1053	          precomputedPE: precomputedPE,
  1054	          stgBlocks: resolvedConfig.stgBlockSet
  1055	        )
  1056	        eval(velocitySTG)
  1057	        let x0STGF32 = latentsFlatF32 - timestepsF32 * velocitySTG.asType(.float32)
  1058	        x0GuidedF32 = x0GuidedF32 + MLXArray(stgScale) * (x0CondF32 - x0STGF32)
  1059	      }
  1060	
  1061	      // Reshape x0 from token space to spatial
  1062	      var denoised = x0GuidedF32
  1063	        .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1064	        .reshaped(b, c, f, h, w)
  1065	
  1066	      // Apply I2V denoise mask
  1067	      if let s = state {
  1068	        denoised = LTX2Conditioning.applyDenoiseMask(
  1069	          denoised: denoised,
  1070	          clean: s.cleanLatent.asType(.float32),
  1071	          denoiseMask: s.denoiseMask
  1072	        )
  1073	      }
  1074	      // Face-region identity hold: pull ONLY the masked face latents (all frames)
  1075	      // toward the source face, softly + every step. No full-frame splicing, so
  1076	      // nothing to collapse between anchors — holds a stationary partner's face.
  1077	      if let s = state, let fm = s.faceMask, let ref = s.faceRef, s.faceAnchorStrength > 0 {
  1078	        let m = fm.asType(.float32)
  1079	        let wm = m * s.faceAnchorStrength
  1080	        denoised = denoised * (MLXArray(Float(1)) - wm) + ref.asType(.float32) * wm
  1081	      }
  1082	
  1083	      // Euler step (ancestral/SDE when LTX2_SAMPLER=euler_ancestral) in float32
  1084	      if sigmaNext > 0 {
  1085	        if useCfgPP, let x0Neg = x0UncondF32 {
  1086	          // CFG++ step (ComfyUI sample_euler_ancestral_cfg_pp, flow/CONST model:
  1087	          // exp(lambda(σ)) = (1-σ)/σ so alpha_s = 1-σ, alpha_t = 1-σ_next).
  1088	          // Direction from the UNCONDITIONAL x0, target the conditional x0:
  1089	          //   d = (x - alpha_s·x0_uncond) / σ
  1090	          //   x = alpha_t·x0_cond + σ_down·d  (+ alpha_t·σ_up·noise, ancestral)
  1091	          let x0UncondSpatial = x0Neg
  1092	            .transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1093	            .reshaped(b, c, f, h, w)
  1094	          // Guard the σ=1.0 first step: alphaS→0 makes sf=σ/alphaS blow up to inf
  1095	          // and NaN the ancestral term. Clamp alphaS to a small floor.
  1096	          let alphaS = max(1.0 - sigma, 1e-4)
  1097	          let alphaT = 1.0 - sigmaNext
  1098	          let d = (currentLatents - MLXArray(alphaS) * x0UncondSpatial) / MLXArray(sigma)
  1099	          var sigmaDown = sigmaNext
  1100	          var sigmaUp: Float = 0
  1101	          if useSDE {
  1102	            // get_ancestral_step(σ/alpha_s, σ_next/alpha_t, eta=1), then
  1103	            // σ_down = alpha_t · σ_down'
  1104	            let sf = sigma / alphaS
  1105	            let st = sigmaNext / alphaT
  1106	            let inner = st * st * (sf * sf - st * st) / (sf * sf)
  1107	            let up = min(st, (inner > 0 ? inner : 0).squareRoot())
  1108	            sigmaDown = alphaT * (max(st * st - up * up, 0)).squareRoot()
  1109	            sigmaUp = up
  1110	          }
  1111	          currentLatents = MLXArray(alphaT) * denoised + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * d
  1112	          if useSDE && sigmaUp > 0 {
  1113	            // Plain Gaussian ancestral noise, matching ComfyUI's noise_sampler
  1114	            // (randn_like). getNewNoise's per-channel normalization flattens the
  1115	            // stochasticity that drives inter-frame motion — do NOT use it here.
  1116	            let noise = MLXRandom.normal(currentLatents.shape, dtype: .float32)
  1117	            currentLatents = currentLatents + MLXArray(alphaT) * noise * MLXArray(sigmaUp)
  1118	          }
  1119	        } else if useSDE {
  1120	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1121	          let eps = (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1122	          let (alphaRatio, sigmaDown, sigmaUp) = getSdeCoeff(sigmaNext: sigmaNext)
  1123	          let noise = MLXRandom.normal(currentLatents.shape, dtype: .float32)
  1124	          currentLatents = MLXArray(alphaRatio) * (denoised + MLXArray(sigmaDown) * eps)
  1125	            + MLXArray(sigmaUp) * noise
  1126	        } else {
  1127	          let sigmaF32 = MLXArray(sigma)
  1128	          let sigmaNextF32 = MLXArray(sigmaNext)
  1129	          currentLatents = denoised + sigmaNextF32 * (currentLatents - denoised) / sigmaF32
  1130	        }
  1131	      } else {
  1132	        currentLatents = denoised
  1133	      }
  1134	      // CFG++ steps along the uncond direction, which drifts frames the denoise
  1135	      // mask pins at timestep 0 (I2V conditioning / refine re-inject); re-snap
  1136	      // them to the clean latent after every step (ComfyUI does the equivalent
  1137	      // in its outer inpaint wrapper).
  1138	      if useCfgPP, let s = state {
  1139	        currentLatents = LTX2Conditioning.applyDenoiseMask(
  1140	          denoised: currentLatents,
  1141	          clean: s.cleanLatent.asType(.float32),
  1142	          denoiseMask: s.denoiseMask
  1143	        )
  1144	      }
  1145	
  1146	      eval(currentLatents)
  1147	
  1148	      // --- Trajectory dump (after step update) ---
  1149	      if let prefix = trajPrefix, let before = trajLatentsBefore {
  1150	        // Scalar stats via small MLX reductions; only scalars are evaluated.
  1151	        let velocityF32 = velocityPos.asType(.float32)
  1152	        let deltaAbs = MLX.abs(currentLatents - before)
  1153	
  1154	        var fields: [(String, Float)] = [
  1155	          ("sigma", sigma),
  1156	          ("sigmaNext", sigmaNext),
  1157	          ("latent_mean", trajMean(currentLatents).item(Float.self)),
  1158	          ("latent_std", trajStd(currentLatents).item(Float.self)),
  1159	          ("x0_mean", trajMean(denoised).item(Float.self)),
  1160	          ("x0_std", trajStd(denoised).item(Float.self)),
  1161	          ("velocity_mean_abs", trajMean(MLX.abs(velocityF32)).item(Float.self)),
  1162	          ("delta_norm", trajMean(deltaAbs).item(Float.self)),
  1163	          // Plain Euler step: no ancestral noise injection (sigmaUp == 0).
  1164	          ("noise_injected", 0),
  1165	        ]
  1166	
  1167	        // Conditioned vs generated token subsets (I2V only).
  1168	        if let s = state {
  1169	          let maskFlat = MLX.broadcast(
  1170	            s.denoiseMask.reshaped(b, 1, f, 1, 1),
  1171	            to: [b, 1, f, h, w]
  1172	          ).reshaped(b, numTokens).asType(.float32)
  1173	          let genW = (maskFlat .> 0.5).asType(.float32)
  1174	          let condW = 1.0 - genW
  1175	
  1176	          // Token-space (B, T, C) views for subset reductions.
  1177	          let curFlat = currentLatents.reshaped(b, c, -1).transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1178	          let x0Flat = denoised.reshaped(b, c, -1).transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1179	          let deltaFlat = deltaAbs.reshaped(b, c, -1).transposed(0, 2, 1)
  1180	
  1181	          for (suffix, weight) in [("cond", condW), ("gen", genW)] {
  1182	            let latentStats = trajMaskedStats(curFlat, weight)
  1183	            let x0Stats = trajMaskedStats(x0Flat, weight)
  1184	            let deltaMean = (deltaFlat * weight.expandedDimensions(axis: -1)).sum()
  1185	              / (weight.sum() * Float(c) + 1e-6)
  1186	            fields.append(("latent_mean_\(suffix)", latentStats.mean.item(Float.self)))
  1187	            fields.append(("latent_std_\(suffix)", latentStats.std.item(Float.self)))
  1188	            fields.append(("x0_mean_\(suffix)", x0Stats.mean.item(Float.self)))
  1189	            fields.append(("x0_std_\(suffix)", x0Stats.std.item(Float.self)))
  1190	            fields.append(("delta_norm_\(suffix)", deltaMean.item(Float.self)))
  1191	          }
  1192	        }
  1193	
  1194	        // Serialize one JSON line and append (create file on first step).
  1195	        var jsonParts = ["\"step\":\(i)"]
  1196	        for (key, value) in fields {
  1197	          let v = value.isFinite ? String(format: "%.8e", value) : "null"
  1198	          jsonParts.append("\"\(key)\":\(v)")
  1199	        }
  1200	        let line = "{" + jsonParts.joined(separator: ",") + "}\n"
  1201	
  1202	        if trajHandle == nil {
  1203	          // Each denoise pass gets its own file (prefix.jsonl, prefix.2.jsonl, …)
  1204	          // so a two-stage render's refine pass doesn't clobber the stage-1 dump.
  1205	          var path = prefix + ".jsonl"
  1206	          var pass = 1
  1207	          while FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: path) {
  1208	            pass += 1
  1209	            path = prefix + ".\(pass).jsonl"
  1210	          }
  1211	          FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: path, contents: nil)
  1212	          trajHandle = FileHandle(forWritingAtPath: path)
  1213	          if trajHandle == nil {
  1214	            logger.warning("LTX2_TRAJ_DUMP: cannot open \(path) for writing")
  1215	          }
  1216	        }
  1217	        if let handle = trajHandle, let data = line.data(using: .utf8) {
  1218	          handle.seekToEndOfFile()
  1219	          handle.write(data)
  1220	          try? handle.synchronize()
  1221	        }
  1222	      }
  1223	
  1224	      progressCallback?(i + 1, numSteps)
  1225	    }
  1226	
  1227	    if let handle = trajHandle {
  1228	      try? handle.close()
  1229	    }
  1230	
  1231	    return currentLatents
  1232	  }
  1233	
  1234	  // MARK: - Internal: Sigma Schedule
  1235	
  1250	  }
  1251	
  1252	  // MARK: - Internal: Position Grid
  1253	
  1254	  /// Create a 3D position grid for RoPE in pixel space.
  1255	  ///
  1256	  /// Matches the Python `create_position_grid` function with causal fix
  1257	  /// and bfloat16 precision quantization.
  1258	  /// Adaptive VAE decode. Plain single-pass decode is clean (no spatial-tile
  1259	  /// mosaic, no temporal-window jitter) but memory-heavy; tiled decode is
  1260	  /// OOM-safe for long/large clips. Decide by latent frame count: plain for
  1261	  /// normal clips, exact streamed decode above the safety gate. Mode switch:
  1262	  /// LTX2_DECODE_MODE=auto|stream|tile|plain. Threshold tunable
  1263	  /// via LTX2_PLAIN_DECODE_MAX_LATF (latent frames; ~8x fewer than output frames).
  1264	  private func decodeAdaptive(_ latents: MLXArray) -> MLXArray {
  1265	    let latF = latents.dim(2)
  1266	    let latH = latents.dim(3)
  1267	    let latW = latents.dim(4)
  1268	    // Gate on TOTAL latent volume, not frame count alone: a 768x1280 clip at
  1269	    // latF 13 (24x40 spatial) is 3x the volume of 448x704 and plain decode at
  1270	    // that size drove 40GB+ of swap with critical memory pressure mid-decode,
  1271	    // corrupting the output (blank/gray frames). Budget = the proven-safe
  1272	    // 448x704x97f working point (13*14*22 = 4004) with ~2x headroom.
  1273	    let volume = latF * latH * latW
  1274	    // Default budget derived from the Metal max-buffer ceiling, measured
  1275	    // empirically (2026-07-24): plain-decoding 49f @ 768x1280 (volume 6720,
  1276	    // ~25GB peak intermediate) SILENTLY CORRUPTS the last ~60% of frames —
  1277	    // no error, just garbage — while tiled decode of the same tensor is clean.
  1278	    // 4004 (97f @ 448x704) is proven-safe; 4500 keeps margin below the cliff.
  1279	    let plainMaxVolume = resolvedConfig.plainDecodeMaxVol
  1280	    // LTX2_DUMP_LATENT=<path>: save the pre-decode latent for offline decode
  1281	    // debugging (real-values reproduction harness, #36).
  1282	    if let dumpPath = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_DUMP_LATENT"] {
  1283	      try? MLX.save(arrays: ["latent": latents.asType(.float32)], url: URL(fileURLWithPath: dumpPath))
  1284	      logger.info("VAE decode: dumped pre-decode latent to \(dumpPath)")
  1285	    }
  1286	    // LTX2_DECODE_F32=1: decode in float32 instead of bf16 (debug probe).
  1287	    let decodeF32 = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_DECODE_F32"] == "1"
  1288	    let bf = latents.asType(decodeF32 ? .float32 : .bfloat16)
  1289	
  1290	    // Decode mode — ONE switch (LTX2_DECODE_MODE): auto | stream | tile | plain.
  1291	    //   auto (default): plain under the corruption-safe volume gate, exact
  1292	    //     streamed chunked-io above it (bit-identical to plain, zero seams).
  1293	    //   stream: streamed always. tile: legacy spatial tiling (LTX2_DECODE_TILE).
  1294	    //   plain: force single-pass — EXPERT/DEBUG ONLY: above the gate, MLX Metal
  1295	    //     silently corrupts boundary frames (int32 offset overflow, mlx #3836;
  1296	    //     unfixed in any mlx-swift release as of 0.31.6).
  1297	    // Legacy alias honored: LTX2_DECODE_STREAM=0 -> tile. config.tiledDecode
  1298	    // no longer disables the safety path (Codex review 2026-07-26: a default-
  1299	    // constructed config could force a corrupting plain decode).
  1300	    let env = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment
  1301	    let mode = (env["LTX2_DECODE_MODE"] ?? (env["LTX2_DECODE_STREAM"] == "0" ? "tile" : "auto")).lowercased()
  1302	    switch mode {
  1303	    case "plain":
  1304	      if volume > plainMaxVolume {
  1305	        logger.warning("VAE decode: FORCED plain above the safety gate (volume \(volume) > \(plainMaxVolume)) — output may be silently corrupt (mlx #3836).")
  1306	      }
  1307	      logger.info("VAE decode: plain single-pass (forced; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]).")
  1308	      return vae.decode(bf)
  1309	    case "tile":
  1310	      let tile = Int(env["LTX2_DECODE_TILE"] ?? "") ?? 16
  1311	      logger.info("VAE decode: tiled (mode=tile; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — tile \(tile).")
  1312	      return vae.decodeTiled(bf, tileSize: tile, tileStride: tile - 2)
  1313	    case "stream":
  1314	      logger.info("VAE decode: streamed exact chunked-io (mode=stream; volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — zero seams.")
  1315	      return vae.decodeStreamed(bf)
  1316	    default:  // auto
  1317	      if volume > plainMaxVolume {
  1318	        logger.info("VAE decode: streamed exact chunked-io (volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)] > \(plainMaxVolume)) — zero seams.")
  1319	        return vae.decodeStreamed(bf)
  1320	      }
  1321	      logger.info("VAE decode: plain single-pass (volume \(volume) [\(latF)x\(latH)x\(latW)]) — under the safety gate.")
  1322	      return vae.decode(bf)
  1323	    }
  1324	  }
  1325	
  1326	  /// Shared two-stage refine (Phase 3): latent upsample x2 -> re-noise ->
  1327	  /// short refine denoise at high res, with the refine-volume OOM gate and
  1328	  /// optional native-res frame-0 re-anchor. Used by generateI2V AND
  1329	  /// generateMultiKeyframe (continuation chunks) so every chunk of a
  1330	  /// multi-chunk render gets identical refine treatment — previously chunks
  1330	  /// multi-chunk render gets identical refine treatment — previously chunks
  1331	  /// 2+ skipped refine entirely, dropping to base resolution mid-video.
  1332	  /// Returns the refined (or gated/upsampled) latents; caller decodes.
  1333	  private func applyTwoStageRefine(
  1334	    _ latents: MLXArray,
  1335	    latF: Int, latH: Int, latW: Int,
  1336	    textEmbeddings: MLXArray,
  1337	    negativeEmbeddings: MLXArray?,
  1338	    cfgScale: Float,
  1339	    seed: UInt64?,
  1340	    refineAnchorImage: MLXArray?,
  1341	    progressCallback: ((Int, Int) -> Void)?
  1342	  ) -> MLXArray {
  1343	    guard let ups = self.upsampler,
  1344	          resolvedConfig.twoStage else {
  1345	      return latents
  1346	    }
  1347	    logger.info("Two-stage refine: upsampling latents 2x...")
  1348	    // The latent upsampler is trained on UN-normalized (VAE-scale) latents.
  1349	    let stats = vae.decoder.perChannelStatistics
  1350	    let denorm = stats.unNormalize(latents.asType(.float32))
  1351	    let upDenorm = ups(denorm)
  1352	    let upLatent = stats.normalize(upDenorm).asType(.float32)
  1353	    eval(upLatent)
  1354	    // --- Refine bisection instrumentation (env-gated, 2026-08-01 band bug) ---
  1355	    // LTX2_REFINE_ROWSTATS=1 logs per-latent-row energy at each refine boundary
  1356	    // so the vertical-truncation fault can be localized (upsample vs re-noise
  1357	    // vs denoise vs decode) from a single short render.
  1358	    // LTX2_REFINE_DUMP_DIR=<dir> additionally saves the boundary latents as
  1359	    // .npy for offline decode/parity tests.
  1360	    let rowStatsOn = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_ROWSTATS"] == "1"
  1361	    let dumpDir = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_DUMP_DIR"]
  1362	    func rowStats(_ name: String, _ x: MLXArray) {
  1363	      guard rowStatsOn else { return }
  1364	      let f32 = x.asType(.float32)
  1365	      let energy = MLX.mean(f32 * f32, axes: [0, 1, 2, 4])  // (H,)
  1366	      eval(energy)
  1367	      let rows = energy.asArray(Float.self).map { String(format: "%.4f", $0) }
  1368	      logger.info("REFINE ROWSTATS \(name) [\(x.dim(3)) rows]: \(rows.joined(separator: " "))")
  1369	      if let dir = dumpDir {
  1370	        try? MLX.save(array: f32, url: URL(fileURLWithPath: dir).appendingPathComponent("\(name).npy"))
  1371	      }
  1372	    }
  1373	    rowStats("base", latents)
  1374	    rowStats("upsampled", upLatent)
  1375	    // Refine-volume OOM gate (2026-07-25 23:24 crash): above it, decode the
  1376	    // upsampled latent directly — upscaled-but-unrefined beats a dead server.
  1377	    let refineVolume = upLatent.dim(2) * (latH * 2) * (latW * 2)
  1378	    let refineMaxVolume = resolvedConfig.refineMaxVol
  1379	    if ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_DECODE_ONLY"] == "1" {
  1380	      logger.info("Two-stage refine: DECODE_ONLY (skipped denoise) — decoding upsampled latent directly.")
  1381	      return upLatent
  1382	    }
  1383	    if refineVolume > refineMaxVolume {
  1384	      logger.info("Two-stage refine: SKIPPED denoise (refine latent volume \(refineVolume) > \(refineMaxVolume)) — decoding upsampled latent directly (OOM guard).")
  1385	      return upLatent
  1386	    }
  1387	    MLX.GPU.clearCache()
  1388	    let rLatH = latH * 2, rLatW = latW * 2
  1389	    let refineSigmas: [Float] = resolvedConfig.refineSigmasEffective
  1390	    if let seed = seed { MLXRandom.seed(seed &+ 1000) }
  1391	    // Flow-matching re-noise: x_σ = (1-σ)·x0 + σ·ε (ComfyUI CONST noise_scaling).
  1392	    let refNoise = MLXRandom.normal(upLatent.shape).asType(.float32)
  1393	    let sigma0 = refineSigmas[0]
  1394	    let mixed = MLXArray(1 - sigma0) * upLatent + refNoise * MLXArray(sigma0)
  1395	    // Frame-0 re-anchor: raw source re-encoded at refine res when available
  1396	    // (workflow nodes 19/20); else the upsampled latent's own frame 0.
  1397	    let rF = upLatent.dim(2)
  1398	    var anchorFrame = upLatent[0..., 0..., 0..<1, 0..., 0...]
  1399	    if var refImg = refineAnchorImage {
  1400	      if refImg.ndim == 4 { refImg = refImg.expandedDimensions(axis: 2) }
  1401	      anchorFrame = vae.encode(refImg).asType(.float32)
  1402	      eval(anchorFrame)
  1403	      logger.info("Two-stage refine: frame 0 re-anchored to source re-encoded at \(rLatW * spatialCompression)x\(rLatH * spatialCompression).")
  1404	    }
  1405	    rowStats("mixed", mixed)
  1406	    let refineInit = MLX.concatenated(
  1407	      [anchorFrame, mixed[0..., 0..., 1..<rF, 0..., 0...]], axis: 2)
  1408	    let refClean = MLX.concatenated(
  1409	      [anchorFrame, upLatent[0..., 0..., 1..<rF, 0..., 0...]], axis: 2)
  1410	    let refMask = MLX.concatenated(
  1411	      [MLXArray.zeros([1, 1, 1, 1, 1]), MLXArray.ones([1, 1, rF - 1, 1, 1])],
  1412	      axis: 2).asType(.float32)
  1413	    let refState = LTX2LatentState(
  1414	      latent: refineInit, cleanLatent: refClean, denoiseMask: refMask)
  1415	    let refinePos = createPositionGrid(batchSize: 1, latF: latF, latH: rLatH, latW: rLatW)
  1416	    let refinePE = ltx2PrecomputeFreqsCIS(
  1417	      indicesGrid: refinePos, dim: innerDim,
  1418	      theta: transformer.positionalEmbeddingTheta,
  1419	      maxPos: transformer.positionalEmbeddingMaxPos,
  1420	      useMiddleIndicesGrid: transformer.useMiddleIndicesGrid,
  1421	      numAttentionHeads: transformer.numHeads,
  1422	      ropeMode: transformer.ropeMode,
  1423	      doublePrecision: transformer.doublePrecisionRoPE)
  1424	    eval(refinePE.cos, refinePE.sin)
  1425	    logger.info("Two-stage refine: denoising at \(rLatW * spatialCompression)x\(rLatH * spatialCompression), \(refineSigmas.count - 1) steps...")
  1426	    // base=euler_ancestral_cfg_pp -> refine=euler_cfg_pp (the author's pairing).
  1427	    var refined = denoisingLoop(
  1428	      latents: refineInit, positions: refinePos, precomputedPE: refinePE,
  1429	      textEmbeddings: textEmbeddings, negativeEmbeddings: negativeEmbeddings,
  1430	      sigmas: refineSigmas, cfgScale: cfgScale, state: refState,
  1431	      forceDeterministic: ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_REFINE_DETERMINISTIC"] != "0",
  1432	      progressCallback: progressCallback)
  1433	    eval(refined)
  1434	    rowStats("refined", refined)
  1435	    MLX.GPU.clearCache()
  1436	    logger.info("Two-stage refine complete.")
  1437	    return refined
  1438	  }
  1439

  4122	/// Submit-and-poll wrapper around `WarmServerCoordinator.enqueueGenerate` so
  4123	/// callers (Bree's async envelope, the Telegram bot, MCP tools) can fire a
  4124	/// render without holding a connection open for the whole denoising run.
  4125	/// A blocking `POST /v1/generate` that takes minutes is what orphaned a
  4126	/// Telegram delivery in production once: the caller's own turn timeout
  4127	/// (180s) expired before the render finished, and there was no live turn
  4128	/// left to deliver through. Queue-submit decouples render time from the
  4129	/// caller's timeout — submit returns a job id immediately, and the caller
  4130	/// polls `GET /v1/generate/status/{id}` (or `/v1/video/status/{id}`'s twin)
  4131	/// until it sees `succeeded`/`failed`, exactly like the video path already
  4132	/// does via `ReplicateVideoProxy`.
  4133	final class ImageJobTracker: @unchecked Sendable {
  4134	  private let lock = NSLock()
  4135	  private var jobs: [String: ImageJob] = [:]
  4136	
  4137	  /// Submit a job. Returns immediately with `queued` status; the render
  4138	  /// itself runs in a detached Task against the existing FIFO render queue,
  4139	  /// so submitting async doesn't skip the line ahead of synchronous callers.
  4140	  fileprivate func submit(_ payload: GeneratePayload, source: String, coordinator: WarmServerCoordinator, rawBody: Data? = nil) -> ImageJobStatus {
  4141	    let jobId = UUID().uuidString
  4142	    let job = ImageJob(id: jobId, source: source)
  4143	    lock.lock(); jobs[jobId] = job; lock.unlock()
  4144	
  4145	    Task { [weak self] in
  4146	      guard let self else { return }
  4147	      self.markProcessing(jobId)
  4148	      do {
  4149	        let result = try await coordinator.enqueueGenerate(payload, source: source, rawBody: rawBody)
  4150	        self.markSucceeded(jobId, result: result)
  4151	      } catch {
  4152	        self.markFailed(jobId, error: error)
  4153	      }
  4154	    }
  4155	    return job.toStatus()
  4156	  }
  4157	
  4158	  func status(jobId: String) -> ImageJobStatus? {
  4159	    lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
  4160	    return jobs[jobId]?.toStatus()
  4161	  }
  4162	
  4163	  private func markProcessing(_ jobId: String) {
  4164	    lock.lock(); jobs[jobId]?.state = .processing; lock.unlock()
  4165	  }
  4166	
  4167	  private func markSucceeded(_ jobId: String, result: GenerateResponse) {
  4168	    lock.lock()
  4169	    if let job = jobs[jobId] {
  4170	      job.state = .succeeded
  4171	      job.outputPath = result.outputPath
  4172	      job.durationMs = result.durationMs
  4173	      job.completedAt = Date()
  4174	    }
  4175	    lock.unlock()
  4176	  }
  4177	
  4178	  private func markFailed(_ jobId: String, error: Error) {
  4179	    lock.lock()
  4180	    if let job = jobs[jobId] {
  4181	      job.state = .failed
  4182	      job.error = error.localizedDescription
  4183	      job.completedAt = Date()
  4184	    }
  4185	    lock.unlock()
  4186	  }
  4187	
  4188	  /// Drop completed/failed jobs older than `ttl` so this doesn't grow
  4189	  /// unboundedly on a long-running server. Mirrors `ReplicateVideoProxy`'s
  4190	  /// prune convention.
  4191	  func pruneCompleted(olderThan ttl: TimeInterval = 3600) {
  4192	    lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
  4193	    let cutoff = Date().addingTimeInterval(-ttl)
  4194	    jobs = jobs.filter { _, job in
  4195	      guard let completedAt = job.completedAt else { return true }
  4196	      return completedAt > cutoff
  4197	    }
  4198	  }
  4199	}

  4250	/// Submit-and-poll tracker for LOCAL LTX-2 video renders — the video twin of
  4251	/// ``ImageJobTracker``. A local render can run for minutes across multiple
  4252	/// chunks; holding the HTTP connection open for the whole thing is what the
  4253	/// async `POST /v1/video/generate/async` + `GET /v1/video/status/{id}` pair
  4254	/// avoids. Submit returns a job id immediately; the render itself runs on the
  4255	/// coordinator's serial GPU queue (so it never shares the GPU with an image
  4256	/// render), streaming progress into the job as it goes.
  4257	///
  4258	/// The state-transition surface (`register`/`markProcessing`/`markSucceeded`/
  4259	/// `markFailed`/`setProgress`/`status`/`pruneCompleted`) is deliberately kept
  4260	/// free of any coordinator dependency so the state machine is unit-testable in
  4261	/// isolation; `submit` is the thin production wrapper that drives it against the
  4262	/// real render queue.
  4263	final class VideoJobTracker: @unchecked Sendable {
  4264	  /// Task #19: append-only lifecycle trace (submitted/started/terminal).
  4265	  /// nil in unit tests that only exercise the state machine.
  4266	  var traceStore: RenderTraceStore?
  4267	  private let lock = NSLock()
  4268	  private var jobs: [String: LocalVideoJob] = [:]
  4269	
  4270	  /// Create a tracked job in `.queued` and return (jobId, its status). Testable
  4271	  /// without a coordinator.
  4272	  @discardableResult
  4273	  func register(
  4274	    source: String, mode: VideoMode,
  4275	    resolvedConfig: [LTX2ResolvedParam]? = nil,
  4276	    tracePayload: [String: String] = [:]
  4277	  ) -> (jobId: String, status: VideoJobStatus) {
  4278	    let jobId = UUID().uuidString
  4279	    let job = LocalVideoJob(id: jobId, source: source, mode: mode)
  4280	    job.resolvedConfig = resolvedConfig
  4281	    lock.lock(); jobs[jobId] = job; lock.unlock()
  4282	    var payload = tracePayload
  4283	    payload["source"] = source
  4284	    payload["mode"] = mode.rawValue
  4285	    if let rc = resolvedConfig {
  4286	      payload["config"] = rc.map { "\($0.name)=\($0.value)(\($0.source.rawValue))" }.joined(separator: " ")
  4287	    }
  4288	    traceStore?.append(RenderTraceEvent(
  4289	      renderId: jobId, event: .submitted, taskKind: .videoRender, payload: payload))
  4290	    return (jobId, job.toStatus())
  4291	  }
  4292	
  4293	  /// Submit a local render. Returns immediately with a `.queued` status; the
  4294	  /// render runs in a detached Task against the coordinator's FIFO GPU queue.
  4295	  /// `render` receives a `report(percent)` callback to stream progress; the
  4296	  /// tracker fans that out to both this job's status and (via the coordinator's
  4297	  /// own report wired in `enqueueLocalVideo`) the /health + /queue trackers.
  4298	  fileprivate func submit(
  4299	    source: String,
  4300	    mode: VideoMode,
  4301	    coordinator: WarmServerCoordinator,
  4302	    resolvedConfig: [LTX2ResolvedParam]? = nil,
  4303	    tracePayload: [String: String] = [:],
  4304	    render: @escaping @Sendable (@escaping @Sendable (Int) -> Void) throws -> LTX2VideoResult
  4305	  ) -> VideoJobStatus {
  4306	    let (jobId, queued) = register(
  4307	      source: source, mode: mode, resolvedConfig: resolvedConfig,
  4308	      tracePayload: tracePayload)
  4309	    Task { [weak self] in
  4310	      guard let self else { return }
  4311	      self.markProcessing(jobId)
  4312	      do {
  4313	        let result = try await coordinator.enqueueLocalVideo { coordReport in
  4314	          try render { pct in
  4315	            // Fan progress to both the coordinator's health/queue trackers and
  4316	            // this job's own status.
  4317	            coordReport(pct)
  4318	            self.setProgress(jobId, pct)
  4319	          }
  4320	        }
  4321	        self.markSucceeded(jobId, result: result)
  4322	      } catch {
  4323	        self.markFailed(jobId, error: error)
  4324	      }
  4325	    }
  4326	    return queued
  4327	  }
  4328	
  4329	  /// Submit a multi-step orchestration (storyboard, #237). Unlike `submit`,
  4330	  /// the work closure is NOT wrapped in a single `enqueueLocalVideo` — it
  4331	  /// issues its OWN coordinator enqueues (one per shot render / i2i insert),
  4332	  /// so each step takes a normal turn on the FIFO GPU queue and other jobs
  4333	  /// can interleave between shots. Wrapping the whole storyboard in one queue
  4334	  /// entry would deadlock: the closure would enqueue from inside the queue.
  4335	  fileprivate func submitOrchestrated(
  4336	    source: String,
  4337	    mode: VideoMode,
  4338	    work: @escaping @Sendable (@escaping @Sendable (Int) -> Void) async throws -> LTX2VideoResult
  4339	  ) -> VideoJobStatus {
  4340	    let (jobId, queued) = register(source: source, mode: mode)
  4341	    Task { [weak self] in
  4342	      guard let self else { return }
  4343	      self.markProcessing(jobId)
  4344	      do {
  4345	        let result = try await work { pct in
  4346	          self.setProgress(jobId, pct)
  4347	        }
  4348	        self.markSucceeded(jobId, result: result)
  4349	      } catch {
  4350	        self.markFailed(jobId, error: error)
  4351	      }
  4352	    }
  4353	    return queued
  4354	  }
  4355	
  4356	  func status(jobId: String) -> VideoJobStatus? {
  4357	    lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
  4358	    return jobs[jobId]?.toStatus()
  4359	  }
  4360	
  4361	  func markProcessing(_ jobId: String) {
  4362	    lock.lock(); jobs[jobId]?.state = .processing; lock.unlock()
  4363	    traceStore?.append(RenderTraceEvent(
  4364	      renderId: jobId, event: .started, taskKind: .videoRender, payload: [:]))
  4365	  }
  4366	
  4367	  func setProgress(_ jobId: String, _ percent: Int) {
  4368	    lock.lock(); jobs[jobId]?.progressPercent = min(100, max(0, percent)); lock.unlock()
  4369	  }
  4370	
  4371	  func markSucceeded(_ jobId: String, result: LTX2VideoResult) {
  4372	    lock.lock()
  4373	    if let job = jobs[jobId] {
  4374	      job.state = .succeeded
  4375	      job.outputPath = result.outputPath
  4376	      job.frameCount = result.frameCount
  4377	      job.videoDurationSeconds = Int(result.durationSeconds.rounded())
  4378	      job.durationMs = Int(result.elapsedSeconds * 1000)
  4379	      job.progressPercent = 100
  4380	      job.completedAt = Date()
  4381	    }
  4382	    lock.unlock()
  4383	    traceStore?.append(RenderTraceEvent(
  4384	      renderId: jobId, event: .terminal, taskKind: .videoRender,
  4385	      payload: [
  4386	        "status": "succeeded",
  4387	        "output_path": result.outputPath,
  4388	        "frames": String(result.frameCount),
  4389	        "elapsed_ms": String(Int(result.elapsedSeconds * 1000)),
  4390	      ]))
  4391	  }
  4392	
  4393	  func markFailed(_ jobId: String, error: Error) {
  4394	    lock.lock()
  4395	    if let job = jobs[jobId] {
  4396	      job.state = .failed
  4397	      job.error = error.localizedDescription
  4398	      job.completedAt = Date()
  4399	    }
  4400	    lock.unlock()
  4401	    traceStore?.append(RenderTraceEvent(
  4402	      renderId: jobId, event: .terminal, taskKind: .videoRender,
  4403	      payload: ["status": "failed", "error": error.localizedDescription]))
  4404	  }
  4405	
  4860	    }
  4861	    let start = Date()
  4862	    let entry = try await modelPool.load(
  4863	      modelSpec: modelSpec,
  4864	      quantization: quantization,
  4865	      initialLoRAs: activeLoRAs
  4866	    )
  4867	    let loadTimeMs = Int(Date().timeIntervalSince(start) * 1000.0)
  4868	
  4869	    if activate {
  4870	      try await poolActivate(modelId: entry.id)
  4871	    }
  4872	
  4873	    return ModelLoadResponse(
  4874	      status: "loaded",
  4875	      model: entry.modelSpec,
  4876	      family: entry.family.rawValue,
  4877	      loadTimeMs: loadTimeMs,
  4878	      vramEstimateMB: entry.vramEstimateMB,
  4879	      poolSize: await modelPool.count(),
  4880	      poolBudgetMB: await modelPool.budget()
  4881	    )
  4882	  }
  4883	
  4884	  /// Activate a model that is already in the pool.
  4885	  @discardableResult
  4886	  func poolActivate(modelId: String) async throws -> ModelActivateResponse {
  4887	    // Try by pool key first, then by model spec.
  4888	    let entry: PoolEntry
  4889	    if let e = await modelPool.findEntry(for: modelId) {
  4890	      entry = try await modelPool.activate(modelId: e.id)
  4891	    } else {
  4892	      throw ModelPoolError.modelNotInPool(modelId)
  4893	    }
  4894	
  4895	    // Sync coordinator state from pool entry.
  4896	    currentModelFamily = entry.family
  4897	    // An image model is now resident and active — clear the video-eviction flag
  4898	    // so a later render doesn't redundantly reload (#218).
  4899	    imageModelsEvicted = false
  4900	    // Track the activated pool model's spec so generation requests use
  4901	    // the correct model instead of the startup configuration.modelSpec.
  4902	    activePoolModelSpec = entry.modelSpec
  4903	    switch entry.family {
  4904	    case .krea2:
  4905	      krea2Pipeline = entry.box.pipeline as? Krea2Pipeline
  4906	    case .chroma:
  4907	      chromaPipeline = entry.box.pipeline as? ChromaPipeline
  4908	      chromaTokenizer = entry.box.context["tokenizer"] as? ChromaTokenizer
  4909	    case .fibo:
  4910	      fiboPipeline = entry.box.pipeline as? FiboPipeline
  4911	      detectedFiboModel = entry.detectedInfo as? FiboDetectedModel
  4912	    case .flux2:
  4913	      flux2Pipeline = entry.box.pipeline as? Flux2Pipeline
  4914	      detectedFlux2Model = entry.detectedInfo as? Flux2DetectedModel
  4915	    case .flux1:
  4916	      // Reassign the pipeline so that runSwap and runFlux1Generate
  4917	      // operate on the pool-loaded pipeline, not the original one (#138).
  4918	      if let poolZImage = entry.box.pipeline as? ZImagePipeline {
  4919	        pipeline = poolZImage
  4920	        // Pre-load full VAE for the pool-activated pipeline to avoid
  4921	        // deadlock on first img2img request (same issue as #141).
  4922	        do {
  4923	          try poolZImage.prepareFullVAE()
  4924	        } catch {
  4925	          logger.warning("Failed to pre-load full VAE for pool model '\(entry.modelSpec)': \(error)")
  4926	        }
  4927	      }
  4928	      zimageVariant = (entry.detectedInfo as? ZImageVariant) ?? .turbo
  4929	    }
  4930	    pipelinePrepared = true
  4931	    // Model/family/variant just changed — refresh the health snapshot (#217).
  4932	    publishHealth()
  4933	
  4934	    return ModelActivateResponse(
  4935	      status: "activated",
  4936	      model: entry.modelSpec,
  4937	      family: entry.family.rawValue
  4938	    )
  4939	  }
  4940	
  4941	  /// Unload a model from the pool.
  4942	  func poolUnload(modelId: String) async throws -> ModelUnloadResponse {
  4943	    // Find the entry to get the model spec before unloading.
  4944	    guard let entry = await modelPool.findEntry(for: modelId) else {
  4945	      throw ModelPoolError.modelNotInPool(modelId)
  4946	    }
  4947	    let freedMB = try await modelPool.unload(modelId: entry.id)
  4948	    return ModelUnloadResponse(
  4949	      status: "unloaded",
  4950	      model: entry.modelSpec,
  4951	      freedMB: freedMB,
  4952	      poolSize: await modelPool.count()
  4953	    )
  4954	  }
  4955	
  4956	  /// List all models in the pool.
  4957	  func poolList() async -> ModelPoolListResponse {
  4958	    let entries = await modelPool.listPool()
  4959	    let activeId = await modelPool.activeModelId()
  4960	    let activeSpec: String?
  4961	    if let aid = activeId, let entry = await modelPool.findEntry(for: aid) {
  4962	      activeSpec = entry.modelSpec
  4963	    } else {
  4964	      activeSpec = nil
  4965	    }
  4966	    return ModelPoolListResponse(
  4967	      active: activeSpec,
  4968	      pool: entries,
  4969	      totalVramMB: await modelPool.totalVramMB(),
  4970	      budgetMB: await modelPool.budget()
  4971	    )
  4972	  }
  4973	
  4974	  func enqueueGenerate(
  4975	    _ payload: GeneratePayload,
  4976	    progressHandler: (@Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void)? = nil,
  4977	    latentPreviewHandler: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler? = nil,
  4978	    source: String = "api",
  4979	    rawBody: Data? = nil
  4980	  ) async throws -> GenerateResponse {
  4981	    if shuttingDown {
  4982	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  4983	    }
  4984	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  4985	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  4986	    }
  4987	
  4988	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  4989	      pending.append(PendingJob(source: source, operation: .generate(payload, ContinuationBox(continuation), progressHandler, latentPreviewHandler), rawBody: rawBody))
  4990	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  4991	    }
  4992	  }
  4993	
  4994	  func enqueueSwap(_ payload: LoRASwapPayload, rawBody: Data? = nil) async throws -> LoRASwapResponse {
  4995	    if shuttingDown {
  4996	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  4997	    }
  4998	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  4999	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  5000	    }
  5001	
  5002	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  5003	      pending.append(PendingJob(operation: .swap(payload, ContinuationBox(continuation)), rawBody: rawBody))
  5004	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  5005	    }
  5006	  }
  5007	
  5008	  func enqueueControlGenerate(_ request: ZImageControlGenerationRequest) async throws -> GenerateResponse {
  5009	    if shuttingDown {
  5010	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  5011	    }
  5012	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  5013	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  5014	    }
  5015	
  5016	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  5017	      pending.append(PendingJob(operation: .controlGenerate(request, ContinuationBox(continuation))))
  5018	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  5019	    }
  5020	  }
  5021	
  5022	  /// Run a Krita model auto-switch through the FIFO render queue so the pool
  5023	  /// load/activate executes after any in-flight render finishes instead of
  5024	  /// mutating the active pipeline underneath it. The body performs the actual
  5025	  /// pool operations and returns whether a switch occurred.
  5026	  func enqueueModelSwitch(_ body: @escaping @Sendable () async throws -> Bool) async throws -> Bool {
  5027	    if shuttingDown {
  5028	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  5029	    }
  5030	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  5031	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  5032	    }
  5033	
  5034	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  5035	      pending.append(PendingJob(operation: .modelSwitch(body, ContinuationBox(continuation))))
  5036	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  5037	    }
  5038	  }
  5039	
  5040	  /// Enqueue a local LTX-2 video generation through the FIFO render queue so
  5041	  /// it never runs the GPU concurrently with an image render.
  5042	  /// Enqueue a local LTX-2 render on the serial GPU queue. `body` receives a
  5043	  /// `report(percent)` callback (0-100) it should call from the generator's
  5044	  /// per-chunk/per-step progress hook; the coordinator wires it into the
  5045	  /// lock-based progress + health trackers so /health and /v1/queue reflect the
  5046	  /// live render without an actor hop (mirrors the image render path, #217).
  5047	  func enqueueLocalVideo(_ body: @escaping @Sendable (@escaping @Sendable (Int) -> Void) throws -> LTX2VideoResult) async throws -> LTX2VideoResult {
  5048	    if shuttingDown {
  5049	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  5050	    }
  5051	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  5052	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  5053	    }
  5054	
  5055	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  5056	      pending.append(PendingJob(operation: .localVideo(body, ContinuationBox(continuation))))
  5057	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  5058	    }
  5059	  }
  5060	
  5061	  func enqueueShutdown() async throws -> ShutdownResponse {
  5062	    if shuttingDown {
  5063	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  5064	    }
  5065	
  5066	    shuttingDown = true
  5067	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  5068	      pending.append(PendingJob(operation: .shutdown(ContinuationBox(continuation))))
  5069	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  5070	    }
  5071	  }
  5072	
  5073	  /// Publish the current health-relevant state into the lock-based
  5074	  /// ``LiveHealthState`` so GET /health reads it without hopping onto this
  5075	  /// actor (which blocks for a whole render). Call at every state transition:
  5076	  /// job start/end, enqueue, model/LoRA change, pause, shutdown, startup (#217).
  5077	  ///
  5078	  /// `isRendering` keys off `activeJobId` (set the instant a job is dequeued,
  5079	  /// before the render method's first await sets `activeRenderStartedAt`), and
  5080	  /// the render start time falls back to `currentJobStartedAt` so the age/stale
  5081	  /// signal is correct throughout the synchronous GPU section.
  5082	  private func publishHealth() {
  5083	    let snap = HealthSnapshot(
  5084	      shuttingDown: shuttingDown,
  5085	      model: activePoolModelSpec ?? configuration.modelSpec ?? ZImageRepository.id,
  5086	      modelFamily: currentModelFamily.rawValue,
  5087	      modelVariant: currentModelFamily == .fibo ? "fibo" : (currentModelFamily == .flux1 ? zimageVariant.rawValue : detectedFlux2Model?.variant),
  5088	      loaded: pipelinePrepared,
  5089	      loras: activeLoRAs.map(LoRAState.init),
  5090	      renderCount: successfulRenderCount,
  5091	      failedRenderCount: failedRenderCount,
  5092	      pendingCount: pending.count,
  5093	      isRendering: activeJobId != nil,
  5094	      activeRenderStartedAt: activeRenderStartedAt ?? currentJobStartedAt,
  5095	      activeJobId: activeJobId,
  5096	      lastRenderDurationMs: lastRenderDurationMs,
  5097	      lastError: lastError,
  5098	      isPaused: isPaused,
  5099	      activeSummary: activeJobSummary,
  5100	      activeSource: activeJobSource,
  5101	      pending: pending.map { job in
  5102	        QueueJobInfo(
  5103	          id: job.id,
  5104	          kind: Self.kind(of: job.operation),
  5105	          summary: Self.describe(job.operation),
  5106	          source: job.source,
  5107	          enqueuedAt: job.enqueuedAt
  5108	        )
  5109	      },
  5110	      maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests
  5111	    )
  5112	    liveHealth.publish(snap)
  5113	  }
  5114	
  5115	  /// Mirror the recoverable slice of the queue (see QueuePersistence.swift)
  5116	  /// to disk so it survives a crash. Called at every mutation: enqueue,
  5117	  /// dequeue-into-active, job completion, cancel, reorder, clear. Cheap
  5118	  /// (small JSON, atomic write) relative to how rarely the queue actually
  5119	  /// changes compared to render duration.
  5120	  private func persistQueueState() {
  5121	    let active: PersistedQueueJob? = {
  5122	      guard let rawBody = activeJobRawBody,
  5123	            let kind = activeJobKindForPersistence,
  5124	            let id = activeJobId else { return nil }
  5125	      return PersistedQueueJob(
  5126	        id: id, kind: kind, source: activeJobSource ?? "api",
  5127	        enqueuedAt: currentJobStartedAt ?? Date(), rawBody: rawBody)
  5128	    }()
  5129	    let pendingJobs: [PersistedQueueJob] = pending.compactMap { job in
  5130	      guard let rawBody = job.rawBody else { return nil }
  5131	      return PersistedQueueJob(
  5132	        id: job.id, kind: Self.kind(of: job.operation), source: job.source,
  5133	        enqueuedAt: job.enqueuedAt, rawBody: rawBody)
  5134	    }
  5135	    QueueStateStore.save(PersistedQueueState(active: active, pending: pendingJobs))
  5136	  }
  5137	
  5138	  /// Queue status for the ComfyUI bridge /queue endpoint.
  5139	  func queueStatus() -> ComfyBridgeQueueStatus {
  5140	    return ComfyBridgeQueueStatus(
  5141	      pendingCount: pending.count,
  5142	      maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests,
  5143	      isRendering: activeRenderStartedAt != nil,
  5144	      currentJobId: activeJobId,
  5145	      progressPercent: progressTracker.get(),
  5146	      renderCount: successfulRenderCount,
  5147	      failedCount: failedRenderCount
  5148	    )
  5149	  }
  5150	
  5151	  /// Cancel the in-flight render, if any (ComfyUI /interrupt).
  5152	  /// Returns true if a render task was cancelled. Pending jobs are unaffected.
  5153	  func cancelActiveRender() -> Bool {
  5154	    guard let task = activeRenderTask else { return false }
  5155	    task.cancel()
  5156	    return true
  5157	  }
  5158	
  5159	  /// Clear all pending jobs from the queue. Active job continues.
  5160	  func clearPending() -> Int {
  5161	    let count = pending.count
  5162	    // Cancel all pending continuations with a queue-clear error (distinct
  5163	    // from shuttingDown — the server keeps running after a queue clear).
  5164	    for job in pending {
  5165	      Self.cancel(job.operation)
  5166	    }
  5167	    pending.removeAll()
  5168	    publishHealth()
  5169	    persistQueueState()
  5170	    return count
  5171	  }
  5172	
  5173	  /// Cancel one pending job by id. Returns false when the id isn't queued
  5174	  /// (already running or already finished).
  5175	  func cancelPending(id: String) -> Bool {
  5176	    guard let index = pending.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return false }
  5177	    Self.cancel(pending[index].operation)
  5178	    pending.remove(at: index)
  5179	    publishHealth()
  5180	    persistQueueState()
  5181	    return true
  5182	  }
  5183	
  5184	  private static func cancel(_ operation: QueuedOperation) {
  5185	    switch operation {
  5186	    case .generate(_, let cont, _, _):
  5187	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5188	    case .controlGenerate(_, let cont):
  5189	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5190	    case .swap(_, let cont):
  5191	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5192	    case .modelSwitch(_, let cont):
  5193	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5194	    case .localVideo(_, let cont):
  5195	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5196	    case .shutdown(let cont):
  5197	      cont.resume(throwing: ServerError.cancelled)
  5198	    }
  5199	  }
  5200	
  5201	  /// One line describing an operation for queue listings.
  5202	  private static func describe(_ operation: QueuedOperation) -> String {
  5203	    switch operation {
  5204	    case .generate(let payload, _, _, _):
  5205	      return "Render: \(payload.prompt.prefix(100))"
  5206	    case .controlGenerate(let request, _):
  5207	      return "ControlNet render: \(request.prompt.prefix(100))"
  5208	    case .swap(let payload, _):
  5209	      return "LoRA swap (\(payload.loras.count))"
  5210	    case .modelSwitch:
  5211	      return "Model switch"
  5212	    case .localVideo:
  5213	      return "LTX-2 video"
  5214	    case .shutdown:
  5215	      return "Shutdown"
  5216	    }
  5217	  }
  5218	
  5219	  private static func kind(of operation: QueuedOperation) -> String {
  5220	    switch operation {
  5221	    case .generate: return "generate"
  5222	    case .controlGenerate: return "controlnet"
  5223	    case .swap: return "lora_swap"
  5224	    case .modelSwitch: return "model_switch"
  5225	    case .localVideo: return "video"
  5226	    case .shutdown: return "shutdown"
  5227	    }
  5228	  }
  5229	
  5230	  /// One pending entry in a /v1/queue listing.
  5231	  struct QueueJobInfo: Sendable {
  5232	    let id: String
  5233	    let kind: String
  5234	    let summary: String
  5235	    let source: String
  5236	    let enqueuedAt: Date
  5237	  }
  5238	
  5239	  // MARK: - Queue controls (pause / resume / reorder)
  5240	
  5241	  func setPaused(_ paused: Bool) {
  5242	    isPaused = paused
  5243	    if !paused { startProcessingIfNeeded() }
  5244	    publishHealth()
  5245	  }
  5246	
  5247	  /// Move a pending job within the queue. direction: up | down | top | bottom.
  5248	  /// Returns true if the job was found and moved.
  5249	  func movePending(id: String, direction: String) -> Bool {
  5250	    guard let idx = pending.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return false }
  5251	    let job = pending.remove(at: idx)
  5252	    let target: Int
  5253	    switch direction {
  5254	    case "top": target = 0
  5255	    case "bottom": target = pending.count
  5256	    case "up": target = max(0, idx - 1)
  5257	    case "down": target = min(pending.count, idx + 1)
  5258	    default: pending.insert(job, at: idx); return false
  5259	    }
  5260	    pending.insert(job, at: target)
  5261	    publishHealth()
  5262	    persistQueueState()
  5263	    return true
  5264	  }
  5265	
  5266	  private func startProcessingIfNeeded() {
  5267	    // Every enqueue routes through here, so this is the one spot that reflects a
  5268	    // just-changed pending count into the lock-based health snapshot (#217).
  5269	    publishHealth()
  5270	    persistQueueState()
  5271	    guard !isProcessing else { return }
  5272	    isProcessing = true
  5273	    Task {
  5274	      await processLoop()
  5275	    }
  5276	  }
  5277	
  5278	  private func processLoop() async {
  5279	    while true {
  5280	      // Paused: stop pulling new jobs until resumed (setPaused restarts the loop).
  5281	      if isPaused {
  5282	        isProcessing = false
  5283	        return
  5284	      }
  5285	      guard !pending.isEmpty else {
  5286	        isProcessing = false
  5287	        return
  5288	      }
  5289	
  5290	      let job = pending.removeFirst()
  5291	      activeJobSummary = Self.describe(job.operation)
  5292	      activeJobSource = job.source
  5293	      // Keep the same id the job had while pending, so clients can correlate.
  5294	      activeJobId = job.id
  5295	      currentJobStartedAt = Date()
  5296	      // Move the job's recoverable data (if any) from "pending" to "active" in
  5297	      // the persisted queue snapshot, so a crash mid-render still recovers it
  5298	      // (replayed from scratch on restart — there's no way to resume a
  5299	      // partial diffusion render, so "at least once" is the correctness goal).
  5300	      activeJobRawBody = job.rawBody
  5301	      activeJobKindForPersistence = Self.kind(of: job.operation)
  5302	      // Publish is_rendering + job id BEFORE the synchronous GPU section begins,
  5303	      // so /health reflects the render for its whole (actor-blocking) duration (#217).
  5304	      publishHealth()
  5305	      persistQueueState()
  5306	      defer {
  5307	        activeJobSummary = nil; activeJobSource = nil; activeJobId = nil; currentJobStartedAt = nil
  5308	        activeJobRawBody = nil; activeJobKindForPersistence = nil
  5309	        publishHealth()
  5310	        persistQueueState()
  5311	      }
  5312	      switch job.operation {
  5313	      case .generate(let payload, let continuation, let progressHandler, let latentPreviewHandler):
  5314	        // Run the render in a retained child task so /interrupt can cancel it
  5315	        // without cancelling the queue's processing loop.
  5310	        persistQueueState()
  5311	      }
  5312	      switch job.operation {
  5313	      case .generate(let payload, let continuation, let progressHandler, let latentPreviewHandler):
  5314	        // Run the render in a retained child task so /interrupt can cancel it
  5315	        // without cancelling the queue's processing loop.
  5316	        let renderTask = Task {
  5317	          await self.runGenerate(payload, continuation: continuation, progressHandler: progressHandler, latentPreviewHandler: latentPreviewHandler)
  5318	        }
  5319	        activeRenderTask = renderTask
  5320	        await renderTask.value
  5321	        activeRenderTask = nil
  5322	      case .controlGenerate(let request, let continuation):
  5323	        let renderTask = Task {
  5324	          await self.runControlGenerate(request, continuation: continuation)
  5325	        }
  5326	        activeRenderTask = renderTask
  5327	        await renderTask.value
  5328	        activeRenderTask = nil
  5329	      case .swap(let payload, let continuation):
  5330	        await runSwap(payload, continuation: continuation)
  5331	      case .modelSwitch(let body, let continuation):
  5332	        do {
  5333	          continuation.resume(returning: try await body())
  5334	        } catch {
  5335	          continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5336	        }
  5337	      case .localVideo(let body, let continuation):
  5338	        // Runs on the serial queue so LTX-2 never shares the GPU with a render.
  5339	        activeRenderStartedAt = Date()
  5340	        // activeJobId is set from job.id at the top of the loop.
  5341	        defer { activeRenderStartedAt = nil; activeJobId = nil }
  5342	        // #218: single-heavy-model residency. Right before the ~65GB LTX-2
  5343	        // stack loads inside body(), vacate ALL image models (pool + per-family
  5344	        // pipelines), then verify there is enough physical RAM to proceed —
  5345	        // refuse cleanly instead of OOM-killing the whole process. Doing this
  5346	        // on the serial render queue guarantees no image render can re-load
  5347	        // between the eviction and the video load.
  5348	        let freedForVideoMB = await releaseImageModelsForVideo()
  5349	        var availableForVideo = MemoryProbe.systemAvailableMemoryBytes()
  5350	        // Precision-keyed (#230): an int8-quantized checkpoint dir gates at
  5351	        // 40GB instead of the bf16 65GB, so video coexists with LM Studio.
  5352	        // Warm-stack discount: when the LTX-2 generator is ALREADY loaded, its
  5353	        // ~46-65GB of weights are resident and counted AGAINST free memory —
  5354	        // demanding the full cold-load estimate double-counts them and refuses
  5355	        // healthy back-to-back videos (observed 2026-07-25 22:38: 45GB free
  5356	        // with the stack warm, admit=false, job bounced). A warm render only
  5357	        // needs activation headroom (streamed decode bounds the decode peak).
  5358	        let videoStackWarm = videoHolder.get()?.isLoaded == true
  5359	        let ltx2Need = videoStackWarm
  5360	          ? 24 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
  5361	          : HeavyModelAdmission.ltx2EstimateBytes(
  5362	              forWeightsPath: configuration.ltx2WeightsPath)
  5363	        // Drain-until-settled (#34): back-to-back renders (e.g. Kira's i2v →
  5364	        // multi-keyframe in the same second) start while the previous job's
  5365	        // MLX buffer pool + lazy macOS reclaim still hold tens of GB. Admission
  5366	        // then either refuses spuriously OR passes on memory that isn't really
  5367	        // reclaimed yet — and the render dies ~60s in on a Metal allocation
  5368	        // abort (SIGKILL, no app error; 3x reproduced 2026-07-25). Actively
  5369	        // drain and re-probe until free ≥ need + margin, up to ~18s, before
  5370	        // deciding. clearCache() returns pooled buffers; the settle sleep gives
  5371	        // the OS time to actually reclaim them.
  5372	        let drainMargin: UInt64 = 6 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
  5373	        var drainAttempts = 0
  5374	        while availableForVideo < ltx2Need + drainMargin && drainAttempts < 6 {
  5375	          GPU.clearCache()
  5376	          try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 3_000_000_000)
  5377	          availableForVideo = MemoryProbe.systemAvailableMemoryBytes()
  5378	          drainAttempts += 1
  5379	          logger.info("LTX-2 admission drain #\(drainAttempts): \(availableForVideo >> 20)MB free (target \((ltx2Need + drainMargin) >> 20)MB)")
  5380	        }
  5381	        let admitVideo = heavyAdmission.admitsAfterEvict(
  5382	          needBytes: ltx2Need, freeBytes: availableForVideo)
  5383	        logger.info("LTX-2 admission: freed ~\(freedForVideoMB)MB image, \(availableForVideo >> 20)MB free after \(drainAttempts) drain(s), need ~\(ltx2Need >> 20)MB\(videoStackWarm ? " (warm stack)" : "") → admit=\(admitVideo) (#218/#34)")
  5384	        if !admitVideo {
  5385	          continuation.resume(throwing: WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  5386	            message: "Insufficient memory for LTX-2 video: only \(availableForVideo >> 20)MB free after evicting image models (need ~\(ltx2Need >> 20)MB)"))
  5387	        } else {
  5388	          videoHolder.beginRender()
  5389	          // Stream render progress into the lock-based trackers /health + /queue
  5390	          // read, exactly like the image path. Both trackers are Sendable, so the
  5391	          // off-actor @Sendable report closure can update them without an actor
  5392	          // hop. Cleared on completion via defer.
  5393	          let progress = self.progressTracker
  5394	          let health = self.liveHealth
  5395	          progress.set(0)
  5396	          health.setProgress(0)
  5397	          let report: @Sendable (Int) -> Void = { pct in
  5398	            progress.set(pct)
  5399	            health.setProgress(pct)
  5400	          }
  5401	          defer {
  5402	            videoHolder.endRender()
  5403	            progress.set(nil)
  5404	            health.setProgress(nil)
  5405	          }
  5406	          do {
  5407	            continuation.resume(returning: try body(report))
  5408	          } catch {
  5409	            continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5410	          }
  5411	        }
  5412	      case .shutdown(let continuation):
  5413	        continuation.resume(
  5414	          returning: ShutdownResponse(
  5415	            success: true,
  5416	            message: "Server shutdown requested"
  5417	          )
  5418	        )
  5419	      }
  5420	    }
  5421	  }
  5422	
  5423	  private func runGenerate(_ payload: GeneratePayload, continuation: ContinuationBox<GenerateResponse>, progressHandler: (@Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void)? = nil, latentPreviewHandler: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler? = nil) async {
  5424	    // Queue telemetry: tag this render with a job id and stream denoising
  5425	    // progress into the tracker that queueStatus() reads. Cleared on return
     1	// HeavyModelAdmission.swift — single-heavy-model residency accounting.
     2	//
     3	// The engine can only afford ONE heavy generative model resident in unified
     4	// memory at a time. A resident image model (krea2 alone footprints ~75GB in
     5	// practice, far above the pool's 22GB estimate) plus LTX-2 video (~60-70GB)
     6	// exceeds a 128GB machine's physical RAM and trips OS_REASON_JETSAM.
     7	//
     8	// The ModelPool's VRAM budget is BLIND to LTX-2 (it loads entirely outside the
     9	// pool), so image + video could co-reside and OOM. This type owns the pure
    10	// accounting decision — "evict the other class, and can this load proceed?" —
    11	// so it is unit-testable with injected byte figures, independent of live
    12	// machine state. WarmServer wires it to MemoryProbe for the live numbers.
    13	//
    14	// Issue: #218
    15	
    16	import Foundation
    17	
    18	/// Which class of heavy model is competing for unified memory.
    19	public enum HeavyModelClass: String, Sendable, Equatable {
    20	  case image
    21	  case video
    22	}
    23	
    24	/// Pure admission-control logic for loading a heavy model into unified memory.
    25	public struct HeavyModelAdmission: Sendable, Equatable {
    26	  /// Physical-RAM headroom to keep free after a heavy load (default 15GB).
    27	  /// Leaving headroom avoids jetsam from transient allocation spikes during
    28	  /// the first denoise/decode pass.
    29	  public let headroomBytes: UInt64
    30	
    31	  public init(headroomBytes: UInt64 = 15 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024) {
    32	    self.headroomBytes = headroomBytes
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  /// Conservative resident-footprint estimate for the LTX-2 video stack
    36	  /// (transformer + connector + VAE + Gemma text encoder), used when deciding
    37	  /// whether an incoming image load must first evict a resident video model.
    38	  public static let ltx2EstimateBytes: UInt64 = 65 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
    39	
    40	  /// Estimate for an int8-quantized LTX-2 stack (`ComfyBox quantize-ltx2`
    41	  /// checkpoint + mlx-community int8 Gemma, comfybox#230): video-DiT blocks
    42	  /// 24→12 GB packed, Gemma 24→12.6 GB, plus VAE/embeds and the same
    43	  /// activation headroom the 65 GB bf16 gate implied. Same-seed A/B validated
    44	  /// 2026-07-16 (RMSE ~0.005 vs bf16, render succeeded on the first try).
    45	  public static let ltx2EstimateBytesInt8: UInt64 = 40 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024
    46	
    47	  /// Precision-keyed LTX-2 estimate: int8 when the weights directory carries
    48	  /// a `quant_manifest.json` (written by `quantize-ltx2`), else the bf16
    49	  /// figure. Keyed off the DIRECTORY so a launch-arg flip back to the bf16
    50	  /// weights restores the conservative gate with no rebuild (FDD §4.3).
    51	  public static func ltx2EstimateBytes(forWeightsPath path: String?) -> UInt64 {
    52	    guard let path, !path.isEmpty else { return ltx2EstimateBytes }
    53	    let manifest = URL(fileURLWithPath: path).appendingPathComponent("quant_manifest.json")
    54	    return FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: manifest.path)
    55	      ? ltx2EstimateBytesInt8 : ltx2EstimateBytes
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  public struct Decision: Sendable, Equatable {
    59	    /// Release the currently-resident models of the *other* class first.
    60	    public let evictOther: Bool
    61	    /// Whether the load may proceed at all.
    62	    public let admit: Bool
    63	    /// Whether headroom will be satisfied (false ⇒ admitted but tight).
    64	    public let hasHeadroom: Bool
    65	    public let reason: String
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  /// Projected free memory once the other heavy class is released.
    69	  /// `availableBytes` already excludes the other class's wired arrays, so
    70	  /// releasing it adds `otherResidentBytes` back to the free pool.
    71	  public func projectedFreeAfterEvict(availableBytes: UInt64, otherResidentBytes: UInt64) -> UInt64 {
    72	    availableBytes &+ otherResidentBytes
    73	  }
    74	
    75	  /// Decide whether a heavy load of `needBytes` may proceed.
    76	  ///
    77	  /// - Parameters:
    78	  ///   - needBytes: estimated footprint of the model we are about to load.
    79	  ///   - availableBytes: reclaimable/free system memory now
    80	  ///     (`MemoryProbe.systemAvailableMemoryBytes()`).
    81	  ///   - otherResidentBytes: estimated footprint of the OTHER heavy class
    82	  ///     currently resident (0 if none). Always evicted first.
    83	  public func decide(needBytes: UInt64, availableBytes: UInt64, otherResidentBytes: UInt64) -> Decision {
    84	    let evict = otherResidentBytes > 0
    85	    let projectedFree = projectedFreeAfterEvict(availableBytes: availableBytes, otherResidentBytes: otherResidentBytes)
    86	
    87	    if projectedFree >= needBytes &+ headroomBytes {
    88	      return Decision(
    89	        evictOther: evict, admit: true, hasHeadroom: true,
    90	        reason: "ok: projected \(projectedFree >> 20)MB free ≥ need \(needBytes >> 20)MB + headroom \(headroomBytes >> 20)MB")
    91	    }
    92	    if projectedFree >= needBytes {
    93	      return Decision(
    94	        evictOther: evict, admit: true, hasHeadroom: false,
    95	        reason: "tight: projected \(projectedFree >> 20)MB free ≥ need \(needBytes >> 20)MB but below headroom target")
    96	    }
    97	    return Decision(
    98	      evictOther: evict, admit: false, hasHeadroom: false,
    99	      reason: "refuse: projected \(projectedFree >> 20)MB free < need \(needBytes >> 20)MB — would OOM")
   100	  }
   101	
   102	  /// Post-eviction gate: after the other class has actually been released and
   103	  /// memory re-probed, is there room for `needBytes`? Admits when free memory
   104	  /// covers the need (headroom preferred but not mandatory once we are already
   105	  /// single-resident — refusing here would strand the only heavy model).
   106	  public func admitsAfterEvict(needBytes: UInt64, freeBytes: UInt64) -> Bool {
   107	    freeBytes >= needBytes
   108	  }
   109	}

  4974	  func enqueueGenerate(
  4975	    _ payload: GeneratePayload,
  4976	    progressHandler: (@Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void)? = nil,
  4977	    latentPreviewHandler: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler? = nil,
  4978	    source: String = "api",
  4979	    rawBody: Data? = nil
  4980	  ) async throws -> GenerateResponse {
  4981	    if shuttingDown {
  4982	      throw ServerError.shuttingDown
  4983	    }
  4984	    if pending.count >= configuration.maxPendingRequests {
  4985	      throw ServerError.queueFull(maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests)
  4986	    }
  4987	
  4988	    return try await withCheckedThrowingContinuation { continuation in
  4989	      pending.append(PendingJob(source: source, operation: .generate(payload, ContinuationBox(continuation), progressHandler, latentPreviewHandler), rawBody: rawBody))
  4990	      startProcessingIfNeeded()
  4991	    }
  4992	  }
