  5073	  /// Publish the current health-relevant state into the lock-based
  5074	  /// ``LiveHealthState`` so GET /health reads it without hopping onto this
  5075	  /// actor (which blocks for a whole render). Call at every state transition:
  5076	  /// job start/end, enqueue, model/LoRA change, pause, shutdown, startup (#217).
  5077	  ///
  5078	  /// `isRendering` keys off `activeJobId` (set the instant a job is dequeued,
  5079	  /// before the render method's first await sets `activeRenderStartedAt`), and
  5080	  /// the render start time falls back to `currentJobStartedAt` so the age/stale
  5081	  /// signal is correct throughout the synchronous GPU section.
  5082	  private func publishHealth() {
  5083	    let snap = HealthSnapshot(
  5084	      shuttingDown: shuttingDown,
  5085	      model: activePoolModelSpec ?? configuration.modelSpec ?? ZImageRepository.id,
  5086	      modelFamily: currentModelFamily.rawValue,
  5087	      modelVariant: currentModelFamily == .fibo ? "fibo" : (currentModelFamily == .flux1 ? zimageVariant.rawValue : detectedFlux2Model?.variant),
  5088	      loaded: pipelinePrepared,
  5089	      loras: activeLoRAs.map(LoRAState.init),
  5090	      renderCount: successfulRenderCount,
  5091	      failedRenderCount: failedRenderCount,
  5092	      pendingCount: pending.count,
  5093	      isRendering: activeJobId != nil,
  5094	      activeRenderStartedAt: activeRenderStartedAt ?? currentJobStartedAt,
  5095	      activeJobId: activeJobId,
  5096	      lastRenderDurationMs: lastRenderDurationMs,
  5097	      lastError: lastError,
  5098	      isPaused: isPaused,
  5099	      activeSummary: activeJobSummary,
  5100	      activeSource: activeJobSource,
  5101	      pending: pending.map { job in
  5102	        QueueJobInfo(
  5103	          id: job.id,
  5104	          kind: Self.kind(of: job.operation),
  5105	          summary: Self.describe(job.operation),
  5106	          source: job.source,
  5107	          enqueuedAt: job.enqueuedAt
  5108	        )
  5109	      },
  5110	      maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests
  5111	    )
  5112	    liveHealth.publish(snap)
  5113	  }
  5114	
  5115	  /// Mirror the recoverable slice of the queue (see QueuePersistence.swift)
  5116	  /// to disk so it survives a crash. Called at every mutation: enqueue,
  5117	  /// dequeue-into-active, job completion, cancel, reorder, clear. Cheap
  5118	  /// (small JSON, atomic write) relative to how rarely the queue actually
  5119	  /// changes compared to render duration.
  5120	  private func persistQueueState() {
  5121	    let active: PersistedQueueJob? = {
  5122	      guard let rawBody = activeJobRawBody,
  5123	            let kind = activeJobKindForPersistence,
  5124	            let id = activeJobId else { return nil }
  5125	      return PersistedQueueJob(
  5126	        id: id, kind: kind, source: activeJobSource ?? "api",
  5127	        enqueuedAt: currentJobStartedAt ?? Date(), rawBody: rawBody)
  5128	    }()
  5129	    let pendingJobs: [PersistedQueueJob] = pending.compactMap { job in
  5130	      guard let rawBody = job.rawBody else { return nil }
  5131	      return PersistedQueueJob(
  5132	        id: job.id, kind: Self.kind(of: job.operation), source: job.source,
  5133	        enqueuedAt: job.enqueuedAt, rawBody: rawBody)
  5134	    }
  5135	    QueueStateStore.save(PersistedQueueState(active: active, pending: pendingJobs))
  5136	  }

  5138	  /// Queue status for the ComfyUI bridge /queue endpoint.
  5139	  func queueStatus() -> ComfyBridgeQueueStatus {
  5140	    return ComfyBridgeQueueStatus(
  5141	      pendingCount: pending.count,
  5142	      maxPending: configuration.maxPendingRequests,

  5148	    )
  5149	  }

  5152	  /// Returns true if a render task was cancelled. Pending jobs are unaffected.
  5153	  func cancelActiveRender() -> Bool {
  5154	    guard let task = activeRenderTask else { return false }
  5155	    task.cancel()
  5156	    return true
  5157	  }

  7010	  case controlGenerate(ZImageControlGenerationRequest, ContinuationBox<GenerateResponse>)

  7014	  /// image renders on the shared GPU. The closure captures the generator+request.

  7019	private final class ContinuationBox<Value>: @unchecked Sendable {
  7020	  private let continuation: CheckedContinuation<Value, Error>
  7021	
  7022	  init(_ continuation: CheckedContinuation<Value, Error>) {
  7023	    self.continuation = continuation
  7024	  }
  7025	
  7026	  func resume(returning value: Value) {
  7027	    continuation.resume(returning: value)
  7028	  }
  7029	
  7030	  func resume(throwing error: Error) {
  7031	    continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  7032	  }
  7033	}
  7034	
  7035	private final class SyncResult<Value> {
  7036	  private let semaphore = DispatchSemaphore(value: 0)
  7037	  private let lock = NSLock()
  7038	  private var result: Result<Value, Error>?
  7039	
  7040	  func succeed(_ value: Value) {
     1	// QueuePersistence.swift — durable FIFO render queue.
     2	//
     3	// The render queue previously lived only in the WarmServerCoordinator
     4	// actor's memory: a crash (jetsam, the SIGTERM-mid-render shutdown bug,
     5	// a future bug) silently dropped every pending and in-flight job with no
     6	// trace. This persists a recoverable snapshot to disk on every queue
     7	// mutation so a restart can replay lost work.
     8	//
     9	// Only wire-driven job kinds with a raw JSON request body can be recovered
    10	// this way — "generate" (POST /v1/generate and /v1/generate/async, which
    11	// both funnel through WarmServerCoordinator.enqueueGenerate) and "lora_swap".
    12	// ControlNet generate closes over resolved temp-file paths built before the
    13	// queue is ever reached, and modelSwitch/localVideo close over live in-memory
    14	// state (a Swift closure, not data) — none of those can be serialized, so
    15	// they are simply never persisted. shutdown never needs recovery.
    16	
    17	import Foundation
    18	
    19	/// One recoverable queue entry: enough to replay the original HTTP request
    20	/// through the exact same decode path a live route handler uses.
    21	struct PersistedQueueJob: Codable, Sendable {
    22	  let id: String
    23	  let kind: String  // "generate" | "lora_swap"
    24	  let source: String
    25	  let enqueuedAt: Date
    26	  let rawBody: Data
    27	}
    28	
    29	/// Full on-disk queue snapshot: the job that was mid-render (if any, so a
    30	/// crash during a render doesn't lose it) plus everything still waiting
    31	/// behind it, in original order.
    32	struct PersistedQueueState: Codable, Sendable {
    33	  var active: PersistedQueueJob?
    34	  var pending: [PersistedQueueJob]
    35	}
    36	
    37	/// Reads/writes the queue snapshot at `~/.comfybox/queue-state.json`. Not
    38	/// actor-isolated itself — the only writer is the WarmServerCoordinator
    39	/// actor, which already serializes access to the queue it mirrors here.
    40	enum QueueStateStore {
    41	  static let path: URL = {
    42	    let dir = FileManager.default.homeDirectoryForCurrentUser
    43	      .appendingPathComponent(".comfybox", isDirectory: true)
    44	    try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
    45	    return dir.appendingPathComponent("queue-state.json")
    46	  }()
    47	
    48	  static func save(_ state: PersistedQueueState) {
    49	    guard state.active != nil || !state.pending.isEmpty else {
    50	      try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: path)
    51	      return
    52	    }
    53	    let encoder = JSONEncoder()
    54	    encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
    55	    guard let data = try? encoder.encode(state) else { return }
    56	    try? data.write(to: path, options: .atomic)
    57	  }
    58	
    59	  static func load() -> PersistedQueueState? {
    60	    guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: path) else { return nil }
    61	    let decoder = JSONDecoder()
    62	    decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
    63	    return try? decoder.decode(PersistedQueueState.self, from: data)
    64	  }
    65	}

  2368	  private func enhancePromptResponse(body: Data) async -> RoutedResponse {
  2369	    guard let req = try? decode(EnhanceRequest.self, from: body),
  2370	          !req.prompt.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty

  2380	    }

  2384	    var base = endpoint.baseUrl
  2385	    while base.hasSuffix("/") { base.removeLast() }

  2388

  2390	      configuration: PromptOptimizer.Configuration(
  2391	        ollamaBaseURL: base,
  2392	        lmStudioBaseURL: nil,
  2393	        model: endpoint.model,
  2394	        timeoutSeconds: 90,
  2395	        enabled: true
  2396	      ),
  2397	      logger: logger
  2398	    )
  2399

  2401	    // caller didn't supply one.

  2403	    var characterDescription = req.characterDescription
  2404	    if characterDescription == nil, let name = req.character,

  2411	      prompt: req.prompt,
  2412	      character: req.character,
  2413	      characterDescription: characterDescription,

  2754	        // For HuggingFace repos with multiple safetensors, specify the 8-step variant
  2755	        controlnetWeightsFile: resolvedControlnetWeights.contains("alibaba-pai")
  2756	          ? "Z-Image-Turbo-Fun-Controlnet-Union-2.1-8steps.safetensors" : nil,
  2757	        maxSequenceLength: configuration.maxSequenceLength,
  2758	        loras: controlLoRAs,
  2759	        progressCallback: progressCallback.map { callback in
  2760	          return { progress in
  2761	            if progress.stage == "Denoising" {
  2762	              callback(progress.stepIndex, progress.totalSteps)
  2763	            }
  2764	          }
  2765	        },
  2766	        enhancePrompt: false,
  2767	        enhanceMaxTokens: 512
  2768	      )
  2769	
  2770	      let start = Date()
  2771	      let result = try await coordinator.enqueueControlGenerate(controlRequest)
  2772	      let durationMs = Int(Date().timeIntervalSince(start) * 1000.0)
  2773	
  2774	      // Clean up temp files
  2775	      if let url = controlImageURL { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
  2776	      if let url = inpaintImageURL { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
  2777	      if let url = maskImageURL { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: url) }
  2778	
  2779	      return ComfyBridgeGenerateResult(
  2780	        outputPath: result.outputPath,
  2781	        durationMs: result.durationMs
  2782	      )
  2783	    }
  2784	
  2785	    // Family-aware defaults for step clamping, guidance, and negative prompts.
  2786	    let family = await coordinator.modelFamily
  2787	    let resolvedSteps: Int
  2788	    let resolvedGuidance: Float
  2789	    let resolvedNegativePrompt: String?
  2790	    let resolvedSampler: String?
  2791	
  2792	    switch family {
  2793	    case .krea2:
  2794	      // Krea-2-Turbo: 8-step distilled, no CFG. Clamp runaway KSampler defaults.
  2795	      resolvedSteps = request.steps > 0 ? min(request.steps, 12) : 9
  2796	      resolvedGuidance = 0.0
  2797	      resolvedNegativePrompt = nil
  2798	      resolvedSampler = request.sampler
  2799	    case .fibo:
  2800	      // FIBO: use model defaults, no step clamping
  2801	      resolvedSteps = request.steps
  2802	      resolvedGuidance = request.guidance > 0 ? request.guidance : 4.0
  2803	      resolvedNegativePrompt = request.negativePrompt
  2804	      resolvedSampler = request.sampler
  2805	    case .chroma:
  2806	      // Chroma: 28 steps default, guidance 0.0 (unconditioned)
  2807	      resolvedSteps = request.steps > 0 ? request.steps : 28
  2808	      resolvedGuidance = request.guidance
  2809	      resolvedNegativePrompt = nil
  2810	      resolvedSampler = request.sampler
  2811	    case .flux1:
  2812	      let zimageVariant = await coordinator.currentZImageVariant
  2813	      if zimageVariant == .base {
  2814	        // Z-Image Base / undistilled checkpoints (Moody, etc.): the ComfyUI/Krita
  2815	        // KSampler defaults are tuned for Turbo (9 steps, euler) and produce noise on
  2816	        // undistilled models. When the request still carries those turbo defaults, apply
  2817	        // the undistilled recommendations (40 steps, dpmpp_2m). If the user changed a
  2818	        // value, respect it. (Model-aware defaults from PR #164, @bree.)
  2819	        resolvedSteps = request.steps <= 9 ? 40 : request.steps
  2820	        resolvedGuidance = request.guidance > 0 ? request.guidance : ZImageModelMetadata.Base.recommendedGuidanceScale
  2821	        resolvedNegativePrompt = request.negativePrompt
  2822	        let sampler = request.sampler ?? "euler"
  2823	        resolvedSampler = sampler == "euler" ? "dpmpp_2m" : sampler
  2824	        if resolvedSteps != request.steps || resolvedSampler != request.sampler {
  2825	          logger.info("[WarmServer] Z-Image Base override: steps=\(resolvedSteps) (was \(request.steps)), sampler=\(resolvedSampler ?? "nil") (was \(request.sampler ?? "nil"))")
  2826	        }
  2827	      } else {
  2828	        // Z-Image Turbo: distilled, optimal at 9 steps. Honor the requested
  2829	        // guidance rather than hardcoding 0 — merged/finetuned "turbo"
  2830	        // checkpoints do respond to CFG, so forcing 0 removed real user control
  2831	        // (0 is the recommended default, passed through when the client sends it).
  2832	        resolvedSteps = min(request.steps, 9)
  2833	        resolvedGuidance = request.guidance
  2834	        resolvedNegativePrompt = nil
  2835	        resolvedSampler = request.sampler
  2836	      }
  2837	    case .flux2:
  2838	      // Base (non-distilled) models support guidance > 1.0 and default to 50 steps;
  2839	      // distilled models default to 4 steps and guidance 1.0.
  2840	      let isBaseModel = await coordinator.isFlux2BaseModel
  2841	      resolvedSteps = request.steps                 // Klein: no step clamp
  2842	      resolvedGuidance = isBaseModel ? request.guidance : 1.0
  2843	      resolvedNegativePrompt = nil                  // Klein: CFG only when guidance > 1.0
  2844	      resolvedSampler = request.sampler
  2845	    }
  2846	
  2847	    let payload = GeneratePayload(
  2848	      prompt: request.prompt,
  2849	      negativePrompt: resolvedNegativePrompt,
  2850	      width: genWidth,
  2851	      height: genHeight,
  2852	      steps: resolvedSteps,
  2853	      guidance: resolvedGuidance,
  2854	      seed: request.seed,
  2855	      outputPath: nil,
  2856	      levelsMin: request.levelsMin,
  2857	      levelsMax: request.levelsMax,
  2858	      scheduler: resolvedSampler,
  2859	      sigmaSchedule: request.sigmaSchedule,
  2860	      inpaintImageData: request.inpaintImageData,
  2861	      maskData: request.maskImageData,
  2862	      denoise: request.denoise,
  2863	      maskGrow: request.maskGrow,
  2864	      maskFeather: request.maskFeather,
  2865	      maskCropX: request.maskCropX,
  2866	      maskCropY: request.maskCropY
  2867	    )
  2868	
  2869	    // Convert bridge progress callback to pipeline progress handler.
  2870	    let pipelineProgress: (@Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void)? = progressCallback.map { callback in
  2871	      return { progress in
  2872	        if progress.stage == .denoising {
  2873	          callback(progress.stepIndex, progress.totalSteps)
  2874	        }
  2875	      }
  2876	    }
  2877	
  2878	    // Forward the latent preview callback directly — it uses the same
  2879	    // (MLXArray, Int, Int, Int, Int) signature as the pipeline handler.
  2880	    let pipelineLatentPreview: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler? = latentPreviewCallback
  2881	
  2882	    // Batch generation: if batchSize > 1 (from RepeatLatentBatch), loop and return last result.
  2883	    if request.batchSize > 1 {
  2884	      logger.info("WarmServer: batch generation — \(request.batchSize) images")
  2885	      var lastResult: ComfyBridgeGenerateResult?
  2886	      var totalDurationMs = 0
  2887	      for i in 0..<request.batchSize {
  2888	        // Vary seed per batch item for unique outputs.
  2889	        var batchPayload = payload
  2890	        if let baseSeed = request.seed {
  2891	          batchPayload = GeneratePayload(
  2892	            prompt: payload.prompt,
  2893	            negativePrompt: payload.negativePrompt,
  2894	            width: payload.width,
  2895	            height: payload.height,
  2896	            steps: payload.steps,
  2897	            guidance: payload.guidance,
  2898	            seed: baseSeed + UInt64(i),
  2899	            outputPath: payload.outputPath,
  2900	            levelsMin: payload.levelsMin,
  3420	      let type: String
  3421	      let durationS: Double?
  3422	    }
  3423	    struct OutputSpec: Decodable {
  3424	      let width: Int?
  3425	      let height: Int?
  3426	      let fps: Int?
  3427	      let path: String?
  3428	    }
  3429	    let shots: [ShotSpec]
  3430	    let transitions: [TransitionSpec]?
  3431	    let output: OutputSpec?
  3432	    let loras: [LoRAEntry]?
  3433	    let source: String?
  3434	  }
  3435	
  3436	  private func storyboardSpec(from payload: StoryboardPayload) throws -> StoryboardSpec {
  3437	    var transitions: [MontageTransition] = []
  3438	    for t in payload.transitions ?? [] {
  3439	      guard let kind = MontageTransition.Kind(rawValue: t.type) else {
  3440	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3441	          message: "transitions[].type must be cut|fade|dissolve (got '\(t.type)')")
  3442	      }
  3443	      transitions.append(MontageTransition(kind: kind, durationS: t.durationS ?? 0.5))
  3444	    }
  3445	    let shots = payload.shots.map { s in
  3446	      StoryboardSpec.Shot(
  3447	        prompt: s.prompt,
  3448	        durationS: s.durationS,
  3449	        anchorImage: s.anchorImage,
  3450	        insert: s.insert.map { ins in
  3451	          StoryboardSpec.Insert(
  3452	            prompt: ins.prompt,
  3453	            creativity: ins.creativity ?? 0.35,
  3454	            negativePrompt: ins.negativePrompt,
  3455	            maskPath: ins.maskPath,
  3456	            maskRegion: ins.maskRegion,
  3457	            maskInvert: ins.maskInvert ?? false,
  3458	            maskGrow: ins.maskGrow ?? 0,
  3459	            maskFeather: ins.maskFeather ?? 0,
  3460	            seed: ins.seed)
  3461	        },
  3462	        negativePrompt: s.negativePrompt,
  3463	        seed: s.seed)
  3464	    }
  3465	    return StoryboardSpec(
  3466	      shots: shots,
  3467	      transitions: transitions,
  3468	      output: StoryboardSpec.Output(
  3469	        width: payload.output?.width ?? 640,
  3470	        height: payload.output?.height ?? 640,
  3471	        fps: payload.output?.fps ?? 24,
  3472	        path: payload.output?.path),
  3473	      loras: (payload.loras ?? []).map { ($0.path, $0.scale ?? 1.0) })
  3474	  }
  3475	
  3476	  /// Execute a storyboard: per-shot [i2i insert →] i2v render, chained on the
  3477	  /// previous shot's extracted last frame, then montage assembly. Runs OUTSIDE
  3478	  /// the GPU queue (via submitOrchestrated) and takes a normal queue turn for
  3479	  /// each render, so other jobs interleave between shots. Intermediates are
  3480	  /// named storyboard-* (NOT ltx2-*) so the daemon's orphan reconciler ignores
  3481	  /// them.
  3482	  private func runStoryboard(
  3483	    spec: StoryboardSpec,
  3484	    source: String,
  3485	    report: @escaping @Sendable (Int) -> Void
  3486	  ) async throws -> LTX2VideoResult {
  3487	    let started = Date()
  3488	    let session = UUID().uuidString.prefix(8)
  3489	    let dir = configuration.allowedOutputDirectory
  3490	    var anchor: String? = nil
  3491	    var clips: [String] = []
  3492	    // Assembly gets the final ~4%; shots split the rest evenly.
  3493	    let shotWeight = 96.0 / Double(spec.shots.count)
  3494	
  3495	    for (i, shot) in spec.shots.enumerated() {
  3496	      var shotAnchor: String
  3497	      if let explicit = shot.anchorImage, !explicit.isEmpty {
  3498	        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: explicit) else {
  3499	          throw StoryboardError.anchorNotFound(shot: i, path: explicit)
  3500	        }
  3501	        shotAnchor = explicit
  3502	      } else if let chained = anchor {
  3503	        shotAnchor = chained
  3504	      } else {
  3505	        throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "anchor", message: "no previous last frame to chain from")
  3506	      }
  3507	
  3508	      // Optional i2i insert on the anchor (e.g. add an element i2v can't
  3509	      // invent) — uses the #239 selective-inpainting path.
  3510	      if let insert = shot.insert {
  3511	        logger.info("Storyboard[\(session)] shot \(i): i2i insert (creativity \(insert.creativity))")
  3512	        let insertOut = (dir as NSString)
  3513	          .appendingPathComponent("storyboard-\(session)-shot\(i)-insert.png")
  3514	        let payload = GeneratePayload(
  3515	          prompt: insert.prompt,
  3516	          negativePrompt: insert.negativePrompt,
  3517	          width: spec.output.width,
  3518	          height: spec.output.height,
  3519	          seed: insert.seed,
  3520	          outputPath: insertOut,
  3521	          maskGrow: insert.maskGrow,
  3522	          maskFeather: insert.maskFeather,
  3523	          imagePath: shotAnchor,
  3524	          creativity: Float(insert.creativity),
  3525	          maskPath: insert.maskPath,
  3526	          maskRegion: insert.maskRegion,
  3527	          maskInvert: insert.maskInvert,
  3528	          source: source)
  3529	        do {
  3530	          let generated = try await coordinator.enqueueGenerate(payload, source: source)
  3531	          guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: generated.outputPath) else {
  3532	            throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "insert", message: "no output produced")
  3533	          }
  3534	          shotAnchor = generated.outputPath
  3535	        } catch let error as StoryboardError {
  3536	          throw error
  3537	        } catch {
  3538	          throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "insert", message: error.localizedDescription)
  3539	        }
  3540	      }
  3541	
  3542	      // i2v render for this shot, through the same preparation the video
  3543	      // routes use (dims snapping, presets, LoRA resolution, validation).
  3544	      logger.info("Storyboard[\(session)] shot \(i)/\(spec.shots.count): i2v from \((shotAnchor as NSString).lastPathComponent)")
  3545	      var body: [String: Any] = [
  3546	        "prompt": shot.prompt,
  3547	        "image_path": shotAnchor,
  3548	        "width": spec.output.width,
  3549	        "height": spec.output.height,
  3550	        "fps": spec.output.fps,
  3551	        "output_path": "storyboard-\(session)-shot\(i).mp4",
  3552	        "source": source,
  3553	      ]
  3554	      if let d = shot.durationS { body["duration"] = d }
  3555	      if let n = shot.negativePrompt { body["negative_prompt"] = n }
  3556	      if let s = shot.seed { body["seed"] = s }
  3557	      if !spec.loras.isEmpty {
  3558	        body["loras"] = spec.loras.map { ["path": $0.path, "scale": $0.scale] as [String: Any] }
  3559	      }
  3560	      let shotResult: LTX2VideoResult
  3561	      do {
  3562	        let bodyData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
  3563	        guard let prep = try await prepareLocalVideo(body: bodyData) else {
  3564	          throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "i2v", message: "local LTX-2 not configured")
  3565	        }

  2937	  /// Whether the given upscale model name should be routed to ESRGAN.
  2938	  private static func isESRGANModel(_ modelName: String) -> Bool {
  2939	    esrganModelPatterns.contains { modelName.hasPrefix($0) || modelName.contains($0) }
  2940	  }
  2941	
  2942	  /// Bridge a ComfyUI upscale workflow request to the appropriate upscale pipeline.
  2943	  /// Routes to ESRGANPipeline for ESRGAN-family models, SeedVR2Pipeline for SeedVR2.
  2944	  /// Both pipelines are lazy-loaded on first use to avoid startup memory costs.
  2945	  private func bridgeUpscale(
  2946	    imageData: Data,
  2947	    modelName: String,
  2948	    progressCallback: ComfyBridgeProgressHandler?
  2949	  ) async throws -> ComfyBridgeGenerateResult {
  2950	    if Self.isESRGANModel(modelName) {
  2951	      return try await bridgeUpscaleESRGAN(imageData: imageData, modelName: modelName)
  2952	    } else {
  2953	      return try await bridgeUpscaleSeedVR2(imageData: imageData, modelName: modelName, progressCallback: progressCallback)
  2954	    }
  2955	  }
  2956	
  2957	  /// ESRGAN upscale path. Lazy-loads the ESRGANPipeline on first use.
  2958	  /// Weights are resolved from ~/bin/zimage/upscale_models/<modelName>/.
  2959	  private func bridgeUpscaleESRGAN(
  2960	    imageData: Data,
  2961	    modelName: String
  2962	  ) async throws -> ComfyBridgeGenerateResult {
  2963	    // Resolve weights directory: ~/bin/zimage/upscale_models/<modelName>/
  2964	    // Strip file extension if present (e.g. "4x-UltraSharp.pth" -> "4x-UltraSharp")
  2965	    let baseName: String
  2966	    if let dotIndex = modelName.lastIndex(of: ".") {
  2967	      baseName = String(modelName[modelName.startIndex..<dotIndex])
  2968	    } else {
  2969	      baseName = modelName
  2970	    }
  2971	    let weightsDir = URL(fileURLWithPath: Self.upscaleModelsDirectoryPath)
  2972	      .appendingPathComponent(baseName)
  2973	
  2974	    // Lazy-load ESRGAN pipeline (re-create if model changed)
  2975	    let pipeline = try loadESRGANPipelineIfNeeded(weightsDirectory: weightsDir)
  2976	
  2977	    // Write input image data to a temp file.
  2978	    let inputTempPath = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-esrgan-input-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  2979	    try imageData.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: inputTempPath))
  2980	    logger.info("WarmServer: wrote ESRGAN input to \(inputTempPath) (\(imageData.count) bytes)")
  2981	
  2982	    let outputTempPath = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-esrgan-output-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  2983	
  2984	    let start = Date()
  2985	    do {
  2986	      let outputPath = try pipeline.upscaleAndSave(
  2987	        imagePath: inputTempPath,
  2988	        outputPath: outputTempPath
  2989	      )
  2990	      let durationMs = Int(Date().timeIntervalSince(start) * 1000.0)
  2991	      try? FileManager.default.removeItem(atPath: inputTempPath)
  2992	      logger.info("WarmServer: ESRGAN upscale complete — \(durationMs)ms, output=\(outputPath)")
  2993	      return ComfyBridgeGenerateResult(outputPath: outputPath, durationMs: durationMs)
  2994	    } catch {
  2995	      try? FileManager.default.removeItem(atPath: inputTempPath)
  2996	      try? FileManager.default.removeItem(atPath: outputTempPath)
  2997	      throw error
  2998	    }
  2999	  }

  3039	  /// Get or lazily create the SeedVR2 pipeline. Double-checked under
  3040	  /// `upscalePipelineLock` so concurrent first-use requests cannot
  3041	  /// double-load the ~6GB weights.
  3042	  private func loadSeedVR2PipelineIfNeeded(weightsPath: String) throws -> SeedVR2Pipeline {
  3043	    upscalePipelineLock.lock()
  3044	    defer { upscalePipelineLock.unlock() }
  3045	
  3046	    if let pipeline = seedvr2Pipeline {
  3047	      return pipeline
  3048	    }
  3049	
  3050	    logger.info("WarmServer: lazy-loading SeedVR2 pipeline from \(weightsPath)...")
  3051	    let pipeline = try SeedVR2Pipeline(weightsPath: weightsPath, logger: logger)
  3052	    seedvr2Pipeline = pipeline
  3053	    logger.info("WarmServer: SeedVR2 pipeline ready (\(pipeline.modelConfig == .preset7B ? "7B" : "3B"))")
  3054	    return pipeline
  3055	  }
  3056	
  3057	  /// Get or lazily create the ESRGAN pipeline for the given weights directory,
  3058	  /// re-creating it when the requested model changes. Serialized under
  3059	  /// `upscalePipelineLock` like SeedVR2 to prevent concurrent double-loads.
  3060	  private func loadESRGANPipelineIfNeeded(weightsDirectory weightsDir: URL) throws -> ESRGANPipeline {
  3061	    upscalePipelineLock.lock()
  3062	    defer { upscalePipelineLock.unlock() }
  3063	
  3064	    if let pipeline = esrganPipeline, pipeline.weightsDirectory.path == weightsDir.path {
  3065	      return pipeline
  3066	    }
  3067	
  3068	    logger.info("WarmServer: lazy-loading ESRGAN pipeline from \(weightsDir.path)...")
  3069	    let pipeline = try ESRGANPipeline(weightsDirectory: weightsDir, logger: logger)
  3070	    esrganPipeline = pipeline
  3071	    logger.info("WarmServer: ESRGAN pipeline ready (scale=\(pipeline.config.scale)x, blocks=\(pipeline.config.numBlock))")
  3072	    return pipeline
  3073	  }

  3075	  // MARK: - Upscale Handler
  3076	
  3077	  /// Handle a direct upscale request via the REST API.
  3078	  /// Lazy-loads the SeedVR2 pipeline on first call.
  3079	  private func handleUpscale(_ payload: UpscalePayload) async throws -> UpscaleResponse {
  3080	    guard let weightsPath = seedvr2WeightsPath else {
  3081	      throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3082	        message: "SeedVR2 upscale not available: no weights path configured"
  3083	      )
  3084	    }
  3085	
  3086	    // Validate input file exists
  3087	    guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: payload.imagePath) else {
  3088	      throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3089	        message: "Input image not found: \(payload.imagePath)"
  3090	      )
  3091	    }
  3092	
  3093	    let targetResolution = payload.targetResolution ?? 1024
  3094	    let softness = payload.softness ?? 0.0
  3095	
  3096	    // Resolution guard
  3097	    if let error = UpscalePayload.validateResolution(targetResolution) {
  3098	      throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: error)
  3099	    }
  3100	
  3101	    // Validate softness range
  3102	    if let error = UpscalePayload.validateSoftness(softness) {
  3103	      throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: error)
  3104	    }
  3105	
  3106	    // Model variant validation
  3107	    if let error = UpscalePayload.validateModel(payload.model) {
  3108	      throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: error)
  3109	    }
  3110	
  3111	    // Lazy-load pipeline
  3112	    let pipeline = try loadSeedVR2PipelineIfNeeded(weightsPath: weightsPath)
  3113	
  3114	    // Check model variant matches request
  3115	    if let requestedModel = payload.model {

  3117	      let requested7B = requestedModel == "seedvr2-7b"

  3119	        let loaded = is7B ? "seedvr2-7b" : "seedvr2-3b"

  3121	          message: "Requested \(requestedModel) but loaded weights are \(loaded)"
  3122	        )
  3123	      }

  3134	      resolvedOutputPath = try WarmServerOutputPathValidator
  3135	        .resolveOutputPath(op, allowedOutputDirectory: configuration.allowedOutputDirectory)
  3136	        .path

  3145	      seed: payload.seed,
  3558	        body["loras"] = spec.loras.map { ["path": $0.path, "scale": $0.scale] as [String: Any] }
  3559	      }

  3562	        let bodyData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
  3563	        guard let prep = try await prepareLocalVideo(body: bodyData) else {
  3564	          throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "i2v", message: "local LTX-2 not configured")
  3565	        }
  3566	        let base = Double(i) * shotWeight
  3567	        shotResult = try await coordinator.enqueueLocalVideo { coordReport in

  3569	            let pct = Self.localVideoProgressPercent(
  3570	              chunk: chunk, totalChunks: totalChunks, step: step, totalSteps: totalSteps)

  3572	            report(Int(base + Double(pct) * shotWeight / 100.0))
  3573	          }
  3574	        }
  3575	      } catch let error as StoryboardError {
  3576	        throw error
  3577	      } catch {
  3578	        throw StoryboardError.shotFailed(shot: i, stage: "i2v", message: error.localizedDescription)
  3579	      }
  3580	      clips.append(shotResult.outputPath)
  3581

  3583	      if i < spec.shots.count - 1 {

  3588	        } catch {

  3590	        }

  3592	    }

  3597	      ? requestedOutput
  3598	      : (dir as NSString).appendingPathComponent(requestedOutput)

  3600	      requested, allowedOutputDirectory: dir).path

  3602	    let montage = try MontageComposer.compose(

  3604	      transitions: spec.transitions,

  3622	  /// Wire response for POST /v1/montage/compose.
  3623	  struct MontageResponse: Encodable {
  3624	    let outputPath: String
  3625	    let durationS: Double
  3626	    let width: Int
  3627	    let height: Int
  3628	    let segmentCount: Int
  3629	    let frameCount: Int
  3630	
  3631	    enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey {
  3632	      case outputPath = "output_path"
  3633	      case durationS = "duration_s"
  3634	      case width, height
  3635	      case segmentCount = "segment_count"
  3636	      case frameCount = "frame_count"
  3637	    }
  3638	  }
  3639	
  3640	  /// Wire payload for POST /v1/montage/compose (snake_case; see the FDD).
  3641	  struct MontagePayload: Decodable {
  3642	    struct Segment: Decodable {
  3643	      struct KenBurnsSpec: Decodable {
  3644	        /// [startScale, endScale]
  3645	        let zoom: [Double]?
  3646	        /// [[x0,y0],[x1,y1]] normalized output-unit offsets
  3647	        let pan: [[Double]]?
  3648	      }
  3649	      let type: String
  3650	      let path: String
  3651	      let durationS: Double?
  3652	      let kenburns: KenBurnsSpec?
  3653	    }
  3654	    struct Transition: Decodable {
  3655	      let type: String
  3656	      let durationS: Double?
  3657	    }
  3658	    struct Output: Decodable {
  3659	      let width: Int?
  3660	      let height: Int?
  3661	      let fps: Int?
  3662	      let path: String?
  3663	    }
  3664	    let segments: [Segment]
  3665	    let transitions: [Transition]?
  3666	    let output: Output?
  3667	    let aspectPolicy: String?
  3668	  }
  3669	
  3670	  private func composeMontage(_ payload: MontagePayload) async throws -> MontageResult {
  3671	    var segments: [MontageSegment] = []
  3672	    for (i, s) in payload.segments.enumerated() {
  3673	      guard let kind = MontageSegment.Kind(rawValue: s.type) else {
  3674	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3675	          message: "segments[\(i)].type must be image|clip (got '\(s.type)')")
  3676	      }
  3677	      var kenBurns: MontageSegment.KenBurns? = nil
  3678	      if let spec = s.kenburns {
  3679	        var kb = MontageSegment.KenBurns()
  3680	        if let zoom = spec.zoom {
  3681	          guard zoom.count == 2 else {
  3682	            throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3683	              message: "segments[\(i)].kenburns.zoom must be [start, end]")
  3684	          }
  3685	          kb.zoomStart = zoom[0]
  3686	          kb.zoomEnd = zoom[1]
  3687	        }
  3688	        if let pan = spec.pan {
  3689	          guard pan.count == 2, pan[0].count == 2, pan[1].count == 2 else {
  3690	            throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3691	              message: "segments[\(i)].kenburns.pan must be [[x0,y0],[x1,y1]]")
  3692	          }
  3693	          kb.panStart = (pan[0][0], pan[0][1])
  3694	          kb.panEnd = (pan[1][0], pan[1][1])
  3695	        }
  3696	        kenBurns = kb
  3697	      }
  3698	      segments.append(MontageSegment(kind: kind, path: s.path, durationS: s.durationS, kenBurns: kenBurns))
  3699	    }
  3700	
  3701	    var transitions: [MontageTransition] = []
  3702	    for t in payload.transitions ?? [] {
  3703	      guard let kind = MontageTransition.Kind(rawValue: t.type) else {
  3704	        throw WarmServerError.invalidRequest(
  3705	          message: "transitions[].type must be cut|fade|dissolve (got '\(t.type)')")
  3760	    // temp file so remote clients don't need a pre-existing server path.
  3761	    if let initData = payload.initImageData, payload.imagePath == nil {
  3762	      let tempPath = NSTemporaryDirectory() + "zimage-init-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  3763	      try initData.write(to: URL(fileURLWithPath: tempPath))
  3764	      payload.imagePath = tempPath
  3765	    }
  3766	    try payload.validateOutputPath(configuration: configuration)
  3767	    return payload
  3768	  }
  3769	
  3770	  /// Replay any queue jobs left over from before a crash (see
  3771	  /// QueuePersistence.swift) — the "active" slot (if any) first, since it
  3772	  /// was originally at the front, then everything still pending, in order.
  3773	  /// Each job is decoded through the exact same path its live route handler
  3774	  /// uses, then re-enqueued with no caller to respond to (fire-and-forget —
  3775	  /// the original HTTP connection is long gone by the time a crashed process
  3776	  /// restarts). Runs as a detached background task so a large recovered
  3777	  /// queue never delays the listener from coming up.
  3778	  private func recoverPersistedQueue() {
  3779	    guard let state = QueueStateStore.load() else { return }
  3780	    let jobs = (state.active.map { [$0] } ?? []) + state.pending
  3781	    guard !jobs.isEmpty else { return }
  3782	    logger.info("Queue recovery: replaying \(jobs.count) job(s) left over from before a restart")
  3783	
  3784	    Task {
  3785	      for job in jobs {
  3786	        do {
  3787	          switch job.kind {
  3788	          case "generate":
  3789	            let payload = try decodedGeneratePayload(from: job.rawBody)
  3790	            _ = try await coordinator.enqueueGenerate(payload, source: job.source, rawBody: job.rawBody)
  3791	            logger.info("Queue recovery: completed generate job \(job.id)")
  3792	          case "lora_swap":
  3793	            let payload = stageNearlineLoras(in: try decode(LoRASwapPayload.self, from: job.rawBody))
  3794	            _ = try await coordinator.enqueueSwap(payload, rawBody: job.rawBody)
  3795	            logger.info("Queue recovery: completed lora_swap job \(job.id)")
  3796	          default:
  3797	            logger.warning("Queue recovery: unknown job kind '\(job.kind)' for \(job.id), skipping")
  3798	          }
  3799	        } catch {
  3800	          logger.error("Queue recovery: job \(job.id) (\(job.kind)) failed — \(error.localizedDescription)")
  3801	        }
  3802	      }
  3803	    }
  3804	  }
  3805	
  3806	  private func response(for error: Error) -> HTTPResponse {

  3814	      case .cancelled:
  3815	        return .error(status: 409, message: "Request cancelled (queue cleared)")
  3816	      }
  3817

  3819	      switch error {
  3820	      case .invalidDimensions(let message):

     3	// Shells out to the local faceswap venv (~/Projects/faceswap), like mflux /
     4	// Découpage. Swaps the source face onto the target image. Desktop-only — the
     5	// ComfyBox server never depends on it. Arg building is pure and unit-tested.
     6	
     7	import Foundation
     8	
     9	@Observable
    10	@MainActor
    11	public final class FaceSwapService {
    12	    public var projectDirectory: String
    13	
    14	    public private(set) var isRunning = false
    15	    public private(set) var consoleLog = ""
    16	    private var process: Process?
    17	
    18	    public init(projectDirectory: String = FaceSwapService.defaultProjectDirectory) {
    19	        self.projectDirectory = projectDirectory
    20	    }
    21	
    22	    public nonisolated static var defaultProjectDirectory: String {
    23	        NSString(string: "~/Projects/faceswap").expandingTildeInPath
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public var pythonPath: String { (projectDirectory as NSString).appendingPathComponent(".venv/bin/python") }
    27	    public var scriptPath: String { (projectDirectory as NSString).appendingPathComponent("swap.py") }
    28	    public var modelPath: String { (projectDirectory as NSString).appendingPathComponent("models/inswapper_128.onnx") }
    29	
    30	    /// Backend is ready only when the venv, script, and model are all present.
    31	    public var isInstalled: Bool {
    32	        let fm = FileManager.default
    33	        return fm.isExecutableFile(atPath: pythonPath)
    34	            && fm.fileExists(atPath: scriptPath)
    35	            && fm.fileExists(atPath: modelPath)
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    // MARK: - Pure args (tested)
    39	
    40	    public nonisolated static func swapArgs(script: String, source: String, target: String,
    41	                                            output: String, allFaces: Bool) -> [String] {
    42	        var args = [script, source, target, output]
    43	        if allFaces { args.append("--all") }
    44	        return args
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    // MARK: - Execution
    48	
    49	    public enum FaceSwapError: Error, LocalizedError {
    50	        case notInstalled(String), alreadyRunning, failed(Int32, String)
    51	        public var errorDescription: String? {
    52	            switch self {
    53	            case .notInstalled(let d): return "Face-swap backend not installed at \(d). Run the setup (insightface + inswapper_128.onnx)."
    54	            case .alreadyRunning: return "A face swap is already running."
    55	            case .failed(let c, let out):
    56	                let msg = out.split(separator: "\n").last.map(String.init) ?? ""
    57	                return "Face swap failed (\(c)): \(msg)"
    58	            }
    59	        }
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    /// Swap `source`'s face onto `target`, writing `output`. Returns the output path.
    63	    @discardableResult
    64	    public func swap(source: String, target: String, output: String, allFaces: Bool) async throws -> String {
    65	        guard !isRunning else { throw FaceSwapError.alreadyRunning }
    66	        guard isInstalled else { throw FaceSwapError.notInstalled(projectDirectory) }
    67	        consoleLog = ""
    68	        isRunning = true
    69	        defer { isRunning = false; process = nil }
    70	
    71	        let args = Self.swapArgs(script: scriptPath, source: source, target: target, output: output, allFaces: allFaces)
    72	        let proc = Process()
    73	        proc.executableURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: pythonPath)
    74	        proc.arguments = args
    75	        proc.currentDirectoryURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: projectDirectory)
    76	        let pipe = Pipe()
    77	        proc.standardOutput = pipe
    78	        proc.standardError = pipe
    79	        process = proc
    80	        append("$ python swap.py \(source) \(target) \(output)\(allFaces ? " --all" : "")\n")
    81	
    82	        let handle = pipe.fileHandleForReading
    83	        let stream = AsyncStream<String> { continuation in
    84	            handle.readabilityHandler = { fh in
    85	                let data = fh.availableData
    86	                if data.isEmpty { continuation.finish() }
    87	                else if let t = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) { continuation.yield(t) }
    88	            }
    89	        }
    90	        try proc.run()
    91	        for await chunk in stream { append(chunk) }
    92	        proc.waitUntilExit()
    93	        handle.readabilityHandler = nil
    94	        guard proc.terminationStatus == 0 else {
    95	            throw FaceSwapError.failed(proc.terminationStatus, consoleLog)
    96	        }
    97	        return output
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    public func cancel() { process?.terminate() }
   101	
   102	    private func append(_ text: String) {
   103	        consoleLog += text
   104	        if consoleLog.count > 100_000 { consoleLog = String(consoleLog.suffix(80_000)) }
   105	    }
   106	}
   150	                }
   151	            }
   152	            consoleTail
   153	        }
   154	        .padding(12)
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    // MARK: - Face swap (pending backend)
   158	
   159	    private var swapSection: some View {
   160	        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
   161	            if !faceSwap.isInstalled {
   162	                Label("Face-swap backend not installed (insightface + inswapper_128.onnx at ~/Projects/faceswap).",
   163	                      systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle")
   164	                    .font(.caption).foregroundStyle(.orange)
   165	            }
   166	            Text("Replace the face in a target image with a source face (insightface + inswapper). Runs locally.")
   167	                .font(.caption).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   168	
   169	            HStack(spacing: 16) {
   170	                swapImageWell("Source face", path: $swapSource, thumb: $swapSourceThumb)
   171	                Image(systemName: "arrow.right").foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   172	                swapImageWell("Target image", path: $swapTarget, thumb: $swapTargetThumb)
   173	            }
   174	            Toggle("Swap all faces in target", isOn: $swapAllFaces)
   175	            if faceSwap.isRunning {
   176	                HStack(spacing: 6) { ProgressView().controlSize(.small); Text("Swapping…").font(.caption) }
   177	            }
   178	            Button { Task { await runSwap() } } label: {
   179	                Label("Swap Face", systemImage: "arrow.triangle.2.circlepath").frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
   180	            }
   181	            .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
   182	            .disabled(faceSwap.isRunning || !faceSwap.isInstalled || swapSource.isEmpty || swapTarget.isEmpty)
   183	
   184	            if let swapStatus {
   185	                Label(swapStatus, systemImage: swapIsError ? "exclamationmark.triangle.fill" : "checkmark.circle.fill")
   186	                    .font(.caption).foregroundStyle(swapIsError ? .orange : .green)
   187	            }
   188	            if let swapResultURL {
   189	                AsyncImage(url: swapResultURL) { phase in
   190	                    if case .success(let img) = phase {
   191	                        img.resizable().scaledToFit().frame(maxHeight: 320)
   192	                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
   193	                    }
   194	                }
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	        .padding(12)
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    private func swapImageWell(_ title: String, path: Binding<String>, thumb: Binding<NSImage?>) -> some View {
   201	        VStack(spacing: 4) {
   202	            Button {
   203	                let panel = NSOpenPanel()
   204	                panel.canChooseFiles = true; panel.allowsMultipleSelection = false
   205	                panel.allowedContentTypes = [.png, .jpeg, .image]
   206	                if panel.runModal() == .OK, let url = panel.url {
   207	                    path.wrappedValue = url.path
   208	                    Task { let i = await Task.detached { NSImage(contentsOfFile: url.path) }.value
   209	                        await MainActor.run { thumb.wrappedValue = i } }
   210	                }
   211	            } label: {
   212	                Group {
   213	                    if let t = thumb.wrappedValue {
   214	                        Image(nsImage: t).resizable().aspectRatio(contentMode: .fill)
   215	                    } else {
   216	                        Image(systemName: "photo.badge.plus").font(.title2).foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
   217	                    }
   218	                }
   219	                .frame(width: 96, height: 96)
   220	                .background(Color(nsColor: .controlBackgroundColor), in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
   221	                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 8))
   222	            }
   223	            .buttonStyle(.plain)
   224	            Text(title).font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   225	        }
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    private func runSwap() async {
   229	        swapStatus = nil; swapResultURL = nil
   230	        let dir = DesktopSettings.load().outputDirectory
   231	        let output = (dir as NSString).appendingPathComponent("faceswap-\(Int(Date().timeIntervalSince1970)).png")
   232	        do {
   233	            _ = try await faceSwap.swap(source: swapSource, target: swapTarget, output: output, allFaces: swapAllFaces)
   234	            swapResultURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: output)
   235	            try? await ingestor?.ingestFile(at: output)
   236	            swapStatus = "Swapped → \(URL(fileURLWithPath: output).lastPathComponent)"
   237	            swapIsError = false
   238	        } catch {
   239	            swapStatus = error.localizedDescription; swapIsError = true
   240	        }
   241	    }

codex
The lease hook is technically possible only after the current `eval(currentLatents)`—that call materializes the MLX graph before the callback—but the existing callback is synchronous and the coordinator actor is deliberately blocked for the render. A lease implemented as another coordinator method would itself wait until the render ends. The design needs a lock-based, non-actor gate and explicit phase state; otherwise the advertised endpoint cannot reach the “pause point.”

  1295	        do {

  1305	            list.activeSummary = dict["active_summary"] as? String

     1	// QueuePanel.swift — Render queue status panel
     2	//
     3	// Shows the current render queue status from the WarmServer health endpoint.
     4	// Displays active render progress, pending queue count, completed render
     5	// statistics, and server resource usage. Auto-refreshes via EngineService
     6	// health polling (every 3 seconds).
     7	
     8	import SwiftUI
     9	
    10	struct QueuePanel: View {
    11	    @Bindable var engine: EngineService
    12	
    13	    @State private var jobs: EngineService.QueueJobList?
    14	    @State private var actionError: String?
    15	
    16	    var body: some View {
    17	        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
    18	            // Header
    19	            HStack {
    20	                Text("Render Queue")
    21	                    .font(.subheadline)
    22	                    .fontWeight(.semibold)
    23	                Spacer()
    24	                if engine.connectionState.isConnected {
    25	                    statusIndicator
    26	                }
    27	            }
    28	
    29	            if let info = engine.queueInfo {
    30	                queueContent(info)
    31	                pendingJobsSection
    32	            } else if engine.connectionState.isConnected {
    33	                Text("Loading queue status...")
    34	                    .font(.caption)
    35	                    .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
    36	            } else {
    37	                Text("Connect to server to view queue")
    38	                    .font(.caption)
    39	                    .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
    40	            }
    41	
    42	            if let actionError {
    43	                Text(actionError)
    44	                    .font(.caption2)
    45	                    .foregroundStyle(.red)
    46	                    .lineLimit(2)
    47	            }
    48	        }
    49	        // The health poll updates queueInfo every few seconds; piggyback on it
    50	        // to refresh the detailed job list only while something is queued or
    51	        // rendering (avoids constant polling when idle).
    52	        .task(id: pollKey) { await reloadJobs() }
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    /// Changes whenever the coarse queue state moves, retriggering the job fetch.
    56	    private var pollKey: String {
    57	        let info = engine.queueInfo
    58	        return "\(info?.pendingCount ?? 0)-\(info?.isRendering ?? false)-\(info?.currentJobId ?? "")-\(Int(info?.progressPercent ?? 0))"
    59	    }
    60	
    61	    private func reloadJobs() async {
    62	        guard engine.connectionState.isConnected else { jobs = nil; return }
    63	        jobs = await engine.fetchQueueJobs()
    64	    }
    65

   210	    private func activeRenderSection(_ info: QueueInfo) -> some View {
   211	        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
   212	            HStack {
   213	                Text("Status")
   214	                    .font(.caption)
   215	                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)

   222	                                .controlSize(.small)

   225	                                .font(.caption)
   226	                                .monospacedDigit()
   227	                                .foregroundStyle(.orange)
   228	                        }
   229	                    } else {
   230	                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
   231	                            ProgressView()
   232	                                .controlSize(.mini)
   233	                            Text("Rendering...")
   234	                                .font(.caption)
   235	                                .foregroundStyle(.orange)
   236	                        }
   237	                    }
   238	                } else {
   239	                    Text("Ready")
   240	                        .font(.caption)
   241	                        .foregroundStyle(.green)
   242	                }
   243	            }
   244	
   245	            // Current job id
   246	            if info.isRendering, let jobId = info.currentJobId {
   247	                HStack {
   248	                    Text("Job")
   249	                        .font(.caption)
   250	                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   251	                    Spacer()
   252	                    Text(jobId)
   253	                        .font(.caption)
   254	                        .monospaced()
   255	                        .lineLimit(1)
   256	                        .truncationMode(.middle)
   257	                }
   258	            }
   259	
   260	            // Pending count
   261	            if info.pendingCount > 0 {
   262	                HStack {
   263	                    Text("Pending")
   264	                        .font(.caption)
   265	                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
   266	                    Spacer()
   267	                    Text("\(info.pendingCount) job\(info.pendingCount == 1 ? "" : "s")")
   268	                        .font(.caption)
   269	                        .foregroundStyle(.orange)
   270	                }
   271	            }
   272	        }
   273	    }

   120	/// `GET /v1/kira/content-scheduler/status` (A2).
   121	public struct KiraSchedulerStatus: Equatable, Sendable {
   122	    public var paused: Bool
   123	    public var enabled: Bool
   124	    public var intervalMinutes: Int?
   125	    public var imageCount: Int?
   126	    public var videoCount: Int?
   127	    public var videoMode: String?
   128	    /// Unlimited-within-cycle images: renders chain until the cycle window
   129	    /// closes; imageCount is ignored while true.
   130	    public var unlimitedImages: Bool
   131	    /// Content-creation window "HH:MM" (server-local; start > end wraps
   132	    /// midnight). nil = 24/7 (the daemon serves explicit null for 24/7 and a
   133	    /// concrete window otherwise — absent also renders as 24/7 for old daemons).
   134	    public var activeHoursStart: String?
   135	    public var activeHoursEnd: String?
   136	    /// Tiered scheduler v2: per-tier schedule + pacing, keyed by tier name.
   137	    /// A tier absent from the map is scheduled off.
   138	    public var tiers: [String: KiraTierConfig] = [:]
   139	
   140	    public static func parse(_ data: Data) -> KiraSchedulerStatus? {
   141	        guard let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
   142	              let paused = json["paused"] as? Bool else { return nil }
   143	        let config = json["config"] as? [String: Any]
   144	        let hours = config?["activeHours"] as? [String: Any]
   145	        var tiers: [String: KiraTierConfig] = [:]
   146	        if let rawTiers = config?["tiers"] as? [String: Any] {
   147	            for (key, value) in rawTiers {
   148	                guard let dict = value as? [String: Any] else { continue }
   149	                tiers[key] = KiraTierConfig.parse(dict)
   150	            }
   151	        }
   152	        return KiraSchedulerStatus(
   153	            paused: paused,
   154	            enabled: config?["enabled"] as? Bool ?? false,
   155	            intervalMinutes: (config?["intervalMinutes"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue,
   156	            imageCount: (config?["imageCount"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue,
   157	            videoCount: (config?["videoCount"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue,
   158	            videoMode: config?["videoMode"] as? String,
   159	            unlimitedImages: config?["unlimitedImages"] as? Bool ?? false,
   160	            activeHoursStart: hours?["start"] as? String,
   161	            activeHoursEnd: hours?["end"] as? String,
   162	            tiers: tiers)
   163	    }
   164	}
   165	
   166	/// One tier's schedule + pacing (tiered scheduler v2). nil window = 24/7.
   167	public struct KiraTierConfig: Equatable, Sendable {
   168	    public var activeHoursStart: String?
   169	    public var activeHoursEnd: String?
   170	    public var imageCount: Int
   171	    public var unlimitedImages: Bool
   172	    public var videoCount: Int
   173	
   174	    public static func parse(_ dict: [String: Any]) -> KiraTierConfig {
   175	        let hours = dict["activeHours"] as? [String: Any]
   176	        return KiraTierConfig(
   177	            activeHoursStart: hours?["start"] as? String,
   178	            activeHoursEnd: hours?["end"] as? String,
   179	            imageCount: (dict["imageCount"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? 2,
   180	            unlimitedImages: dict["unlimitedImages"] as? Bool ?? false,
   181	            videoCount: (dict["videoCount"] as? NSNumber)?.intValue ?? 1)
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    /// PUT payload fragment. The tiers PUT is a FULL REPLACEMENT server-side,
   185	    /// and every tier must carry the activeHours key (null = 24/7).
   186	    public func payload(isNeutral: Bool) -> [String: Any] {
   187	        var out: [String: Any] = [:]
   188	        if let start = activeHoursStart, let end = activeHoursEnd {
   189	            out["activeHours"] = ["start": start, "end": end]
   190	        } else {
   191	            out["activeHours"] = NSNull()
   192	        }
   193	        out["imageCount"] = imageCount
   194	        out["unlimitedImages"] = unlimitedImages
   195	        if !isNeutral { out["videoCount"] = videoCount }
   196	        return out
   197	    }
   198	}
   199	
   200	/// `GET/PUT /v1/kira/stream-mode` — who steers the 24/7 stream.
   201	/// Precedence: todd override > Kira's choice > schedule.
   202	public struct KiraStreamStatus: Equatable, Sendable {
   203	    /// nil exactly when schedule-driven and no tier window is open.
   204	    public var effectiveMode: String?
   205	    public var source: String
   206	    public var todd: String?
   207	    public var kira: String?
   208	    public var openTiers: [String]
   209	
   210	    public static func parse(_ json: [String: Any]) -> KiraStreamStatus? {
   610	            request.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
   611	            request.httpBody = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   612	        }
   613	        return request
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    private func fetch(_ path: String, timeout: TimeInterval = 8) async -> Data? {
   617	        guard let request = request(path, timeout: timeout) else { return nil }
   618	        guard let (data, response) = try? await URLSession.shared.data(for: request),
   619	              (response as? HTTPURLResponse)?.statusCode == 200 else { return nil }
   620	        return data
   621	    }
   622	
   623	    // MARK: - Dashboard (D2–D4, Workstream A bindings)
   624	
   625	    public func refreshDashboard() async {
   626	        async let stateData = fetch("v1/kira/state")
   627	        async let schedulerData = fetch("v1/kira/content-scheduler/status")
   628	        async let modeData = fetch("v1/kira/content-mode")
   629	        async let mediaData = fetch("v1/kira/media/recent")
   630	        async let suggestionsData = fetch("v1/kira/suggestions")
   631	
   632	        if let data = await stateData, let parsed = KiraStateSnapshot.parse(data) {
   633	            state = parsed
   634	            stateError = nil
   635	        } else if state == nil {
   636	            stateError = "GET /v1/kira/state unavailable"
   637	        } else {
   638	            // Keep the last snapshot on screen but SAY it's stale (Kimi review
   639	            // 2026-07-27) — the card's relative fetchedAt stops advancing and
   640	            // this line explains why.
   641	            stateError = "state refresh failed — showing last snapshot"
   642	        }
   643	        if let data = await schedulerData {
   644	            scheduler = KiraSchedulerStatus.parse(data)
   645	        }
   646	        if let data = await modeData,
   647	           let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any] {
   648	            contentMode = json["mode"] as? String
   649	            // Absent/empty allow-list keeps the previous value ON PURPOSE — a
   650	            // transiently malformed payload must not blank the mode picker.
   651	            allowedModes = json["allowed"] as? [String] ?? allowedModes
   652	        }
   653	        if let data = await mediaData,
   654	           let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
   655	           let items = json["items"] as? [[String: Any]] {
   656	            recentMedia = items.prefix(12).compactMap { item in
   657	                guard let kind = item["kind"] as? String,
   658	                      let path = item["path"] as? String else { return nil }
   659	                return KiraMediaItem(
   660	                    kind: kind, path: path,
   661	                    mtimeMs: (item["mtimeMs"] as? NSNumber)?.doubleValue ?? 0)
   662	            }
   663	        }
   664	        if let data = await suggestionsData,
   665	           let json = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
   790	            let status = (response as? HTTPURLResponse)?.statusCode ?? 0
   791	            guard (200..<300).contains(status) else {
   792	                let detail = String(data: data.prefix(200), encoding: .utf8) ?? ""
   793	                actionError = "\(method) \(path): HTTP \(status) \(detail)"
   794	                return
   795	            }
   796	        } catch {
   797	            actionError = error.localizedDescription
   798	        }
   799	        await refreshDashboard()
   800	    }

   802	    /// B1: toggles the Kira CONTENT scheduler sentinel — deliberately not the
   803	    /// generic /v1/scheduler (the FDD §3.2 conflation trap).
   804	    public func setSchedulerPaused(_ paused: Bool) async {
   805	        await perform("v1/kira/content-scheduler/\(paused ? "pause" : "resume")", method: "POST")
   806	    }

   808	    /// B5: set the default content mode (allowlisted server-side).
   809	    public func setContentMode(_ mode: String) async {
   810	        await perform("v1/kira/content-mode", method: "PUT", body: ["mode": mode])
   811	    }
   812	
   813	    /// Update the 24/7 content-scheduler pacing (partial update; validated
   814	    /// server-side). Live: the scheduler re-reads policy every cycle, so
   815	    /// edits take effect on the next tick without a daemon restart.
   816	    public func updateSchedulerPolicy(_ changes: [String: Any]) async {
   817	        // perform() already refreshes the dashboard on success (Kimi review
   818	        // 2026-07-27: this used to call refreshDashboard() a second time).
   819	        await perform("v1/kira/content-scheduler/policy", method: "PUT", body: changes)
   820	    }
   821	
   822	    /// Tiered scheduler v2: replace the whole tiers map (server semantics —
   823	    /// a tier omitted from the payload is scheduled off).
   824	    public func updateSchedulerTiers(_ tiers: [String: KiraTierConfig]) async {
   825	        var payload: [String: Any] = [:]
   826	        for (mode, tier) in tiers {
   827	            payload[mode] = tier.payload(isNeutral: mode == "neutral")
   828	        }
   829	        await perform("v1/kira/content-scheduler/policy", method: "PUT", body: ["tiers": payload])
   830	    }

   833	    public func setStreamOverride(_ mode: String?) async {
   834	        await perform("v1/kira/stream-mode", method: "PUT",
   835	                      body: ["todd": mode.map { $0 as Any } ?? NSNull()])
   836	    }

   515	            .contentGated()   // G-rated by default (Todd 2026-07-17)
   516	        }
   517	    }
   518	
   519	    /// Display order for tier rows — mildest first (the scheduler's own
   520	    /// overlap precedence is the reverse: most explicit wins).
   521	    static let tierOrder = ["neutral", "apple", "banana", "avocado"]
   522	
   523	    private func cadenceLine(_ scheduler: KiraSchedulerStatus) -> String {
   524	        var parts: [String] = []
   525	        if let interval = scheduler.intervalMinutes { parts.append("every \(interval)m") }
   526	        if scheduler.tiers.isEmpty {
   527	            // Pre-v2 daemon: legacy flat summary.
   528	            if scheduler.unlimitedImages {
   529	                parts.append("∞ img (fit cycle)")
   530	            } else if let images = scheduler.imageCount {
   531	                parts.append("\(images) img")
   532	            }
   533	            if let videos = scheduler.videoCount { parts.append("\(videos) video") }
   534	            if let start = scheduler.activeHoursStart, let end = scheduler.activeHoursEnd {
   535	                parts.append("\(Self.windowLabel(start))–\(Self.windowLabel(end))")
   536	            }
   537	        } else {
   538	            for mode in Self.tierOrder {
   539	                guard let tier = scheduler.tiers[mode] else { continue }
   540	                var bit = modeEmoji(mode)
   541	                if let start = tier.activeHoursStart, let end = tier.activeHoursEnd {
   542	                    bit += " \(Self.windowLabel(start))–\(Self.windowLabel(end))"
   543	                } else {
   544	                    bit += " 24/7"
   545	                }
   546	                bit += tier.unlimitedImages ? " ∞" : " \(tier.imageCount)img"
   547	                if mode != "neutral" && tier.videoCount > 0 { bit += " \(tier.videoCount)v" }
   548	                parts.append(bit)
   549	            }
   550	        }
   551	        return parts.joined(separator: " · ")
   552	    }
   553	
   554	    private func streamSummary(_ stream: KiraStreamStatus) -> String {
   555	        let mode = stream.effectiveMode.map { "\(modeEmoji($0)) \($0)" } ?? "idle (no window open)"
   556	        switch stream.source {
   557	        case "override-todd": return "\(mode) — your override"
   558	        case "override-kira": return "\(mode) — Kira's choice"
   559	        case "none-open": return mode
   560	        default: return "\(mode) — schedule"
   561	        }
   562	    }
   563	
   564	    // ── Tier rows (tiered scheduler v2) ──
   565	
   566	    /// Mutate-and-PUT: the server treats the tiers map as a full replacement.
   567	    private func putTiers(_ mutate: (inout [String: KiraTierConfig]) -> Void) {
   568	        guard var tiers = client.scheduler?.tiers else { return }
   569	        mutate(&tiers)
   570	        Task { await client.updateSchedulerTiers(tiers) }
   571	    }
   572	
   573	    @ViewBuilder private func tierRow(_ mode: String, scheduler: KiraSchedulerStatus) -> some View {
   574	        HStack(spacing: 10) {
   575	            Toggle(isOn: Binding(
   576	                get: { scheduler.tiers[mode] != nil },
   577	                set: { on in
   578	                    putTiers { tiers in
   579	                        if on {
   580	                            tiers[mode] = KiraTierConfig(
   581	                                activeHoursStart: nil, activeHoursEnd: nil,
   582	                                imageCount: 2, unlimitedImages: false,
   583	                                videoCount: mode == "neutral" ? 0 : 1)
   584	                        } else {
   585	                            tiers.removeValue(forKey: mode)
   586	                        }
   587	                    }
   588	                })) {
   589	                Text("\(modeEmoji(mode)) \(mode)")
   590	                    .font(.caption)
   591	                    .frame(width: 78, alignment: .leading)
   592	            }
   593	            .toggleStyle(.checkbox)
   594	            .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   595	
   596	            if let tier = scheduler.tiers[mode] {
   597	                Toggle("window", isOn: Binding(
   598	                    get: { tier.activeHoursStart != nil },
   599	                    set: { on in
   600	                        putTiers { tiers in
   601	                            tiers[mode]?.activeHoursStart = on ? "20:00" : nil
   602	                            tiers[mode]?.activeHoursEnd = on ? "08:00" : nil
   603	                        }
   604	                    }))
   605	                    .toggleStyle(.checkbox).font(.caption)
   606	                    .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   607	                    .help("Off = this tier is eligible 24/7. Overnight windows wrap midnight; the most explicit open tier wins overlaps.")
   608	                if tier.activeHoursStart != nil {
   609	                    Picker("", selection: tierHourBinding(mode, isStart: true)) {
   610	                        ForEach(0..<24, id: \.self) { Text(Self.hourLabel($0)).tag($0) }
   611	                    }
   612	                    .labelsHidden().frame(width: 86)
   613	                    .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   614	                    Text("–").font(.caption).foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
   615	                    Picker("", selection: tierHourBinding(mode, isStart: false)) {
   616	                        ForEach(0..<24, id: \.self) { Text(Self.hourLabel($0)).tag($0) }
   617	                    }
   618	                    .labelsHidden().frame(width: 86)
   619	                    .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   620	                }
   621	                Stepper("img: \(tier.unlimitedImages ? "∞" : String(tier.imageCount))",
   622	                        onIncrement: { putTiers { $0[mode]?.imageCount = tier.imageCount + 1 } },
   623	                        onDecrement: { putTiers { $0[mode]?.imageCount = max(0, tier.imageCount - 1) } })
   624	                    .font(.caption)
   625	                    .disabled(client.actionInFlight || tier.unlimitedImages)
   626	                Toggle("∞", isOn: Binding(
   627	                    get: { tier.unlimitedImages },
   628	                    set: { on in putTiers { $0[mode]?.unlimitedImages = on } }))
   629	                    .toggleStyle(.checkbox).font(.caption)
   630	                    .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   631	                    .help("Unlimited-within-cycle: image renders chain until the cycle (or this tier's window) closes.")
   632	                if mode != "neutral" {
   633	                    Stepper("vid: \(tier.videoCount)",
   634	                            onIncrement: { putTiers { $0[mode]?.videoCount = tier.videoCount + 1 } },
   635	                            onDecrement: { putTiers { $0[mode]?.videoCount = max(0, tier.videoCount - 1) } })
   636	                        .font(.caption)
   637	                        .disabled(client.actionInFlight)
   638	                } else {
   639	                    Text("stills only").font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
   640	                        .help("Neutral is the Autocord film stream — no video.")
   641	                }
   642	                Spacer(minLength: 0)
   643	            } else {
   644	                Text("off").font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
   645	                Spacer(minLength: 0)
   646	            }
   647	        }
   648	    }
   649	
   650	    /// Hour-grain binding for one end of a tier's window (writes the pair).
   651	    private func tierHourBinding(_ mode: String, isStart: Bool) -> Binding<Int> {
   652	        Binding(
   653	            get: {
   654	                let tier = client.scheduler?.tiers[mode]
   655	                return Self.hour(from: isStart ? tier?.activeHoursStart : tier?.activeHoursEnd) ?? (isStart ? 20 : 8)
   656	            },
   657	            set: { h in
   658	                putTiers { tiers in
   659	                    guard var tier = tiers[mode] else { return }
   660	                    var start = Self.hour(from: tier.activeHoursStart) ?? 20

   444	export function createVideoTools(config: VideoConfig): BuiltInTool[] {
   445	    const tools: BuiltInTool[] = [];
   446	
   447	    // Build tool descriptions from the seal gate. When sealed, Claude MUST NOT
   448	    // author the detailed prompt — the local engine does it on-device. The
   449	    // description is the only signal Claude has to change its behavior, so
   450	    // the sealed variant strips the "write long/detailed" instructions and
   451	    // asks only for brief non-explicit intent. When not sealed, the current
   452	    // detailed instructions stand.
   453	    const sealingActive = shouldSeal(config.sealedOrchestrator);
   454	
   455	    const generateVideoDescription = sealingActive
   456	        ? `Generate a short video clip via ComfyBox MCP. Two modes:
   457	
   458	1. **Image-to-video** (preferred): Pass image_path and a BRIEF NON-EXPLICIT motion description (mood, camera intent, ~1 sentence). The local engine authors the full 80-120-word motion prompt on-device.
   459	
   460	2. **Text-to-video**: Pass a BRIEF NON-EXPLICIT scene intent (~1 sentence: subject, mood, atmosphere). The local engine authors the full cinematic prompt on-device.
   461	
   462	Do NOT compose long/detailed cinematic prose — that authorship happens locally under seal.
   463	
   464	Returns a local .mp4 file path. Send via send_telegram_document for Telegram delivery.`
   465	        : `Generate a short video clip via ComfyBox MCP. Two modes:
   466	
   467	1. **Image-to-video** (preferred): Pass image_path from a previously generated image. Describe the MOTION you want — the image already provides the scene. Example: "slow smile, turns head to the right, warm breeze lifting her hair, camera dollying in gently." ~5 seconds, 480p/720p.
   468	
   469	2. **Text-to-video**: Pass only a prompt. Must be LONG and cinematic — describe camera angles, lighting, movement, atmosphere in detail. Short prompts produce bad results. Up to 20 seconds, up to 1080p.
   470	
   471	Returns a local .mp4 file path. Send via send_telegram_document for Telegram delivery.`;

   223	/** Polling interval for ComfyBox video_status checks (ms) */
   224	const POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 5_000;
   225	
   226	/**
   227	 * Duration-aware poll deadline (ms). One ~4s chunk gets the historical 10-min
   228	 * budget (a cold/GPU-contended LTX i2v exceeded 300s nightly — 2026-07-10, job
   229	 * cancelled at deadline while rendering fine). A long clip renders as
   230	 * ceil(duration/4) spliced chunks (~2.5–6 min each); a fixed 10-min budget
   231	 * cancels a still-rendering long job and orphans it on the Mac (2026-07-15).
   232	 * Add headroom per additional chunk.
   233	 */
   234	/**
   235	 * Outer ToolExecutor cap for the video tools. INVARIANT: this must EXCEED
   236	 * every value computeVideoDeadlineMs can return (the clamp below enforces
   237	 * it), or the executor kills the tool call while the poll loop still thinks
   238	 * it has budget — orphaning a healthy render (codex review, PR #1458).
   239	 * 160 min covers the worst clamped inner budget (150 min) with margin.
   240	 */
   241	export const VIDEO_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MS = 9_600_000;
   242	
   243	export function computeVideoDeadlineMs(durationSec?: number, quality?: string): number {
   244	    const BASE_MS = 1_500_000;      // one ~4s chunk, 25 min (quality recipe: 97f two-stage measured 923s, 2026-07-25)
   245	    const PER_CHUNK_MS = 1_200_000; // +20 min per additional chunk (lost-job fail-fast below keeps dead jobs from burning this)
   246	    const CHUNK_SEC = 4;
   247	    const d = Number(durationSec);
   248	    const chunks = Number.isFinite(d) && d > CHUNK_SEC ? Math.ceil(d / CHUNK_SEC) : 1;
   249	    const budget = BASE_MS + (chunks - 1) * PER_CHUNK_MS;
   250	    // Two-stage refine (#1443) reruns the sampler on the upscaled latent —
   251	    // roughly doubles wall-clock, so double the whole poll budget.
   252	    const scaled = quality === 'hq' ? budget * 2 : budget;
   253	    // Clamp 10 min under the outer tool timeout so the inner deadline always
   254	    // fires first (also catches pre-existing >28s chunked fast renders, whose
   255	    // unclamped budget already breached the old 80-min outer cap).
   256	    return Math.min(scaled, VIDEO_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MS - 600_000);
   257	}

  1240	/**
  1241	 * Best-effort cancel of an in-flight ComfyBox video job. ComfyBox only exposes
  1242	 * clear_queue (no per-job cancel), so on a poll timeout we flush the queue to
  1243	 * stop the orphaned render. Swallows all errors — cancellation is advisory.
  1244	 */
  1245	async function cancelComfyBoxJob(config: VideoConfig, jobId: string): Promise<void> {
  1246	    if (!config.executor) return;
  1247	    try {
  1248	        await config.executor.execute('mcp_comfybox__clear_queue', {});
  1249	        console.warn(`[video] Cancelled ComfyBox queue after job ${jobId} timed out`);
  1250	    } catch (err) {
  1251	        console.warn(`[video] Failed to cancel ComfyBox job ${jobId}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  1252	    }
  1253	}

   287	export function computeI2VMotionRecipe(
   288	    promptText: string,
   289	    durationSec: number,
   290	    contentMode?: VideoContentMode,
   291	): { isAction: boolean; recipe: { strength?: number; guidance?: number }; singlePassFrames: number } {
   292	    const t = String(promptText).toLowerCase();
   293	    const hasPartner = /\b(older man|man |partner|behind her|him|his |thrust|penetrat|doggy|cowgirl|riding|spooning|missionary|blowjob|standing sex)\b/.test(t);
   294	    const hasAction = /\b(thrust|penetrat|rock|bounce|riding|driving|grind|pump|slam)\b/.test(t);
   295	    const isAction = hasPartner && hasAction;
   296	    const d = Number(durationSec) || 0;
   297	    const isNsfw = contentMode === 'banana' || contentMode === 'avocado';
   298	    // NSFW_MIN_FRAMES 241 vs the SFW/neutral 97f floor — see the doc note above.
   299	    const minFrames = isNsfw ? 241 : 97;
   300	    // 289f = 1+8*36 (LTX trained window). frames overrides the chunked `duration` path.
   301	    // ALL clips ≤12s render single-pass, not just action clips (2026-07-28 fix):
   302	    // the chunked continuation path degenerates in the second chunk — verified
   303	    // live when the first non-avocado rotation cycles rendered solo concepts.
   304	    const singlePassFrames = d > 0 && d <= 12
   305	        ? Math.min(289, Math.max(minFrames, Math.round((d * LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS - 1) / 8) * 8 + 1))
   306	        : 0;
