   308	    // strength 0.5 (REVERTED 2026-08-03 after ~3h at 0.75): at Kira's real
   309	    // config (832x448, 241f single-pass) 0.75 collapses mid-clip — subject
   310	    // balloons toward camera, faces smear, scene washes out. NAG carries
   311	    // prompt adherence but does NOT substitute for the seed hold on long
   312	    // single passes. 0.5 is the validated value; a proper 0.5/0.6/0.75 ladder
   313	    // at production config is the way back up, not another point change.
   314	    // guidance 1.0 as of 2026-08-02 (was 3.0). ComfyBox now implements NAG
   315	    // (Normalized Attention Guidance), which is how the reference PinkCherry
   316	    // author gets prompt adherence — he runs cfg 1.0 on BOTH passes with
   317	    // NAG 11.0/0.25/2.5, verified from the API graphs embedded in his published
   318	    // mp4s across v1.6/v1.7/v1.8. NAG replaces high CFG rather than supplementing
   319	    // it: CFG extrapolates the final prediction with no magnitude bound, while
   320	    // NAG extrapolates inside cross-attention and renormalises, so adherence

   211	
   212	    /// A frame count is valid when it's 1 + 8k and ≥ 9.
   213	    public static func isValidFrameCount(_ n: Int) -> Bool {
   214	        n >= 9 && (n - 1) % 8 == 0
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    /// fps must be positive and sane (chunk planning divides by it; the RoPE
   218	    /// temporal coords divide by it too).
   219	    public static func isValidFPS(_ fps: Int) -> Bool {
   220	        fps >= 1 && fps <= 120
   221	    }
   222	
   223	    /// Dimensions must be positive multiples of 32.
   224	    public static func areValidDimensions(width: Int, height: Int) -> Bool {
   225	        width > 0 && height > 0 && width % 32 == 0 && height % 32 == 0
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    public struct ChunkPlan: Equatable, Sendable {
   229	        public let totalChunks: Int
   230	        public let totalFrames: Int
   231	        public let durationSeconds: Float
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    /// How many chunks and frames a request produces. Each continuation chunk
   235	    /// re-uses the previous chunk's last frame, so it adds `framesPerChunk - 1`
   236	    /// new frames. `extendToSeconds == 0` → a single chunk.
   237	    public static func chunkPlan(framesPerChunk: Int, extendToSeconds: Float, fps: Int) -> ChunkPlan {
   238	        let totalChunks: Int
   239	        if extendToSeconds > 0 {
   240	            let targetFrames = Int(extendToSeconds * Float(fps))
   241	            let continuations = max(0, Int(ceil(Float(targetFrames - framesPerChunk) / Float(framesPerChunk - 1))))
   242	            totalChunks = 1 + continuations
   243	        } else {
   244	            totalChunks = 1
   245	        }
   246	        let totalFrames = framesPerChunk + (framesPerChunk - 1) * (totalChunks - 1)
   247	        return ChunkPlan(
   248	            totalChunks: totalChunks,
   249	            totalFrames: totalFrames,
   250	            durationSeconds: Float(totalFrames) / Float(fps)
   251	        )
   252	    }
   253	
   254	    /// Validate a request without loading anything.
   255	    public func validate(_ request: LTX2VideoRequest) throws {
   256	        guard Self.isValidFrameCount(request.framesPerChunk) else {
   257	            throw LTX2VideoError.invalidFrameCount(request.framesPerChunk)
   258	        }
   259	        guard Self.areValidDimensions(width: request.width, height: request.height) else {
   260	            throw LTX2VideoError.invalidDimensions(request.width, request.height)
   261	        }
   262	        let weightsURL = resolveWeightsFileURL()
   263	        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: weightsURL.path) else {
   264	            throw LTX2VideoError.weightsMissing(weightsURL.path)
   265	        }
   470	        // Running it as one giant pass is the leading suspect for the
   471	        // uniform grid/mesh artifact seen in every local I2V test tonight.
   472	        // Two-stage refine (Phase 3): load the spatial latent upsampler if enabled.
   473	        // ltx-2.3-spatial-upscaler-x2-1.1 keys map 1:1 to LTX2LatentUpsampler.
   474	        var upsampler: LTX2LatentUpsampler? = nil
   475	        if ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_TWO_STAGE"] == "1",
   476	           let upPath = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_UPSAMPLER_PATH"],
   477	           FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: upPath) {
   478	            upsampler = Self.loadUpsampler(path: upPath, logger: logger)
   479	        }
   480	        // Tiled/chunked VAE decode is OOM-safe on long/large clips but seams on
   481	        // fast motion (spatial-tile mosaic + temporal-window jitter). Plain
   482	        // single-pass decode (as ComfyUI does) is clean but memory-heavier.
   483	        // LTX2_TILED_DECODE=0 selects plain decode. Default stays tiled.
   484	        let tiled = ProcessInfo.processInfo.environment["LTX2_TILED_DECODE"] != "0"
   485	        // NOTE: the warm pipeline is built ONCE here, so per-request fps cannot
   486	        // flow through config.fps (it would bake in the first request's value).
   487	        // The temporal-RoPE conditioning fps (the motion dial) is therefore
   488	        // controlled per-render via LTX2_COND_FPS (read fresh in createPositionGrid).
   489	        let pipelineConfig = LTX2PipelineConfig(modelPath: modelDir, pipelineType: .distilled, hasPromptAdaLN: true, tiledDecode: tiled)
   490	        self.pipeline = LTX2Pipeline(vae: vae, textEncoder: textEncoder, transformer: transformer, config: pipelineConfig, upsampler: upsampler)
   491	        self.tokenizer = try LTX2GemmaTokenizer.load(from: URL(fileURLWithPath: config.gemmaPath), maxLength: 128)
   492	        isLoaded = true
   493	        loadedLoraKey = wantKey
   494	        logger.info("LTX-2: models ready.")
   495	    }
   496	
   497	
   498	    /// Load + validate the spatial latent upsampler. Shared by the load-time
   499	    /// path (env) and the per-request lazy path (finding #18). Returns nil —
   500	    /// loudly — when the file's keys do not bind the module completely: a
   501	    /// partially bound upsampler renders a periodic mesh (2026-08-01).
   502	    static func loadUpsampler(path: String, logger: Logger) -> LTX2LatentUpsampler? {
   503	        let up = LTX2LatentUpsampler()
   504	        guard let w = try? MLX.loadArrays(url: URL(fileURLWithPath: path)) else {
   505	            logger.error("LTX-2: upsampler file unreadable: \(path)")
  1440	  private func createPositionGrid(
  1441	    batchSize: Int,
  1442	    latF: Int,
  1443	    latH: Int,
  1444	    latW: Int,
  1445	    refFrames: Int = 0,   // IC-control: appended reference frames positioned at temporal frame_idx 0
  1446	    spatialScaleMul: Float = 1.0   // refine: scale spatial positions to keep RoPE in-distribution at 2x
  1447	  ) -> MLXArray {
  1448	    let temporalScale = Float(temporalCompression)
  1449	    let spatialScale = Float(spatialCompression) * spatialScaleMul
  1450	    // Conditioning frame_rate drives the temporal RoPE spacing, which is the
  1451	    // motion-vs-coherence dial: LOWER fps → coords spread further apart → the
  1452	    // model assumes bigger inter-frame time gaps → MORE motion per frame (until
  1453	    // coords exceed the trained temporal max_pos ~20 → shimmer). LTX2_COND_FPS
  1454	    // decouples this from the OUTPUT/playback fps (config.fps) so we can drive
  1455	    // motion from a sharp seed without making the mp4 choppy. Default: match
  1456	    // playback fps (temporally-correct). See QA-CAMPAIGN-2026-07-26 motion sweep.
  1457	    let fps = resolvedConfig.condFps ?? Float(config.fps)
  1458	    let totalF = latF + refFrames
  1459	    let numPatches = totalF * latH * latW
  1460	
  1461	    // Build position indices
  1462	    var positions = [Float](repeating: 0, count: batchSize * 3 * numPatches * 2)
  1463	
  1464	    for b in 0..<batchSize {
  1465	      for f in 0..<totalF {
  1466	        // IC-control reference frames (grid index >= latF) take temporal
  1467	        // frame_idx 0 (their own 0-based reference index) so RoPE treats them as
  1468	        // reference context at the sequence start, not trailing frames. Mirrors
  1469	        // ComfyUI add_keyframe_index: pixel_coords[:,0] += frame_idx(=0).
  1470	        let tf = f < latF ? f : (f - latF)
  1471	        for h in 0..<latH {
  1472	          for w in 0..<latW {
  1473	            let tokenIdx = f * latH * latW + h * latW + w
  1474	            let baseIdx = b * 3 * numPatches * 2
  1475	
  1476	            // Time start/end in pixel space
  1477	            var tStart = Float(tf) * temporalScale
  1478	            var tEnd = Float(tf + 1) * temporalScale
  1479	
  1480	            // Causal fix: shift temporal coordinates (matches reference
  1481	            // latent_to_pixel_coords: pixel_coords[:,0] = (pc + 1 - scale[0]).clamp(0)).
  1482	            tStart = max(0, tStart + 1 - temporalScale)
  1483	            tEnd = max(0, tEnd + 1 - temporalScale)
  1484	
  1485	            // fps division RESTORED (2026-07-25): ComfyUI's
  1486	            // _prepare_positional_embeddings (ldm/lightricks/model.py) does
  1487	            // `fractional_coords[:, 0] *= 1/frame_rate` before RoPE — temporal
  1488	            // coordinates are in SECONDS. Without it our coords span 0..48
  1489	            // against the trained temporal max_pos of 20 (out of range) and
  1490	            // adjacent frames sit 24x further apart than trained — the model
  1491	            // renders them near-independently: motion survives but fine detail
  1492	            // decorrelates frame to frame (the shimmer: flicker 0.45-0.8 vs
  1493	            // reference 0.157). The earlier removal ("fixed near-static
  1494	            // motion") misattributed the freeze — that was the pristine-still
  1495	            // conditioning bug (LTX2_I2V_COMPRESSION=0 freezing i2v), fixed
  1496	            // separately.
  1497	            tStart /= fps
  1498	            tEnd /= fps
  1499	
  1500	            // Spatial in pixel space
  1501	            let hStart = Float(h) * spatialScale
  1502	            let hEnd = Float(h + 1) * spatialScale
  1503	            let wStart = Float(w) * spatialScale
  1504	            let wEnd = Float(w + 1) * spatialScale
  1505	
  1506	            // Time dimension
  1507	            positions[baseIdx + 0 * numPatches * 2 + tokenIdx * 2 + 0] = tStart
  1508	            positions[baseIdx + 0 * numPatches * 2 + tokenIdx * 2 + 1] = tEnd
  1509	
  1510	            // Height dimension
   210	}

   214	/// Tier A tuning fields a REQUEST or PRESET may carry. All optional — nil
   215	/// means "defer to the next precedence level". This is the wire shape for
   216	/// both the video request extension and the preset `videoTuning` block.
   217	public struct LTX2VideoTuning: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
   218	  public var guidanceRescale: Float?

   220	  public var stage1Sigmas: [Float]?
   221	  public var refineSigmas: [Float]?

   240	/// The authoritative, TYPED configuration for one render. Codex finding #14:
   241	/// this object — not scattered env reads — is what render code must consume.
   242	/// `params` carries the string readout (for the API/card/log); `provenance`
   243	/// records which level supplied each final value.
   244	public struct LTX2ResolvedVideoConfig: Sendable {
   245	  // Tier A
   246	  public let guidanceRescale: Float
   247	  public let cfgSchedule: [Float]
   248	  public let stage1Sigmas: [Float]

   506	  /// generate_video -> POST /v1/video/generate/async (submit; poll video_status)
   507	  private func executeGenerateVideo(_ params: MCPParams?) async throws -> MCPToolResult {
   508	    guard let prompt = params?.string("prompt"), !prompt.isEmpty else {
   509	      return MCPToolResult(error: "Error: 'prompt' is required")
   510	    }
   511	
   512	    var body: [String: Any] = ["prompt": prompt]
   513	
   514	    if let imagePath = params?.string("image_path") {
   515	      body["image_path"] = imagePath
   516	    }
   517	    if let duration = params?.integer("duration") {
   518	      body["duration"] = duration
   519	    }
   520	    if let resolution = params?.string("resolution") {
   521	      body["resolution"] = resolution
   522	    }
   523	    if let aspectRatio = params?.string("aspect_ratio") {
   524	      body["aspect_ratio"] = aspectRatio
   525	    }
   526	    // T2V has no source frame, so translate aspect_ratio into explicit
   527	    // width/height here: WarmServer's video request decodes width/height, NOT
   528	    // aspect_ratio, so otherwise every t2v render fell back to the 704x448
   529	    // landscape default regardless of the requested orientation. Portrait
   530	    // ("9:16") swaps to 448x704 (both /64). I2V leaves dims unset so WarmServer
   531	    // keeps matching the source image aspect.
   532	    if params?.string("image_path") == nil {
   533	      // Respect caller-supplied dims; otherwise orient the standard t2v dims by
   534	      // aspect_ratio. WarmServer reads width/height (not aspect_ratio/resolution),
   535	      // so without this every t2v fell back to the 704x448 landscape default.
   536	      if let w = params?.integer("width"), let h = params?.integer("height") {
   537	        body["width"] = w
   538	        body["height"] = h
   539	      } else {
   540	        let portrait = (params?.string("aspect_ratio") ?? "16:9") == "9:16"
   541	        body["width"] = portrait ? 448 : 704
   542	        body["height"] = portrait ? 704 : 448
   543	      }
   544	    }
   545	    if let seed = params?.integer("seed") {
   546	      body["seed"] = seed
   547	    }
   548	    if let outputPath = params?.string("output_path") {
   549	      body["output_path"] = outputPath
   550	    }
   551	    if let preset = params?.string("preset") {
   552	      body["preset"] = preset
   553	    }
   554	    // Motion vs fidelity per content type (#40): the daemon sends a high
   555	    // img_compression + lower strength for partnered-action prompts (motion)
   556	    // and low compression + strength 1.0 for solo/portrait (fidelity).
   557	    if let strength = params?.number("strength") {
   558	      body["strength"] = strength
   559	    }
   560	    if let comp = params?.integer("img_compression") {
   561	      body["img_compression"] = comp
   562	    }
   563	    if let cfg = params?.number("guidance") {
   564	      body["guidance"] = cfg
   565	    }
   566	    if let tuning = params?.dict("tuning") {
   567	      body["tuning"] = tuning.mapValues { $0.value }
   568	    }
   569	    if let attemptId = params?.string("optimization_attempt_id") {
   570	      body["optimization_attempt_id"] = attemptId
   571	    }
   572	    if let frames = params?.integer("frames") {
   573	      body["frames"] = frames
   574	    }
   575	    if let neg = params?.string("negative_prompt") {
   576	      body["negative_prompt"] = neg
   577	    }
   578	    // Callers that ran their OWN optimizer send enhance:false — a second
   579	    // server-side rewrite drifts the prompt off concrete staging (limb
   580	    // placement, figure count) and double-injects the character description.
   581	    if let enhance = params?.bool("enhance") {
   582	      body["enhance"] = enhance
   583	    }
   584	
   585	    let jsonData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   585	    let jsonData = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
   586	    // Async route for BOTH backends: local renders return 202 + job_id
   587	    // immediately (poll video_status), instead of blocking the MCP call for
   588	    // the entire multi-minute render — which overran the daemon-side 300s
   589	    // MCP tool timeout on every cold-start render (#219).
   590	    let (status, data) = try await client.post("/v1/video/generate/async", body: jsonData)
   591	    if status == 200 || status == 202 {
   592	      let text = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) ?? "{}"
   593	      return MCPToolResult(text: text)
   594	    }
   595	    return mapHTTPResponse(status: status, data: data)
   596	  }
   597

342:export const LTX_CONDITIONING_FPS = 24;
     1	/**
     2	 * engine-templates.ts — model-aware prompt template registry (FDD 2026-07-12).
     3	 *
     4	 * The prompt optimizer historically formatted EVERY image prompt for Z-Image
     5	 * (Qwen3-4B encoder) and every video prompt for Wan 2.2. But Kira's default
     6	 * image model is now Krea 2 (`krea-kira` render set → `krea2`) and the default
     7	 * video model is LTX. Neither was tailored.
     8	 *
     9	 * This module classifies the active model into an engine *family* and provides
    10	 * the per-family template behaviour (structural wrap, LLM persona system
    11	 * prompt, negative-prompt policy, length target, post-rewrite clean-up rules).
    12	 *
    13	 * Regression invariant: `z-image` and `wan` behaviour is unchanged. The
    14	 * optimizer keeps its existing Z-Image / Wan code paths inline; this module
    15	 * adds the Krea 2 (image) and LTX (video) templates and the classifier that
    16	 * routes to them. Unknown / undefined engines classify as `z-image`, preserving
    17	 * today's default behaviour.
    18	 *
    19	 * Zero npm dependencies.
    20	 */
    21	
    22	import { normalizeModelId } from '../providers/comfybox/client';
    23	
    24	export type EngineFamily = 'z-image' | 'krea2' | 'ltx' | 'wan' | 'generic';
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Per-engine template descriptor (FDD §4.1). Image engines carry a structural
    28	 * `wrap` (rule-based fallback) and an LLM `systemPrompt` persona; video engines
    29	 * carry a `fixedNegative`. The post-rewrite flags (`antiBoosterTags`,
    30	 * `quoteLiteralText`) drive Krea 2's clean-up pass.
    31	 */
    32	export interface EngineTemplate {
    33	    family: EngineFamily;
    34	    /** Structural format applied on the rule-based (LLM-disabled/fallback) path. */
    35	    wrap?(prompt: string, ctx?: { contentMode?: string; preserveAnchors?: string[] }): string;
    36	    /** LLM optimizer persona for this engine, per content mode. */
    37	    systemPrompt?(mode?: string): string;
    38	    /** false for guidance-0 engines (z-image, krea2) — negatives are inert. */
    39	    supportsNegatives: boolean;
    40	    /** Canonical negative string for engines that use one (ltx). */
    41	    fixedNegative?: string;
    42	    /** Soft length target (krea2 token ceiling; ltx/wan word slots). */
    43	    lengthTarget?: { min?: number; max?: number; maxTokens?: number };
    44	    /** krea2: strip masterpiece/8k/hyperdetailed booster stacks. */
    45	    antiBoosterTags?: boolean;
    46	    /** krea2: wrap literal on-image text in double quotes. */
    47	    quoteLiteralText?: boolean;
    48	}
    49	
    50	/**
    51	 * Classify a model id/path into an engine family (FDD §4.1).
    52	 *
    53	 *   - `krea2*`, `krea-kira`, `krea*`               → krea2
    54	 *   - `cyberrealistic*`, `z-image*`, `zimage*`, `moody*` → z-image
    55	 *   - `ltx*`                                       → ltx
    56	 *   - `wan*`                                       → wan
    57	 *   - unknown / undefined                          → z-image  (regression-safe default)
    58	 *
    59	 * Reuses `normalizeModelId` (basename, lowercased, checkpoint extension
    60	 * stripped) then collapses separators so both `z-image-turbo` and `zimage`,
    61	 * and both `krea2` and `krea-kira`, match their family.
    62	 */
    63	export function classifyEngine(model?: string): EngineFamily {
    64	    if (!model || !model.trim()) return 'z-image';
    65	    const n = normalizeModelId(model).replace(/[-_\s.]/g, '');
    66	    if (!n) return 'z-image';
    67	
    68	    if (n.startsWith('krea')) return 'krea2';
    69	    if (n.startsWith('cyberrealistic') || n.startsWith('zimage') || n.startsWith('moody')) return 'z-image';
    70	    if (n.startsWith('ltx')) return 'ltx';
    71	    if (n.startsWith('wan')) return 'wan';
    72	
    73	    // Unknown → z-image preserves today's behaviour (regression-safe default).
    74	    return 'z-image';
    75	}
    76	
    77	// ── Krea 2 post-rewrite clean-up helpers ─────────────────────────────────────
    78	
    79	/**
    80	 * Booster tag stacks Krea 2 was trained to exclude (anti-"AI look"). These
730:                    description: 'Exactly segments-1 entries, or omit for hard cuts. Each: {type: "cut"|"fade"|"dissolve", duration_s (default 0.5)}. Total duration = sum(segments) - sum(transitions).',
1266:    const duration = Number(params.duration) || (isNsfw ? T2V_NSFW_DEFAULT_DURATION_SEC : T2V_DEFAULT_DURATION_SEC);

     6	/// lock to be held while doing any eval or asyncEval.  This is
     7	/// a recursive lock to handle any cases where a closure might
     8	/// call back into eval.
     9	let evalLock = NSRecursiveLock()
    10	
    11	/// Evaluate one or more `MLXArray`
    12	///
    13	/// ### See Also
    14	/// - <doc:lazy-evaluation>
    15	public func eval(_ arrays: MLXArray...) {
    16	    let vector_array = new_mlx_vector_array(arrays)
    17	    _ = evalLock.withLock {
    18	        mlx_eval(vector_array)
    19	    }
    20	    mlx_vector_array_free(vector_array)
    21	}
    22	
    23	/// Evaluate one or more `MLXArray`
    24	///
    25	/// ### See Also
    26	/// - <doc:lazy-evaluation>
    27	public func eval(_ arrays: [MLXArray]) {
    28	    let vector_array = new_mlx_vector_array(arrays)
    29	    _ = evalLock.withLock {
    30	        mlx_eval(vector_array)
    31	    }
    32	    mlx_vector_array_free(vector_array)
    33	}
    34	
    35	/// Evaluate one or more `MLXArray` asynchronously.
    36	///
    37	/// ### See Also
    38	/// - <doc:lazy-evaluation>
    39	/// - ``asyncEval(_:)-6j4zg``
    40	public func asyncEval(_ arrays: [MLXArray]) {
    41	    let vector_array = new_mlx_vector_array(arrays)
    42	    _ = evalLock.withLock {
    43	        mlx_async_eval(vector_array)
    44	    }
    45	    mlx_vector_array_free(vector_array)
    46	}
    47	
    48	/// Evaluate one or more `MLXArray`.
    49	///
    50	/// This variant allows several structured types:
    51	///
    52	/// ```swift
    53	/// let a: MLXArray
    54	/// let b: [MLXArray]
    55	/// let c: [String:MLXArray]
    56	/// let d: [String:[MLXArray]]
    57	/// let e: (MLXArray, MLXArray)
    58	/// let f: [(String, MLXArray)]
    59	/// let nested: [(MLXArray, [MLXArray])]
    60	///
    61	/// eval(a, b, c, d, e, f)
    62	/// ```
    63	///
    64	/// Other structured types may be supported -- check the implementation.
    65	///
    66	/// ### See Also
    67	/// - <doc:lazy-evaluation>
    68	/// - ``asyncEval(_:)-6j4zg``
    69	public func eval(_ values: Any...) {
    70	    var arrays = [MLXArray]()
    71	
    72	    for item in values {
    73	        collect(item, into: &arrays)
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    eval(arrays)
    77	}
    78	
    79	/// Evaluate one or more `MLXArray`.
    80	///
    81	/// See ``eval(_:)``
    82	public func eval(_ values: [Any]) {
    83	    var arrays = [MLXArray]()
    84	
    85	    for item in values {
    86	        collect(item, into: &arrays)
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    eval(arrays)
    90	}
    91	
    92	/// Variant of ``eval(_:)-3b2g9`` that checks for errors in MLX and throws.
    93	///
    94	/// ### See Also
    95	/// - <doc:lazy-evaluation>
    96	public func checkedEval(_ values: Any...) throws {
    97	    var arrays = [MLXArray]()
    98	
    99	    for item in values {
   100	        collect(item, into: &arrays)
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    try withError {
   104	        eval(arrays)
   105	    }
   535	    /// ### See Also
   536	    /// - <doc:conversion>
   537	    public func realPart(stream: StreamOrDevice = .default) -> MLXArray {
   538	        precondition(dtype.isComplex)
   539	        return asType(Float.self)
   540	    }

   543	    ///
   544	    /// ### See Also
   545	    /// - <doc:conversion>

   547	        precondition(dtype.isComplex)
   548	        let i = MLXArray(real: 0, imaginary: 1)
   549	        return (self / i).asType(.float32)

   554	    /// MLX is lazy and arrays are not fully realized until they are evaluated.  This method is typically
   555	    /// not needed as all reads ensure the contents are evaluated.
   556	    public func eval() {

    52	array eval_impl(std::vector<array> outputs, bool async) {
    53	  std::deque<array> tape;
    54	
    55	  // Make an effort to choose a good output stream
    56	  Stream stream = default_stream(default_device());
    57	  for (auto& o : outputs) {
    58	    if (o.status() == array::Status::unscheduled && o.has_primitive()) {
    59	      stream = o.primitive().stream();
    60	      break;
    61	    }
    62	  }
    63	
    64	  // Map of array id that needs fence and stream it's computed on
    65	  std::unordered_map<uintptr_t, uint32_t> needs_fence;
    66	
    67	  auto synchronizer = array(
    68	      {}, bool_, std::make_shared<Synchronizer>(stream), std::move(outputs));
    69	
    70	  // Stream fences for inter-stream synchronization
    71	  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Fence> fences;
    72	
    73	  // Stream events for synchronization after eval
    74	  std::unordered_map<uint32_t, Event> events;
    75	  {
    76	    auto e = Event{stream};
    77	    e.set_value(1);
    78	    synchronizer.attach_event(e);
    79	    events.emplace(stream.index, std::move(e));
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  {
    83	    // Record the degree of each input
    84	    std::unordered_map<std::uintptr_t, int> cache;
    85	
    86	    std::stack<std::pair<std::reference_wrapper<array>, int>> dfs;
    87	    dfs.emplace(synchronizer, 0);
    88	    while (!dfs.empty()) {
    89	      auto& [a_ref, idx] = dfs.top();
    90	      auto& a = a_ref.get();
    91	
    92	      if (idx < a.inputs().size()) {
    93	        // Add an input, and continue
    94	        auto& in = a.inputs()[idx++];
    95	
    96	        if (in.status() == array::Status::unscheduled) {
    97	          if (async && in.is_tracer()) {
    98	            throw std::invalid_argument(
    99	                "[async_eval] Not allowed inside a graph transformation.");
   100	          }
   101	          if (!in.has_primitive()) {
   102	            if (in.is_tracer()) {
   103	              throw std::invalid_argument(
   104	                  "[eval] Attempting to eval an array during function"
   105	                  " transformations like compile or vmap is not allowed.");
   106	            }
   107	            throw std::runtime_error(
   108	                "[eval] Attempting to eval an array without a primitive.\n"
   109	                "If you are compiling a function, make sure all the inputs "
   110	                "and outputs are captured:\n"
   111	                "https://ml-explore.github.io/mlx/build/html/usage/compile.html#pure-functions.\n"
   112	                "If you are not using compile, this may be a bug. "
   113	                "Please file an issue here:\n"
   114	                "https://github.com/ml-explore/mlx/issues.");
   115	          }
   116	          if (a.primitive().stream() != in.primitive().stream()) {
   117	            needs_fence.emplace(in.id(), in.primitive().stream().index);
   118	          }
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        // All siblings have the same degree
   122	        auto cache_it = cache.find(in.id());
   123	        if (cache_it == cache.end()) {
   124	          dfs.emplace(in, 0);
   125	          cache.insert({in.id(), 1});
   126	          for (auto& s : in.siblings()) {
   127	            cache.insert({s.id(), 1});
   128	          }
   129	        } else {
   130	          cache_it->second++;
   131	          for (auto& s : in.siblings()) {
   132	            cache[s.id()]++;
   133	          }
   134	        }
   135	        continue;
   136	      }
   137	      if ((a.status() != array::Status::unscheduled) && !a.is_tracer() &&
   138	          a.has_primitive()) {
   139	        // If the array is evaluated and is no longer a tracer, detach it
   140	        a.detach();
   141	      }
   142	      dfs.pop();
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    // Build the tape in BFS order with a width limit
   146	    int max_width = env::bfs_max_width();
   147	    dfs = std::stack<std::pair<std::reference_wrapper<array>, int>>();
   148	    tape.push_back(synchronizer);
   149	    for (int i = 0; !cache.empty() && (i < tape.size() || !dfs.empty());) {
   150	      auto& a = (i >= tape.size()) ? dfs.top().first.get() : tape[i];
   151	      int j = 0;
   152	      if (i >= tape.size()) {
   153	        j = dfs.top().second;
   154	        dfs.pop();
   155	      } else {
   156	        i++;
   157	      }
   158	      for (; j < a.inputs().size(); ++j) {
   159	        auto& in = a.inputs()[j];
   160	        if (in.status() != array::Status::unscheduled) {
   161	          continue;
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        // If the width limit is exceeded, push the array on the stack
   165	        // and go down a level
    70	    d.commit_command_buffer(s.index);
    71	    d.get_command_buffer(s.index);
    72	  } else {
    73	    command_buffer->addCompletedHandler(
    74	        [s, buffers = std::move(buffers)](MTL::CommandBuffer* cbuf) {
    75	          check_error(cbuf);
    76	        });
    77	  }
    78	}
    79	
    80	void finalize(Stream s) {
    81	  auto pool = metal::new_scoped_memory_pool();
    82	  auto& d = metal::device(s.device);
    83	  auto cb = d.get_command_buffer(s.index);
    84	  d.end_encoding(s.index);
    85	  cb->addCompletedHandler([s](MTL::CommandBuffer* cbuf) { check_error(cbuf); });
    86	  d.commit_command_buffer(s.index);
    87	  d.get_command_buffer(s.index);
    88	}
    89	
    90	void synchronize(Stream s) {
    91	  auto pool = metal::new_scoped_memory_pool();
    92	  auto& d = metal::device(s.device);
    93	  auto cb = d.get_command_buffer(s.index);
    94	  cb->retain();
    95	  d.end_encoding(s.index);
    96	  d.commit_command_buffer(s.index);
    97	  cb->waitUntilCompleted();
    98	  check_error(cb);
    99	  cb->release();
   100	}

   289	
   290	void async_eval(std::vector<array> outputs) {
   291	  if (outputs.empty()) {
   292	    return;
   293	  }
   294	
   295	  if (std::none_of(outputs.begin(), outputs.end(), [](array& x) {
   296	        return x.status() == array::Status::unscheduled;
   297	      })) {
   298	    return;
   299	  }
   300	
   301	  eval_impl(std::move(outputs), true);
   302	}
   303	
   304	void eval(std::vector<array> outputs) {
   305	  if (outputs.empty()) {
   306	    return;
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  if (std::none_of(outputs.begin(), outputs.end(), [](array& x) {
   310	        return x.status() == array::Status::unscheduled;
   311	      })) {
   312	    for (auto& x : outputs) {
   313	      x.wait();
   314	    }
   315	    return;
   316	  }
   317	
   318	  eval_impl(std::move(outputs), false).wait();
   319	}

  5698	  private func runKrea2Generate(_ payload: GeneratePayload, continuation: ContinuationBox<GenerateResponse>) async {
  5699	    activeRenderStartedAt = Date()
  5700	    let start = Date()
  5701	    var resumed = false
  5702	    defer {
  5703	      if !resumed {
  5704	        logger.error("runKrea2Generate: continuation was not resumed — resuming with error.")
  5705	        failedRenderCount += 1
  5706	        lastError = "Krea2 generation failed unexpectedly (continuation not resumed)"
  5707	        activeRenderStartedAt = nil
  5708	        continuation.resume(throwing: WarmServerError.invalidRequest(message: "Krea2 generation failed unexpectedly"))
  5709	      }
  5710	    }
  5711	    do {
  5712	      guard let k2 = krea2Pipeline else {
  5713	        throw WarmServerError.krea2NotLoaded
  5714	      }
  5715	      let outputURL = try payload.resolvedOutputURL(
  5716	        configuration: configuration,
  5717	        defaultFilename: "zimage-krea2-\(UUID().uuidString).png"
  5718	      )
  5719	
  5720	      let seed = payload.seed ?? UInt64.random(in: 1..<UInt64(UInt32.max))
  5721	      let steps = payload.steps ?? 9
  5722	      let width = payload.width ?? 1024
  5723	      let height = payload.height ?? 1024
  5724	      // Krea-2 builds its requests straight from the payload rather than going
  5725	      // through makePipelineRequest, so resolve DyPE explicitly here.
  5726	      let krea2DyPE = payload.resolvedDyPEConfig(width: width, height: height)
  5727	
  5728	      // img2img fix (2026-07-19): Krea2Pipeline.generateImg2Img already
  5729	      // implements VAE-encode + partial-denoise; runKrea2Generate simply never
  5730	      // wired it, so an init image was silently ignored (txt2img). When an init
  5731	      // image is present, load+normalize it to NHWC [-1,1] and run img2img.
  5732	      // strength = 1 - denoise, matching flux1 makeImg2ImgRequest's convention.
  5733	      // Depth Control-LoRA: load control weights + encode control tokens when a control image is supplied.
  5734	      var controlPixels: MLXArray? = nil
  5735	      let resolvedControlData: Data? = payload.controlImageData ?? payload.controlImage.flatMap { try? Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: ($0 as NSString).expandingTildeInPath)) }
  5736	      if let controlData = resolvedControlData {
  5737	        let ccg = try InpaintUtilities.loadCGImage(from: controlData)
  5738	        let cpix = try QwenImageIO.resizedPixelArray(from: ccg, width: width, height: height)
  5739	        controlPixels = QwenImageIO.normalizeForEncoder(cpix).transposed(0, 2, 3, 1)
  5740	        let loraURL = URL(fileURLWithPath: "/Volumes/Bolt/Models/krea2-controlnet/depth-control-lora.safetensors")
  5741	        try await k2.setControlLoRA(loraURL, scale: payload.controlnetStrength ?? 1.0)
  5742	        logger.info("Krea2: depth Control-LoRA active (strength=\(payload.controlnetStrength ?? 1.0))")
  5743	      } else if k2.controlLoRAActive {
  5744	        try await k2.setControlLoRA(nil)
  5745	      }
  5746	      let image: MLXArray
  5747	      if let initPath = payload.imagePath {
  5748	        let imageData = try Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: initPath))
  5749	        let cg = try InpaintUtilities.loadCGImage(from: imageData)
  5750	        let pixNCHW = try QwenImageIO.resizedPixelArray(from: cg, width: width, height: height)
  5751	        let sourceNHWC = QwenImageIO.normalizeForEncoder(pixNCHW).transposed(0, 2, 3, 1)
  5752	        let strength: Float
  5753	        if let c = payload.creativity {
  5754	          strength = 1.0 - max(0.01, min(0.99, c))
  5755	        } else if let sVal = payload.imageStrength {
  5756	          strength = max(0.01, min(0.99, sVal))
  5757	        } else if let d = payload.denoise {
  5758	          strength = 1.0 - max(0.01, min(0.99, d))
  5759	        } else {
  5760	          strength = 0.3
  5761	        }
  5762	        logger.info("Krea2: img2img init=\(initPath) strength=\(strength)")
  5763	        image = k2.generateImg2Img(
  5764	          .init(prompt: payload.prompt, sourceImage: sourceNHWC, width: width, height: height,
  5765	                steps: steps, seed: seed, strength: strength, dyPE: krea2DyPE)
  5766	        ) { [logger] step, total in
  5767	          logger.info("Krea2 img2img: step \(step)/\(total)")
  5768	        }
  5769	      } else {
  5770	        image = k2.generate(
  5771	          .init(prompt: payload.prompt, width: width, height: height, steps: steps, seed: seed,
  5772	                controlImagePixels: controlPixels, dyPE: krea2DyPE)
  5773	        ) { [logger] step, total in
  5774	          logger.info("Krea2: step \(step)/\(total)")
  5775	        }
  5776	      }
  5777	      let metadata = QwenImageIO.ImageMetadata.generation(
  5778	        prompt: payload.prompt,
  5779	        seed: seed,
  5780	        steps: steps,
  5781	        guidance: 0,
  5782	        width: width,
  5783	        height: height,
  5784	        model: "krea-2-turbo",
  5785	        generatedBy: payload.source,
  5786	        contentMode: payload.contentMode,
  5787	        loras: k2.loadedLoRAConfigs
  5788	      )
  5789	      try QwenImageIO.saveImage(array: image.transposed(2, 0, 1), to: outputURL, metadata: metadata)
  5790	
  5791	      let durationMs = Int(Date().timeIntervalSince(start) * 1000.0)
  5792	      successfulRenderCount += 1
  5793	      lastRenderDurationMs = durationMs
  5794	      lastError = nil
  5795	      activeRenderStartedAt = nil
  5796	      resumed = true
  5797	      continuation.resume(returning: GenerateResponse(success: true, outputPath: outputURL.path, durationMs: durationMs))
  5798	    } catch {
  5799	      failedRenderCount += 1
  5800	      lastError = error.localizedDescription
  5801	      activeRenderStartedAt = nil
  5802	      resumed = true
  5803	      continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5804	    }
  5805	  }

  5423	  private func runGenerate(_ payload: GeneratePayload, continuation: ContinuationBox<GenerateResponse>, progressHandler: (@Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void)? = nil, latentPreviewHandler: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler? = nil) async {
  5424	    // Queue telemetry: tag this render with a job id and stream denoising
  5425	    // progress into the tracker that queueStatus() reads. Cleared on return
  5426	    // (success or failure) via defer. flux1 forwards the wrapped handler so the
  5427	    // pipeline's per-step callback updates progress; other families currently
  5428	    // have no per-step callback, so they report only is_rendering + job id.
  5429	    // (activeJobId is set from job.id at the top of the process loop.)
  5430	    progressTracker.set(0)
  5431	    liveHealth.setProgress(0)
  5432	    let tracker = progressTracker
  5433	    // Feed the lock-based health snapshot too, so /health's progress_percent
  5434	    // advances live during the render without an actor hop (#217).
  5435	    let health = liveHealth
  5436	    let trackedHandler: @Sendable (ZImagePipeline.GenerationProgress) -> Void = { progress in
  5437	      if progress.stage == .denoising {
  5438	        let pct = Int(progress.fractionCompleted * 100)
  5439	        tracker.set(pct)
  5440	        health.setProgress(pct)
  5441	      }
  5442	      progressHandler?(progress)
  5443	    }
  5444	
  5445	    // Live denoising preview (GH #216): approximate each step's latents as a
  5446	    // small JPEG and stash it for REST/polling clients (Desktop already
  5447	    // polls /health for progress_percent — this rides the same cadence).
  5448	    // Krita/ComfyUI still get their own frame pushed via the bridge
  5449	    // WebSocket, forwarded first so that behavior is unchanged.
  5450	    let preview = previewTracker
  5451	    let trackedPreviewHandler: ZImagePipeline.LatentPreviewHandler = { latents, step, total, latentH, latentW in
  5452	      latentPreviewHandler?(latents, step, total, latentH, latentW)
  5453	      #if canImport(CoreGraphics)
  5454	      guard step > 0, step % Self.previewStepInterval == 0, step < total else { return }
  5455	      guard let approx = LatentPreviewApproximator.latentsToRGBA(latents, latentHeight: latentH, latentWidth: latentW) else { return }
  5456	      guard let framed = ComfyBridgePreviewEncoder.encodePreviewFrame(
  5457	        fromRGBA: approx.data, width: approx.width, height: approx.height,
  5458	        maxDimension: Self.previewMaxDimension, jpegQuality: Self.previewJPEGQuality
  5459	      ) else { return }
  5460	      // Strip the 8-byte ComfyUI WebSocket binary-frame header — REST
  5461	      // clients just want the raw JPEG bytes.
  5462	      preview.set(framed.dropFirst(8))
  5463	      #endif
  5464	    }
  5465	    defer { activeJobId = nil; progressTracker.set(nil); liveHealth.setProgress(nil); previewTracker.set(nil) }
  5466	
  5467	    // #218: if a prior LTX-2 video render evicted the image models, restore the
  5468	    // previously-active image model before this render can run. poolLoad also
  5469	    // releases any resident video stack, so image and video stay mutually
  5470	    // exclusive. Runs before the per-job model/LoRA application below.
  5471	    do {
  5472	      try await reloadImageModelIfEvicted(requestedModel: payload.model)
  5473	    } catch {
  5474	      lastError = error.localizedDescription
  5475	      continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5476	      return
  5477	    }
  5478	
  5479	    // Per-job model/LoRA application (queue-submit race fix): a job's own
  5480	    // model+loras are applied right before it runs, instead of trusting
  5481	    // whatever the shared pool's "currently active" model/LoRAs happen to
  5482	    // be by the time it dequeues — a plain synchronous /v1/generate caller
  5483	    // activates the model right before calling generate, but async
  5484	    // queue-submit (POST /v1/generate/async) can dequeue well after a
  5485	    // different request has changed global state. nil model/loras preserve
  5486	    // the old caller-activates-first behavior exactly.
  5487	    if let modelSpec = payload.model, !modelSpec.isEmpty {
  5488	      let resolvedSpec = WarmServer.parseModelSpec(from: modelSpec)
  5489	      let currentSpec = activePoolModelSpec ?? configuration.modelSpec
  5490	      if resolvedSpec != currentSpec {
  5491	        let resolvedQuant = WarmServer.parseQuantization(from: modelSpec)
  5492	        do {
  5493	          _ = try await poolLoad(modelSpec: resolvedSpec, quantization: resolvedQuant, activate: true)
  5494	        } catch {
  5495	          lastError = error.localizedDescription
  5496	          continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5497	          return
  5498	        }
  5499	      }
  5500	    }
  5501	    if let loraEntries = payload.loras {
  5502	      do {
  5503	        let newLoRAs = try loraEntries.map { try $0.makeConfiguration() }
  5504	        try await applyActiveLoRAs(newLoRAs)
  5505	      } catch {
  5506	        lastError = error.localizedDescription
  5507	        continuation.resume(throwing: error)
  5508	        return
  5509	      }
  5510	    }
  4750	      }
  4751	      let variantLabel = zimageVariant == .base ? "Base (non-distilled)" : "Turbo (distilled)"
  4752	      logger.info("Preloading warm server pipeline (Flux 1 / Z-Image \(variantLabel))")
  4753	      try await pipeline.prepare(
  4754	        modelSpec: modelSpec,
  4755	        textEncoderPath: configuration.textEncoderPath,
  4756	        loras: activeLoRAs,
  4757	        forceTransformerOverrideOnly: configuration.forceTransformerOverrideOnly
  4758	      )
  4759	      pipelinePrepared = true
  4760	      logger.info("Warm server pipeline ready (Flux 1 / Z-Image \(zimageVariant.rawValue))")
  4761	
  4762	      // Pre-load the full VAE encoder for img2img support.
  4763	      // Without this, the first img2img request triggers synchronous weight
  4764	      // loading inside the actor-isolated render path, which can deadlock
  4765	      // the cooperative thread pool (issue #141).
  4766	      do {
  4767	        try pipeline.prepareFullVAE()
  4768	        logger.info("Full VAE encoder pre-loaded for img2img")
  4769	      } catch {
  4770	        logger.warning("Failed to pre-load full VAE encoder: \(error). First img2img request will attempt lazy load.")
  4771	      }
  4772	    }
  4773	
  4774	    // Register the initial model in the pool so it appears in pool listings
  4775	    // and can be managed alongside hot-swapped models.
  4776	    // We register the already-loaded pipeline to avoid double-loading.
  4777	    if let spec = modelSpec {
  4778	      let box: PipelineBox
  4779	      let detectedInfo: Any?
  4780	      let vramMB: Int
  4781	      switch currentModelFamily {
  4782	      case .chroma:
  4783	        box = PipelineBox(pipeline: chromaPipeline! as AnyObject)
  4784	        if let tok = chromaTokenizer { box.context["tokenizer"] = tok as AnyObject }
  4785	        detectedInfo = nil
  4786	        vramMB = 17408
  4787	      case .fibo:
  4788	        box = PipelineBox(pipeline: fiboPipeline! as AnyObject)
  4789	        detectedInfo = detectedFiboModel
  4790	        vramMB = 22528
  4791	      case .flux2:
  4792	        box = PipelineBox(pipeline: flux2Pipeline! as AnyObject)
  4793	        detectedInfo = detectedFlux2Model
  4794	        vramMB = (detectedFlux2Model?.variant.contains("9b") ?? false) ? 18432 : 8704
  4795	      case .flux1:
  4796	        box = PipelineBox(pipeline: pipeline as AnyObject)
  4797	        detectedInfo = zimageVariant
  4798	        vramMB = 12288
  4799	      case .krea2:
  4800	        box = PipelineBox(pipeline: krea2Pipeline! as AnyObject)
  4801	        detectedInfo = nil
  4802	        vramMB = 22528
  4803	      }

I’m organizing the findings in the order I would fold them into the spec so the migration does not destabilize the live stream.
